Input

  "filePath": "/source/standards/karpathy-guidelines.mdc"

Output

<path>/source/standards/karpathy-guidelines.mdc</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Use when writing, reviewing, or refactoring code to avoid overcomplication, make surgical changes, surface assumptions, and define verifiable success criteria.
3: alwaysApply: true
4: ---
5: 
6: # Karpathy behavioral guidelines
7: 
8: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Merge with project-specific instructions as needed.
9: 
10: **Tradeoff:** These guidelines bias toward caution over speed. For trivial tasks, use judgment.
11: 
12: ## 1. Think Before Coding
13: 
14: **Don't assume. Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.**
15: 
16: Before implementing:
17: 
18: - State your assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, ask.
19: - If multiple interpretations exist, present them - don't pick silently.
20: - If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.
21: - If something is unclear, stop. Name what's confusing. Ask.
22: 
23: ## 2. Simplicity First
24: 
25: **Minimum code that solves the problem. Nothing speculative.**
26: 
27: - No features beyond what was asked.
28: - No abstractions for single-use code.
29: - No "flexibility" or "configurability" that wasn't requested.
30: - No error handling for impossible scenarios.
31: - If you write 200 lines and it could be 50, rewrite it.
32: 
33: Ask yourself: "Would a senior engineer say this is overcomplicated?" If yes, simplify.
34: 
35: ## 3. Surgical Changes
36: 
37: **Touch only what you must. Clean up only your own mess.**
38: 
39: When editing existing code:
40: 
41: - Don't "improve" adjacent code, comments, or formatting.
42: - Don't refactor things that aren't broken.
43: - Match existing style, even if you'd do it differently.
44: - If you notice unrelated dead code, mention it - don't delete it.
45: 
46: When your changes create orphans:
47: 
48: - Remove imports/variables/functions that YOUR changes made unused.
49: - Don't remove pre-existing dead code unless asked.
50: 
51: The test: Every changed line should trace directly to the user's request.
52: 
53: ## 4. Goal-Driven Execution
54: 
55: **Define success criteria. Loop until verified.**
56: 
57: Transform tasks into verifiable goals:
58: 
59: - "Add validation" → "Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass"
60: - "Fix the bug" → "Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass"
61: - "Refactor X" → "Ensure tests pass before and after"
62: 
63: For multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:
64: 
65: ```
66: 1. [Step] → verify: [check]
67: 2. [Step] → verify: [check]
68: 3. [Step] → verify: [check]
69: ```
70: 
71: Strong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria ("make it work") require constant clarification.
72: 
73: ---
74: 
75: **These guidelines are working if:** fewer unnecessary changes in diffs, fewer rewrites due to overcomplication, and clarifying questions come before implementation rather than after mistakes.

(End of file - total 75 lines)
</content>

read · completed

Input

{
  "filePath": "/source/standards/ponytail.mdc"
}

Output

<path>/source/standards/ponytail.mdc</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode. Always pick the simplest solution that works.
3: globs:
4: alwaysApply: true
5: ---
6: 
7: # Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode
8: 
9: You are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. The best code is the code never written.
10: 
11: Before writing any code, stop at the first rung that holds:
12: 
13: 1. Does this need to be built at all? (YAGNI)
14: 2. Does it already exist in this codebase? Reuse the helper, util, or pattern that's already here, don't re-write it.
15: 3. Does the standard library already do this? Use it.
16: 4. Does a native platform feature cover it? Use it.
17: 5. Does an already-installed dependency solve it? Use it.
18: 6. Can this be one line? Make it one line.
19: 7. Only then: write the minimum code that works.
20: 
21: The ladder runs after you understand the problem, not instead of it: read the task and the code it touches, trace the real flow end to end, then climb.
22: 
23: Bug fix = root cause, not symptom: a report names a symptom. Grep every caller of the function you touch and fix the shared function once — one guard there is a smaller diff than one per caller, and patching only the path the ticket names leaves a sibling caller still broken.
24: 
25: Rules:
26: 
27: - No abstractions that weren't explicitly requested.
28: - No new dependency if it can be avoided.
29: - No boilerplate nobody asked for.
30: - Deletion over addition. Boring over clever. Fewest files possible.
31: - Shortest working diff wins, but only once you understand the problem. The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.
32: - Question complex requests: "Do you actually need X, or does Y cover it?"
33: - Pick the edge-case-correct option when two stdlib approaches are the same size, lazy means less code, not the flimsier algorithm.
34: - Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path.
35: 
36: Not lazy about: understanding the problem (read it fully and trace the real flow before picking a rung, a small diff you don't understand is just laziness dressed up as efficiency), input validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs (the platform is never the spec ideal, a clock drifts, a sensor reads off), anything explicitly requested. Lazy code without its check is unfinished: non-trivial logic leaves ONE runnable check behind, the smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks (an assert-based demo/self-check or one small test file; no frameworks, no fixtures). Trivial one-liners need no test.

(End of file - total 36 lines)
</content>

The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.\n32: - Question complex requests: \"Do you actually need X, or does Y cover it?\"\n33: - Pick the edge-case-correct option when two stdlib approaches are the same size, lazy means less code, not the flimsier algorithm.\n34: - Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path.\n35: \n36: Not lazy about: understanding the problem (read it fully and trace the real flow before picking a rung, a small diff you don't understand is just laziness dressed up as efficiency), input validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs (the platform is never the spec ideal, a clock drifts, a sensor reads off), anything explicitly requested. Lazy code without its check is unfinished: non-trivial logic leaves ONE runnable check behind, the smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks (an assert-based demo/self-check or one small test file; no frameworks, no fixtures). Trivial one-liners need no test.\n\n(End of file - total 36 lines)\n</content>",

Always pick the simplest solution that works.\nglobs:\nalwaysApply: true\n---\n\n# Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode\n\nYou are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. The best code is the code never written.\n\nBefore writing any code, stop at the first rung that holds:\n\n1. Does this need to be built at all? (YAGNI)\n2. Does it already exist in this codebase? Reuse the helper, util, or pattern that's already here, don't re-write it.\n3. Does the standard library already do this? Use it.\n4. Does a native platform feature cover it? Use it.\n5.         "text": "---\ndescription: Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode. Always pick the simplest solution that works.\nglobs:\nalwaysApply: true\n---\n\n# Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode\n\nYou are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. The best code is the code never written.\n\nBefore writing any code, stop at the first rung that holds:\n\n1. Does this need to be built at all? (YAGNI)\n2. Does it already exist in this codebase? Reuse the helper, util, or pattern that's already here, don't re-write it.\n3. Does the standard library already do this? Use it.\n4. Does a native platform feature cover it? Use it.\n5. Does an already-installed dependency solve it? Use it.\n6. Can this be one line? Make it one line.\n7. Only then: write the minimum code that works.\n\nThe ladder runs after you understand the problem, not instead of it: read the task and the code it touches, trace the real flow end to end, then climb.\n\nBug fix = root cause, not symptom: a report names a symptom. Grep every caller of the function you touch and fix the shared function once — one guard there is a smaller diff than one per caller, and patching only the path the ticket names leaves a sibling caller still broken.\n\nRules:\n\n- No abstractions that weren't explicitly requested.\n- No new dependency if it can be avoided.\n- No boilerplate nobody asked for.\n- Deletion over addition. Boring over clever. Fewest files possible.\n- Shortest working diff wins, but only once you understand the problem. The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.\n- Question complex requests: \"Do you actually need X, or does Y cover it?\"\n- Pick the edge-case-correct option when two stdlib approaches are the same size, lazy means less code, not the flimsier algorithm.\n- Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path.\n\nNot lazy about: understanding the problem (read it fully and trace the real flow before picking a rung, a small diff you don't understand is just laziness dressed up as efficiency), input validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs (the platform is never the spec ideal, a clock drifts, a sensor reads off), anything explicitly requested. Lazy code without its check is unfinished: non-trivial logic leaves ONE runnable check behind, the smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks (an assert-based demo/self-check or one small test file; no frameworks, no fixtures). Trivial one-liners need no test.",

21: @@ -464,10 +464,20 @@ Containerlab will configure the `netconf-mgmt` ssh server running over port 830
22:  SR Linux container can run without a license emulating the datacenter types (7220 IXR) :partying_face:.  
23:  In that license-less mode, the datapath is limited to 1000 PPS and the `sr_linux` process will restart once a week.
24:  
25:  The license file lifts these limitations as well as unlocks chassis-based platform variants and a path to it can be provided with [`license`](../nodes.md#license) directive.
26:  
27: +### Reboot
28: +
29: +A reboot issued from within the node, for example with `tools platform chassis reboot`, restarts the SR Linux container. The node comes back with its links, saved configuration and management address intact.
30: +
31: +To support that, SR Linux nodes default to the `unless-stopped` [restart policy](../nodes.md#restart-policy), and containerlab keeps the links and the management address of each node in an internal `<node-name>-netns` container. The internal container is only listed by `containerlab inspect --all`, and it is removed together with the node.
32: +
33: +Use `containerlab stop`, `start` and `restart` to manage the lifecycle of SR Linux nodes instead of restarting the containers with the container runtime directly.
34: +
35: +Nodes with a user-defined [`network-mode`](../nodes.md#network-mode) do not use the internal container.
36: +
37:  ## Container configuration
38:  
39:  To start an SR Linux NOS containerlab uses the configuration that is described in SR Linux Software Installation Guide
40:  
41:  /// tab | Startup command
42: diff --git a/docs/manual/nodes.md b/docs/manual/nodes.md
43: index f9b8ba3..d5c0171 100644
44: --- a/docs/manual/nodes.md
45: +++ b/docs/manual/nodes.md
46: @@ -140,11 +140,11 @@ Valid values are:
47:  - `no` - Don't automatically restart the container.
48:  - `on-failure` - Restart the container if it exits due to an error, which manifests as a non-zero exit code. The on-failure policy only prompts a restart if the container exits with a failure. It doesn't restart the container if the daemon restarts.
49:  - `always` - Always restart the container if it stops. If it's manually stopped, it's restarted only when Docker daemon restarts or the container itself is manually restarted.
50:  - `unless-stopped` Similar to always, except that when the container is stopped (manually or otherwise), it isn't restarted even after Docker daemon restarts.
51:  
52: -`no` is the default restart policy value for all kinds, but `linux`. Linux kind defaults to `always`.
53: +`no` is the default restart policy value for all kinds, but `linux` and `nokia_srlinux`. Linux kind defaults to `always`, and [SR Linux](kinds/srl.md#reboot) kind defaults to `unless-stopped`.

65: +package srl
66: +
67: +import (
68: +	"context"
69: +	"errors"
70: +	"fmt"
71: +	"maps"
72: +	"strconv"
73: +
74: +	clabconstants "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/constants"
75: +	clabnodes "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/nodes"
76: +	clabnodesstate "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/nodes/state"
77: +	clabruntime "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/runtime"
78: +	clabtypes "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/types"
79: +	clabutils "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/utils"
80: +)
81: +
82: +const netnsNodeType = "srl-netns"
83: +
84: +// namespaceNode owns the network namespace of an SR Linux node, so that the dataplane links and
85: +// the management address survive a restart of the SR Linux container.
86: +type namespaceNode struct {
87: +	clabnodes.DefaultNode
88: +}
89: +
90: +func (n *namespaceNode) Init(cfg *clabtypes.NodeConfig, opts ...clabnodes.NodeOption) error {
91: +	n.DefaultNode = *clabnodes.NewDefaultNode(n)
92: +	n.Cfg = cfg
93: +	n.StopSignal = clabtypes.SIGTERM
94: +
95: +	for _, o := range opts {
96: +		o(n)
97: +	}
98: +
99: +	return nil
100: +}
101: +
102: +// usesNetnsNode reports whether the node runs in the network namespace of a holder container.
103: +// Nodes with a user-defined network mode keep the network namespace of their own container.
104: +func (n *srl) usesNetnsNode() bool {
105: +	return n.Cfg.NetworkMode == "" && !n.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased
106: +}
107: +
108: +func (n *srl) initNetnsNode() error {
109: +	if !n.usesNetnsNode() {
110: +		return nil
111: +	}
112: +
113: +	n.netnsNode = new(namespaceNode)
114: +
115: +	return n.netnsNode.Init(n.newNetnsConfig(), clabnodes.WithRuntime(n.GetRuntime()))
116: +}
117: +
118: +func (n *srl) newNetnsConfig() *clabtypes.NodeConfig {
119: +	labels := maps.Clone(n.Cfg.Labels)
120: +
121: +	if labels == nil {
122: +		labels = map[string]string{}
123: +	}
124: +
125: +	shortName := n.Cfg.ShortName + "-netns"
126: +	labels[clabconstants.NodeName] = shortName
127: +
128: +	longName := n.Cfg.LongName + "-netns"
129: +	labels[clabconstants.LongName] = longName
130: +
131: +	labels[clabconstants.NodeType] = netnsNodeType
132: +	labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] = "true"
133: +
134: +	labels[clabconstants.RootNodeName] = n.Cfg.ShortName
135: +	labels[clabconstants.RootNodeLongName] = n.Cfg.LongName
136: +
137: +	return &clabtypes.NodeConfig{
138: +		ShortName:             shortName,
139: +		LongName:              longName,
140: +		Hostname:              n.Cfg.GetHostname(),
141: +		Fqdn:                  n.Cfg.Fqdn,
142: +		LabDir:                n.Cfg.LabDir,
143: +		Index:                 n.Cfg.Index,
144: +		Kind:                  n.Cfg.Kind,
145: +		NodeType:              netnsNodeType,
146: +		Image:                 n.Cfg.Image,
147: +		ImagePullPolicy:       n.Cfg.ImagePullPolicy,
148: +		RestartPolicy:         n.Cfg.RestartPolicy,
149: +		Sysctls:               maps.Clone(n.Cfg.Sysctls),
150: +		User:                  "0:0",
151: +		Entrypoint:            "/bin/sh",
152: +		Cmd:                   `-c "trap 'exit 0' TERM INT; sleep infinity & wait $!"`,
153: +		Env:                   map[string]string{},
154: +		PortBindings:          n.Cfg.PortBindings,
155: +		PortSet:               n.Cfg.PortSet,
156: +		MgmtNet:               n.Cfg.MgmtNet,
157: +		MgmtIntf:              n.Cfg.MgmtIntf,
158: +		MgmtIPv4Address:       n.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Address,
159: +		MgmtIPv4AddressAuto:   n.Cfg.MgmtIPv4AddressAuto,
160: +		MgmtIPv4PrefixLength:  n.Cfg.MgmtIPv4PrefixLength,
161: +		MgmtIPv6Address:       n.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Address,
162: +		MgmtIPv6AddressAuto:   n.Cfg.MgmtIPv6AddressAuto,
163: +		MgmtIPv6PrefixLength:  n.Cfg.MgmtIPv6PrefixLength,
164: +		MgmtIPv4Gateway:       n.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Gateway,
165: +		MgmtIPv6Gateway:       n.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Gateway,
166: +		MacAddress:            n.Cfg.MacAddress,
167: +		Aliases:               n.Cfg.Aliases,
168: +		DNS:                   n.Cfg.DNS,
169: +		ExtraHosts:            n.Cfg.ExtraHosts,
170: +		Labels:                labels,
171: +		Runtime:               n.Cfg.Runtime,
172: +		ResultingPortBindings: n.Cfg.ResultingPortBindings,
173: +	}
174: +}
175: +
176: +// containerConfig returns the config of the SR Linux container attached to the holder's
177: +// network namespace. The runtime rejects network settings for such containers, as they
178: +// belong to the holder.
179: +func (n *srl) containerConfig() *clabtypes.NodeConfig {
180: +	cfg := *n.Cfg
181: +	cfg.NetworkMode = "container:" + n.netnsNode.GetShortName()
182: +	cfg.DNS = nil
183: +	cfg.ExtraHosts = nil
184: +	cfg.MacAddress = ""
185: +	cfg.PortBindings = nil
186: +	cfg.PortSet = nil
187: +
188: +	return &cfg
189: +}
190: +
191: +func (n *srl) ensureNetnsRunning(ctx context.Context) error {
192: +	switch status := n.netnsNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx); status {
193: +	case clabruntime.Running:
194: +	case clabruntime.Created, clabruntime.Stopped:
195: +		if err := n.netnsNode.Start(ctx); err != nil {
196: +			return fmt.Errorf("node %q network namespace container start error: %w",
197: +				n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
198: +		}
199: +	case clabruntime.Paused:
200: +		if err := n.Runtime.UnpauseContainer(ctx, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName); err != nil {
201: +			return fmt.Errorf("node %q network namespace container unpause error: %w",
202: +				n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
203: +		}
204: +	case clabruntime.NotFound:
205: +		return fmt.Errorf(
206: +			"node %q network namespace container %q not found; recreate the node",
207: +			n.Cfg.ShortName, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName,
208: +		)
209: +	default:
210: +		return fmt.Errorf("node %q network namespace container %q is %s",
211: +			n.Cfg.ShortName, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName, status)
212: +	}
213: +
214: +	return nil
215: +}
216: +
217: +// linkNetns points the node's netns symlink to the holder, as the namespace path of the
218: +// SR Linux container changes whenever the container restarts.
219: +func (n *srl) linkNetns(ctx context.Context) error {
220: +	nsPath, err := n.netnsNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
221: +	if err != nil {
222: +		return err
223: +	}
224: +
225: +	return clabutils.LinkContainerNS(nsPath, n.Cfg.LongName)
226: +}
227: +
228: +func (n *srl) Deploy(ctx context.Context, params *clabnodes.DeployParams) error {
229: +	if n.netnsNode == nil {
230: +		return n.DefaultNode.Deploy(ctx, params)
231: +	}
232: +
233: +	// network settings are finalized after Init, so the holder config is rebuilt here.
234: +	*n.netnsNode.Config() = *n.newNetnsConfig()
235: +	if err := n.netnsNode.Deploy(ctx, params); err != nil {
236: +		return fmt.Errorf("deploy network namespace node %q: %w", n.netnsNode.GetShortName(), err)
237: +	}
238: +
239: +	n.Cfg.Env[clabconstants.ClabEnvIntfs] = strconv.Itoa(len(n.GetEndpoints()))
240: +
241: +	cID, err := n.Runtime.CreateContainer(ctx, n.containerConfig())
242: +	if err != nil {
243: +		return err
244: +	}
245: +
246: +	if _, err := n.Runtime.StartContainer(ctx, cID, n); err != nil {
247: +		return err
248: +	}
249: +
250: +	if err := n.linkNetns(ctx); err != nil {
251: +		return err
252: +	}
253: +
254: +	n.SetState(clabnodesstate.Deployed)
255: +
256: +	return nil
257: +}
258: +
259: +func (n *srl) Start(ctx context.Context) error {
260: +	if n.netnsNode == nil {
261: +		return n.DefaultNode.Start(ctx)
262: +	}
263: +
264: +	if err := n.ensureNetnsRunning(ctx); err != nil {
265: +		return err
266: +	}
267: +
268: +	if err := n.DefaultNode.Start(ctx); err != nil {
269: +		return err
270: +	}
271: +
272: +	return n.linkNetns(ctx)
273: +}
274: +
275: +// Stop also stops the holder, so that the next start gets a fresh network namespace without
276: +// interfaces left behind by the stopped SR Linux container.
277: +func (n *srl) Stop(ctx context.Context) error {
278: +	if err := n.DefaultNode.Stop(ctx); err != nil || n.netnsNode == nil {
279: +		return err
280: +	}
281: +
282: +	if n.netnsNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx) != clabruntime.Running {
283: +		return nil
284: +	}
285: +
286: +	return n.Runtime.StopContainer(ctx, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName, n.netnsNode.StopSignal)
287: +}
288: +
289: +func (n *srl) Delete(ctx context.Context) error {
290: +	if n.netnsNode == nil {
291: +		return n.DefaultNode.Delete(ctx)
292: +	}
293: +
294: +	return errors.Join(n.DefaultNode.Delete(ctx), n.netnsNode.Delete(ctx))
295: +}
296: +
297: +// GetContainers returns the SR Linux container with the network settings of the holder.
298: +// When only the holder exists, it is returned so that it is not orphaned on destroy.
299: +func (n *srl) GetContainers(ctx context.Context) ([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, error) {
300: +	cnts, err := n.DefaultNode.GetContainers(ctx)
301: +	if n.netnsNode == nil {
302: +		return cnts, err
303: +	}
304: +
305: +	if err != nil {
306: +		if errors.Is(err, clabnodes.ErrContainersNotFound) {
307: +			return n.netnsNode.GetContainers(ctx)
308: +		}
309: +
310: +		return nil, err
311: +	}
312: +
313: +	holder, err := n.netnsNode.GetContainers(ctx)
314: +	if err != nil {
315: +		if errors.Is(err, clabnodes.ErrContainersNotFound) {
316: +			return cnts, nil
317: +		}
318: +
319: +		return nil, err
320: +	}
321: +
322: +	cnts[0].NetworkName = holder[0].NetworkName
323: +	cnts[0].NetworkSettings = holder[0].NetworkSettings
324: +	cnts[0].Ports = holder[0].Ports
325: +
326: +	return cnts, nil
327: +}
328: +
329: +// GetNSPath retrieves the Namespace Path.
330: +func (n *srl) GetNSPath(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {
331: +	if n.netnsNode == nil {
332: +		return n.DefaultNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
333: +	}
334: +
335: +	return n.netnsNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
336: +}
337: +
338: +// DeleteNetnsSymlink deletes the symlink file created for the container netns.
339: +func (n *srl) DeleteNetnsSymlink() error {
340: +	if n.netnsNode == nil {
341: +		return n.DefaultNode.DeleteNetnsSymlink()
342: +	}
343: +
344: +	return errors.Join(n.DefaultNode.DeleteNetnsSymlink(), n.netnsNode.DeleteNetnsSymlink())
345: +}

404: +func TestNetnsNodeConfig(t *testing.T) {
405: +	cfg := newSRLTestConfig(t)

407: +	n := newSRLTestNode(t, cfg, nil)

427: +	assert.Empty(t, n.Cfg.Labels[clabconstants.InternalNode])
428: +}

448: +func TestContainerConfigUsesNetnsNode(t *testing.T) {
449: +	cfg := newSRLTestConfig(t)
450: +	cfg.DNS = &clabtypes.DNSConfig{Servers: []string{"1.1.1.1"}}
451: +	cfg.ExtraHosts = []string{"srl2:172.20.20.3"}
452: +	cfg.MacAddress = "02:00:00:00:00:01"
453: +	cfg.PortSet = nat.PortSet{"443/tcp": struct{}{}}
454: +	cfg.PortBindings = nat.PortMap{"443/tcp": []nat.PortBinding{{HostPort: "50443"}}}
455: +	n := newSRLTestNode(t, cfg, nil)
456: +
457: +	got := n.containerConfig()
458: +
459: +	assert.Equal(t, "container:"+testNetnsShort, got.NetworkMode)
460: +	assert.Nil(t, got.DNS)
461: +	assert.Nil(t, got.ExtraHosts)
462: +	assert.Empty(t, got.MacAddress)
463: +	assert.Nil(t, got.PortBindings)
464: +	assert.Nil(t, got.PortSet)
465: +	assert.Equal(t, n.Cfg.Entrypoint, got.Entrypoint)
466: +	assert.Equal(t, n.Cfg.Cmd, got.Cmd)
467: +	assert.Equal(t, "bar", got.Env["FOO"])
468: +
469: +	// the node config keeps the network settings, they are applied to the holder
470: +	assert.Empty(t, n.Cfg.NetworkMode)
471: +	assert.Equal(t, []string{"srl2:172.20.20.3"}, n.Cfg.ExtraHosts)
472: +	assert.NotNil(t, n.Cfg.DNS)
473: +}

476: +	ctx := context.Background()

The node comes back with its links, saved configuration and management address intact.\n+\n+To support that, SR Linux nodes default to the `unless-stopped` [restart policy](../nodes.md#restart-policy), and containerlab keeps the links and the management address of each node in an internal `<node-name>-netns` container. The internal container is only listed by `containerlab inspect --all`, and it is removed together with the node.\n+\n+Use `containerlab stop`, `start` and `restart` to manage the lifecycle of SR Linux nodes instead of restarting the containers with the container runtime directly.\n+\n+Nodes with a user-defined [`network-mode`](../nodes.md#network-mode) do not use the internal container.\n+\n ## Container configuration\n \n To start an SR Linux NOS containerlab uses the configuration that is described in SR Linux Software Installation Guide\n \n /// tab | Startup command\ndiff --git a/docs/manual/nodes.md b/docs/manual/nodes.md\nindex f9b8ba3..d5c0171 100644\n--- a/docs/manual/nodes.md\n+++ b/docs/manual/nodes.md\n@@ -140,11 +140,11 @@ Valid values are:\n - `no` - Don't automatically restart the container.\n - `on-failure` - Restart the container if it exits due to an error, which manifests as a non-zero exit code. The on-failure policy only prompts a restart if the container exits with a failure. It doesn't restart the container if the daemon restarts.\n - `always` - Always restart the container if it stops. If it's manually stopped, it's restarted only when Docker daemon restarts or the container itself is manually restarted.\n - `unless-stopped` Similar to always, except that when the container is stopped (manually or otherwise), it isn't restarted even after Docker daemon restarts.\n \n-`no` is the default restart policy value for all kinds, but `linux`. Linux kind defaults to `always`.\n+`no` is the default restart policy value for all kinds, but `linux` and `nokia_srlinux`. 20: // namespaceNode owns the network namespace of an SR Linux node, so that the dataplane links and
21: // the management address survive a restart of the SR Linux container.
22: type namespaceNode struct {
23: 	clabnodes.DefaultNode
24: }
25: 
26: func (n *namespaceNode) Init(cfg *clabtypes.NodeConfig, opts ...clabnodes.NodeOption) error {
27: 	n.DefaultNode = *clabnodes.NewDefaultNode(n)
28: 	n.Cfg = cfg
29: 	n.StopSignal = clabtypes.SIGTERM
30: 
31: 	for _, o := range opts {
32: 		o(n)
33: 	}
34: 
35: 	return nil
36: }
37: 
38: // usesNetnsNode reports whether the node runs in the network namespace of a holder container.
39: // Nodes with a user-defined network mode keep the network namespace of their own container.
40: func (n *srl) usesNetnsNode() bool {
41: 	return n.Cfg.NetworkMode == "" && !n.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased
42: }
43: 
44: func (n *srl) initNetnsNode() error {
45: 	if !n.usesNetnsNode() {
46: 		return nil
47: 	}
48: 
49: 	n.netnsNode = new(namespaceNode)
50: 
51: 	return n.netnsNode.Init(n.newNetnsConfig(), clabnodes.WithRuntime(n.GetRuntime()))
52: }
53: 
54: func (n *srl) newNetnsConfig() *clabtypes.NodeConfig {
55: 	labels := maps.Clone(n.Cfg.Labels)
56: 
57: 	if labels == nil {
58: 		labels = map[string]string{}
59: 	}
60: 
61: 	shortName := n.Cfg.ShortName + "-netns"
62: 	labels[clabconstants.NodeName] = shortName
63: 
64: 	longName := n.Cfg.LongName + "-netns"
65: 	labels[clabconstants.LongName] = longName
66: 
67: 	labels[clabconstants.NodeType] = netnsNodeType
68: 	labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] = "true"
69: 
70: 	labels[clabconstants.RootNodeName] = n.Cfg.ShortName
71: 	labels[clabconstants.RootNodeLongName] = n.Cfg.LongName
72: 
73: 	return &clabtypes.NodeConfig{
74: 		ShortName:             shortName,
75: 		LongName:              longName,
76: 		Hostname:              n.Cfg.GetHostname(),

112: // containerConfig returns the config of the SR Linux container attached to the holder's
113: // network namespace. The runtime rejects network settings for such containers, as they
114: // belong to the holder.
115: func (n *srl) containerConfig() *clabtypes.NodeConfig {
116: 	cfg := *n.Cfg
117: 	cfg.NetworkMode = "container:" + n.netnsNode.GetShortName()
118: 	cfg.DNS = nil
119: 	cfg.ExtraHosts = nil
120: 	cfg.MacAddress = ""
121: 	cfg.PortBindings = nil
122: 	cfg.PortSet = nil
123: 
124: 	return &cfg
125: }

127: func (n *srl) ensureNetnsRunning(ctx context.Context) error {
128: 	switch status := n.netnsNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx); status {
129: 	case clabruntime.Running:
130: 	case clabruntime.Created, clabruntime.Stopped:
131: 		if err := n.netnsNode.Start(ctx); err != nil {
132: 			return fmt.Errorf("node %q network namespace container start error: %w",
133: 				n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
134: 		}
135: 	case clabruntime.Paused:
136: 		if err := n.Runtime.UnpauseContainer(ctx, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName); err != nil {
137: 			return fmt.Errorf("node %q network namespace container unpause error: %w",
138: 				n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
139: 		}
140: 	case clabruntime.NotFound:
141: 		return fmt.Errorf(
142: 			"node %q network namespace container %q not found; recreate the node",
143: 			n.Cfg.ShortName, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName,
144: 		)
145: 	default:
146: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q network namespace container %q is %s",
147: 			n.Cfg.ShortName, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName, status)
148: 	}
149: 
150: 	return nil
151: }
152: 
153: // linkNetns points the node's netns symlink to the holder, as the namespace path of the
154: // SR Linux container changes whenever the container restarts.
155: func (n *srl) linkNetns(ctx context.Context) error {
156: 	nsPath, err := n.netnsNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
157: 	if err != nil {
158: 		return err
159: 	}
160: 
161: 	return clabutils.LinkContainerNS(nsPath, n.Cfg.LongName)
162: }
163: 
164: func (n *srl) Deploy(ctx context.Context, params *clabnodes.DeployParams) error {
165: 	if n.netnsNode == nil {
166: 		return n.DefaultNode.Deploy(ctx, params)
167: 	}
168: 
169: 	// network settings are finalized after Init, so the holder config is rebuilt here.
170: 	*n.netnsNode.Config() = *n.newNetnsConfig()
171: 	if err := n.netnsNode.Deploy(ctx, params); err != nil {
172: 		return fmt.Errorf("deploy network namespace node %q: %w", n.netnsNode.GetShortName(), err)
173: 	}
174: 
175: 	n.Cfg.Env[clabconstants.ClabEnvIntfs] = strconv.Itoa(len(n.GetEndpoints()))
176: 
177: 	cID, err := n.Runtime.CreateContainer(ctx, n.containerConfig())
178: 	if err != nil {
179: 		return err
180: 	}
181: 
182: 	if _, err := n.Runtime.StartContainer(ctx, cID, n); err != nil {
183: 		return err
184: 	}
185: 
186: 	if err := n.linkNetns(ctx); err != nil {
187: 		return err
188: 	}
189: 
190: 	n.SetState(clabnodesstate.Deployed)
191: 
192: 	return nil
193: }
194: 
195: func (n *srl) Start(ctx context.Context) error {
196: 	if n.netnsNode == nil {
197: 		return n.DefaultNode.Start(ctx)
198: 	}
199: 
200: 	if err := n.ensureNetnsRunning(ctx); err != nil {
201: 		return err
202: 	}
203: 
204: 	if err := n.DefaultNode.Start(ctx); err != nil {
205: 		return err
206: 	}
207: 
208: 	return n.linkNetns(ctx)
209: }
210: 
211: // Stop also stops the holder, so that the next start gets a fresh network namespace without
212: // interfaces left behind by the stopped SR Linux container.
213: func (n *srl) Stop(ctx context.Context) error {
214: 	if err := n.DefaultNode.Stop(ctx); err != nil || n.netnsNode == nil {
215: 		return err
216: 	}
217: 
218: 	if n.netnsNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx) != clabruntime.Running {
219: 		return nil
220: 	}
221: 
222: 	return n.Runtime.StopContainer(ctx, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName, n.netnsNode.StopSignal)
223: }
224: 
225: func (n *srl) Delete(ctx context.Context) error {
226: 	if n.netnsNode == nil {
227: 		return n.DefaultNode.Delete(ctx)
228: 	}
229:

243: func (n *srl) kindSpecificCfg() *KindSpecificConfig { return kindSpecificConfig.Of(n.Cfg) }

245: func (n *srl) Init(cfg *clabtypes.NodeConfig, opts ...clabnodes.NodeOption) error {
246: 	// Init DefaultNode
247: 	n.DefaultNode = *clabnodes.NewDefaultNode(n)
248: 	// set virtualization requirement
249: 	n.HostRequirements.SSSE3 = true
250: 	n.HostRequirements.MinVCPU = 2
251: 	n.HostRequirements.MinVCPUFailAction = clabtypes.FailBehaviourError
252: 	n.HostRequirements.MinAvailMemoryGb = 2
253: 	n.HostRequirements.MinAvailMemoryGbFailAction = clabtypes.FailBehaviourLog
254: 
255: 	n.Cfg = cfg
256: 
257: 	// force cert creation for srlinux nodes as they by make use of tls certificate in the default
258: 	// config
259: 	n.Cfg.Certificate.Issue = new(true)
260: 
261: 	for _, o := range opts {
262: 		o(n)
263: 	}
264: 
265: 	if n.Cfg.NodeType != "" && !strings.Contains(n.Cfg.NodeType, "-") {
266: 		log.Warn(
267: 			"Deprecation notice",
268: 			"notice",
269: 			"You are using a deprecated SR Linux node type name. "+
270: 				"Consider using a new format with dashes. Example: ixr-d2l, ixr-h5-64d, etc.\n"+
271: 				"Deprecated type format will be removed after January 2026",
272: 			"deprecated type",
273: 			n.Cfg.NodeType,
274: 		)
275: 	}
276: 
277: 	if n.Cfg.NodeType == "" {
278: 		n.Cfg.NodeType = SRLinuxDefaultType
279: 	}
280: 
281: 	if _, found := srlTypes[n.Cfg.NodeType]; !found {
282: 		keys := make([]string, 0, len(srlTypes))
283: 		for key := range srlTypes {
284: 			keys = append(keys, key)
285: 		}
286: 
287: 		return fmt.Errorf("wrong node type. '%s' doesn't exist. should be any of %s",
288: 			n.Cfg.NodeType, strings.Join(keys, ", "))
289: 	}
290: 
291: 	// fail on an unusable components block here rather than midway through the deploy
292: 	if _, err := resolveSRLTopology(n.Cfg); err != nil {
293: 		return err
294: 	}
295: 
296: 	if len(n.kindSpecificCfg().Components) > 1 {
297: 		log.Warn("Multiple line cards are rendered into the SR Linux topology file, but "+
298: 			"deploying a node with more than one line card is not supported yet",
299: 			"node", n.Cfg.ShortName, "line cards", len(n.kindSpecificCfg().Components))
300: 	}
301: 
302: 	if n.Cfg.Cmd == "" {
303: 		// set default Cmd if it was not provided by a user
304: 		// the additional touch is needed to support non docker runtimes
305: 		n.Cfg.Cmd = "sudo -E bash -c 'touch /.dockerenv && /opt/srlinux/bin/sr_linux'"
306: 	}
307: 
308: 	n.Cfg.Env = clabutils.MergeStringMaps(srlEnv, n.Cfg.Env)
309: 
310: 	// if user was not initialized to a value, use root
311: 	if n.Cfg.User == "" {
312: 		n.Cfg.User = "0:0"
313: 	}
314: 
315: 	// restart the node when it is rebooted from within.
316: 	// unless-stopped is used, as some images exit with code 0 on reboot;
317: 	// on-failure is enough once all supported images exit with a non-zero code.
318: 	if n.Cfg.RestartPolicy == "" {
319: 		n.Cfg.RestartPolicy = "unless-stopped"
320: 	}
321: 
322: 	maps.Copy(n.Cfg.Sysctls, srlSysctl)
323: 
324: 	if n.Cfg.License != "" {
325: 		// we mount a fixed path node.Labdir/license.key as the license referenced in topo file will
326: 		// be copied to that path
327: 		n.Cfg.Binds = append(n.Cfg.Binds, fmt.Sprint(
328: 			filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, "license.key"), ":/opt/srlinux/etc/license.key:ro"))
329: 	}
330: 
331: 	// mount config directory
332: 	cfgPath := filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, "config")
333: 	n.Cfg.Binds = append(n.Cfg.Binds, fmt.Sprint(cfgPath, ":/etc/opt/srlinux/:rw"))
334: 
335: 	// mount srlinux topology
336: 	srcTopoPath := filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, "topology.yml")
337: 	dstTopoPath := "/tmp/topology.yml"
338: 	// if a user provided a topology file, it means that they want to use a custom srl topology file
339: 	// in that case we do not need to mount the one for the provided type
340: 	if !clabutils.DestinationBindMountExists(n.Cfg.Binds, dstTopoPath) {
341: 		n.Cfg.Binds = append(n.Cfg.Binds, fmt.Sprint(srcTopoPath, ":", dstTopoPath, ":ro"))
342: 	}
343: 
344: 	// mounting /run/netnns as tmpfs ensures the network-instances
345: 	// namespaces don't persist through stop/start/restarts.
346: 	if n.Cfg.Tmpfs == nil {
347: 		n.Cfg.Tmpfs = map[string]string{}
348: 	}
349: 
350: 	n.Cfg.Tmpfs["/run/netns"] = "rw,nosuid,nodev,noexec"
351: 
352: 	n.InterfaceRegexp = InterfaceRegexp
353: 	n.InterfaceHelp = InterfaceHelp
354: 
355: 	return n.initNetnsNode()
356: }
357: 
358: func (n *srl) PreDeploy(ctx context.Context, params *clabnodes.PreDeployParams) error {
359: 	clabutils.CreateDirectory(n.Cfg.LabDir, clabconstants.PermissionsOpen)
360: 
361: 	// store provided pubkeys
362: 	n.sshPubKeys = params.SSHPubKeys
363: 
364: 	// store the certificate-related parameters
365: 	// for cert generation to happen in Post-Deploy phase with mgmt IPs as SANs
366: 	n.cert = params.Cert
367: 	n.topologyName = params.TopologyName
368: 
369: 	// platform specific pre-deploy actions
370: 	// modular chassis boot in the mode required by the line card generation they are populated
371: 	// with, unless the user pinned the mode explicitly.
372: 	if n.Config().Env["SRL_CHASSIS_MODE"] == "" {
373: 		topology, err := resolveSRLTopology(n.Cfg)
374: 		if err != nil {
375: 			return err
376: 		}
377: 
378: 		if topology.ChassisMode != "" {
379: 			n.Config().Env["SRL_CHASSIS_MODE"] = topology.ChassisMode
380: 		}
381: 	}
382: 
383: 	return n.createSRLFiles()
384: }
385: 
386: func (n *srl) PostDeploy(ctx context.Context, params *clabnodes.PostDeployParams) error {
387: 	log.Info("Running postdeploy actions",
388: 		"kind", n.Cfg.Kind,
389: 		"node", n.Cfg.ShortName)
390: 
391: 	// generate the certificate
392: 	certificate, err := n.LoadOrGenerateCertificate(n.cert, n.topologyName)
393: 	if err != nil {
394: 		return err
395: 	}
396: 
397: 	// set the certificate data
398: 	n.Config().TLSCert = string(certificate.Cert)
399: 	n.Config().TLSKey = string(certificate.Key)
400: 
401: 	// Populate /etc/hosts for service discovery on mgmt interface
402: 	if err := n.populateHosts(ctx, params.Nodes); err != nil {
403: 		log.Warnf("Unable to populate hosts for node %q: %v", n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
404: 	}
405: 
406: 	// start waiting for initial commit and mgmt server ready
407: 	if err := n.Ready(ctx); err != nil {
408: 		return err
409: 	}
410: 
411: 	n.swVersion, err = n.RunningVersion(ctx)
412: 	if err != nil {
413: 		return err
414: 	}
415: 
416: 	n.supportsOpenconfig, err = n.OpenConfigFeatureEnabled(ctx)
417: 	if err != nil {
418: 		return err
419: 	}
420: 
421: 	// return if config file is found in the lab directory.
422: 	// This can be either if the startup-config has been mounted by that path
423: 	// or the config has been previously generated and saved
424: 	if clabutils.FileExists(filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, "config", "config.json")) {
425: 		return nil
426: 	}
427: 
428: 	if err := n.addDefaultConfig(ctx); err != nil {
429: 		return err
430: 	}
431: 
432: 	if err := n.addOverlayCLIConfig(ctx); err != nil {
433: 		return err
434: 	}
435: 
436: 	// once default and overlay config is added, we can commit the config
437: 	if err := n.commitConfig(ctx); err != nil {
438: 		return err
439: 	}
440: 
441: 	return n.generateCheckpoint(ctx)
442: }
443: 
444: func (n *srl) SaveConfig(ctx context.Context) (*clabnodes.SaveConfigResult, error) {
445: 	cmd, _ := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(saveCmd)
446: 
447: 	execResult, err := n.RunExec(ctx, cmd)
448: 	if err != nil {
449: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: failed to execute cmd: %v", n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
450: 	}
451: 
452: 	if execResult.GetStdErrString() != "" {
453: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s errors: %s", n.Cfg.ShortName, execResult.GetStdErrString())
454: 	}
455: 
456: 	cfgPath := filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, "config", "config.json")
457: 
458: 	log.Infof(
459: 		"saved SR Linux configuration from %s node. Output:\n%s",
460: 		n.Cfg.ShortName,
461: 		execResult.GetStdOutString(),
462: 	)
463: 
464: 	return &clabnodes.SaveConfigResult{
465: 		ConfigPath: cfgPath,
466: 	}, nil
467: }
468: 
469: // Ready returns when the node boot sequence reached the stage when it is ready to accept config
470: // commands
471: // returns an error if not ready by the expiry of the timer readyTimeout.
472: func (n *srl) Ready(ctx context.Context) error {
473: 	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, readyTimeout)
474: 	defer cancel()
475: 
476: 	var err error
477: 
478: 	log.Debugf("Waiting for SR Linux node %q to boot...", n.Cfg.ShortName)
479: 
480: 	for {
481: 		select {
482: 		case <-ctx.Done():
483: 			return fmt.Errorf(
484: 				"timed out waiting for SR Linux node %s to boot: %v",
485: 				n.Cfg.ShortName,
486: 				err,
487: 			)
488: 		default:
489: 			// two commands are checked, first if the mgmt_server is running
490: 			cmd, _ := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(mgmtServerRdyCmd)
491: 
492: 			execResult, err := n.RunExec(ctx, cmd)
493: 			if err != nil || (execResult != nil && execResult.GetReturnCode() != 0) {
494: 				logMsg := "mgmt_server status check failed"
495: 
496: 				if err != nil {
497: 					logMsg += fmt.Sprintf(" error: %v", err)
498: 				}
499: 
500: 				if execResult != nil && execResult.GetReturnCode() != 0 {
501: 					logMsg += fmt.Sprintf(", output: \n%s", execResult)
502: 				}
503: 
504: 				log.Debug(logMsg)
505: 				time.Sleep(retryTimer)
506: 
507: 				continue
508: 			}
509: 
510: 			if execResult.GetStdErrString() != "" {
511: 				log.Debugf(
512: 					"error during checking SR Linux boot status: %s",
513: 					execResult.GetStdErrString(),
514: 				)
515: 				time.Sleep(retryTimer)
516: 
517: 				continue
518: 			}
519: 
520: 			if !strings.Contains(execResult.GetStdOutString(), "running") {
521: 				time.Sleep(retryTimer)
522: 
523: 				continue
524: 			}
525: 
526: 			// once mgmt server is running, we need to check if it is ready to accept configuration
527: 			// commands
528: 			// this is done with checking readyForConfigCmd
529: 			cmd, _ = clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(readyForConfigCmd)
530: 
531: 			execResult, err = n.RunExec(ctx, cmd)
532: 			if err != nil {
533: 				log.Debugf("error during readyForConfigCmd execution: %s", err)
534: 				time.Sleep(retryTimer)
535: 
536: 				continue
537: 			}
538: 
539: 			if execResult.GetStdErrString() != "" {
540: 				log.Debugf("readyForConfigCmd stderr: %s", execResult.GetStdErrString())
541: 				time.Sleep(retryTimer)
542: 
543: 				continue
544: 			}
545: 
546: 			if !strings.Contains(execResult.GetStdOutString(), "loaded initial configuration") {
547: 				log.Debugf(
548: 					"Management server readiness files doesn't contain the marker string %s",
549: 					execResult.GetStdOutString(),
550: 				)
551: 				time.Sleep(retryTimer)
552: 
553: 				continue
554: 			}
555: 
556: 			log.Debugf("Node %s is ready to accept configs", n.Cfg.ShortName)
557: 
558: 			return nil
559: 		}
560: 	}
561: }
562: 
563: // checkKernelVersion emits a warning if the present kernel version is lower than the required one.
564: func (*srl) checkKernelVersion() error {
565: 	// retrieve running kernel version
566: 	kv, err := clabutils.GetKernelVersion()
567: 	if err != nil {
568: 		return err
569: 	}
570: 
571: 	// do the comparison
572: 	if !kv.GreaterOrEqual(requiredKernelVersion) {
573: 		log.Infof(
574: 			"Nokia SR Linux v23.3.1+ requires a kernel version greater than %s. Detected kernel version: %s",
575: 			requiredKernelVersion,
576: 			kv,
577: 		)
578: 	}
579: 
580: 	return nil
581: }
582: 
583: func (n *srl) CheckDeploymentConditions(ctx context.Context) error {
584: 	// perform the srl specific kernel version check
585: 	err := n.checkKernelVersion()
586: 	if err != nil {
587: 		return err
588: 	}
589: 
590: 	return n.DefaultNode.CheckDeploymentConditions(ctx)
591: }
592: 
593: func (n *srl) createSRLFiles() error {
594: 	log.Debugf("Creating directory structure for SRL container: %s", n.Cfg.ShortName)
595: 
596: 	var src string
597: 
598: 	if n.Cfg.License != "" {
599: 		// copy license file to node specific directory in lab
600: 		src = n.Cfg.License
601: 
602: 		licPath := filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, "license.key")
603: 		if err := clabutils.CopyFile(context.Background(), src, licPath,
604: 			clabconstants.PermissionsFileDefault); err != nil {
605: 			return fmt.Errorf("CopyFile src %s -> dst %s failed %v", src, licPath, err)
606: 		}
607: 
608: 		log.Debugf("CopyFile src %s -> dst %s succeeded", src, licPath)
609: 	}
610: 
611: 	// generate SRL topology file, including base MAC
612: 	err := generateSRLTopologyFile(n.Cfg)
613: 	if err != nil {
614: 		return err
615: 	}
616: 
617: 	clabutils.CreateDirectory(path.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, "config"),
618: 		clabconstants.PermissionsOpen)
619: 
620: 	// create repository files (for yum/apt) that
621: 	// are mounted to srl container during the init phase
622: 	err = n.createRepoFiles()
623: 	if err != nil {
624: 		return err
625: 	}
626: 
627: 	// generate a startup config file
628: 	// if the node has a `startup-config:` statement, the file specified in that section
629: 	// will be used as a template in GenerateConfig()
630: 	var cfgTemplate string
631: 
632: 	cfgPath := filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, "config", "config.json")
633: 	if n.Cfg.StartupConfig != "" {
634: 		log.Debug("Reading startup-config", "file", n.Cfg.StartupConfig)
635: 
636: 		c, err := os.ReadFile(n.Cfg.StartupConfig)
637: 		if err != nil {
638: 			return err
639: 		}
640: 
641: 		cBuf, err := clabutils.SubstituteEnvsAndTemplate(bytes.NewReader(c), n.Cfg)
642: 		if err != nil {
643: 			return err
644: 		}
645: 
646: 		// Determine if startup-config is a JSON file
647: 		// Get slice of data with optional leading whitespace removed.
648: 		// See RFC 7159, Section 2 for the definition of JSON whitespace.
649: 		x := bytes.TrimLeft(cBuf.Bytes(), " \t\r\n")
650: 
651: 		isJSON := len(x) > 0 && x[0] == '{'
652: 		if !isJSON {
653: 			log.Debugf(
654: 				"startup-config passed to %s is in the CLI format. Will apply it in post-deploy stage",
655: 				n.Cfg.ShortName,
656: 			)
657: 
658: 			n.startupCliCfg = cBuf.Bytes()
659: 
660: 			// no need to generate and mount startup-config passed in a CLI format
661: 			// as we will apply it over the top of a default config in the post deploy stage
662: 			return nil
663: 		}
664: 
665: 		cfgTemplate = cBuf.String()
666: 	}
667: 
668: 	if cfgTemplate == "" {
669: 		log.Debugf(
670: 			"configuration template for node %s is empty, skipping startup config file generation",
671: 			n.Cfg.ShortName,
672: 		)
673: 
674: 		return nil
675: 	}
676: 
677: 	err = n.GenerateConfig(cfgPath, cfgTemplate)
678: 	if err != nil {
679: 		log.Errorf("node=%s, failed to generate config: %v", n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
680: 	}
681: 
682: 	return err
683: }
684: 
685: // srlTemplateData top level data struct.
686: type srlTemplateData struct {
687: 	TLSKey     string
688: 	TLSCert    string
689: 	TLSAnchor  string
690: 	TLSConfig  string
691: 	Banner     string
692: 	IFaces     map[string]tplIFace
693: 	SSHPubKeys string
694: 	MgmtMTU    int
695: 	MgmtIPMTU  int
696: 	DNSServers []string
697: 	// EnableGNMIUnixSockServices enables GNMI unix socket services
698: 	// for the node. This is needed for "23.10 <= ver < 24.3" versions
699: 	EnableGNMIUnixSockServices bool
700: 	// EnableCustomPrompt enables custom prompt with added newline
701: 	// before the prompt.
702: 	EnableCustomPrompt bool
703: 	CustomPrompt       string
704: 	// SNMPConfig is a string containing SNMP configuration
705: 	SNMPConfig string
706: 	// GRPCConfig is a string containing GRPC configuration
707: 	GRPCConfig string
708: 	// ACLConfig is a string containing ACL configuration
709: 	ACLConfig string
710: 	// NetconfConfig is a string containing Netconf server configuration
711: 	NetconfConfig string
712: 	// EDAConfig is a string containing EDA configuration
713: 	EDAConfig string
714: 	// OCServerConfig is a string containing OpenConfig server configuration
715: 	OCServerConfig string
716: 	// NDKServerConfig is a string containing NDK server configuration
717: 	NDKServerConfig string
718: 	// DNSServersConfig is a string containing DNS servers configuration
719: 	DNSServersConfig string
720: }
721: 
722: // tplIFace template interface struct.
723: type tplIFace struct {
724: 	BaseName    string // base interface excl. breakout
725: 	FullName    string // full interface name incl. breakout
726: 	HasBreakout bool
727: 	Mtu         int
728: 	IPv4        string
729: 	IPv6        string
730: }
731: 
732: // addDefaultConfig adds srl default configuration such as tls certs, gnmi/json-rpc, login-banner.
733: func (n *srl) addDefaultConfig(ctx context.Context) error {
734: 	b, err := n.banner()
735: 	if err != nil {
736: 		return err
737: 	}
738: 
739: 	var dnsServers []string
740: 	if n.Config().DNS != nil {
741: 		dnsServers = n.Config().DNS.Servers
742: 	}
743: 
744: 	// tplData holds data used in templating of the default config snippet
745: 	tplData := srlTemplateData{
746: 		TLSKey:           n.Cfg.TLSKey,
747: 		TLSCert:          n.Cfg.TLSCert,
748: 		TLSAnchor:        n.Cfg.TLSAnchor,
749: 		Banner:           b,
750: 		IFaces:           map[string]tplIFace{},
751: 		MgmtMTU:          0,
752: 		MgmtIPMTU:        0,
753: 		DNSServers:       dnsServers,
754: 		SNMPConfig:       snmpv2Config,
755: 		GRPCConfig:       grpcConfig,
756: 		OCServerConfig:   "",
757: 		NDKServerConfig:  "",
758: 		DNSServersConfig: "",
759: 	}
760: 
761: 	if err := n.setVersionSpecificParams(&tplData); err != nil {
762: 		return err
763: 	}
764: 
765: 	n.setCustomPrompt(&tplData)
766: 
767: 	// set MgmtMTU to the MTU value of the runtime management network
768: 	// so that the two MTUs match.
769: 	tplData.MgmtIPMTU = n.Runtime.Mgmt().MTU
770: 
771: 	if err := n.populateInterfaceConfig(&tplData); err != nil {
772: 		return err
773: 	}
774: 
775: 	buf := new(bytes.Buffer)
776: 
777: 	err = srlCfgTpl.Execute(buf, tplData)
778: 	if err != nil {
779: 		return err
780: 	}
781: 
782: 	log.Debugf("Node %q additional config:\n%s", n.Cfg.ShortName, buf.String())
783: 
784: 	// Copy overlay config to container
785: 	tmpFilePath, err := clabutils.WriteToTempFile(buf.String())
786: 	if err != nil {
787: 		return err
788: 	}
789: 	defer os.Remove(tmpFilePath)
790: 
791: 	err = n.Runtime.CopyToContainer(ctx, n.Cfg.LongName, defaultCfgPath, tmpFilePath)
792: 	if err != nil {
793: 		return fmt.Errorf("error copying configuration to container: %w", err)
794: 	}
795: 
796: 	// su to admin user to apply the default config
797: 	// to make sure that the 'environment save' command will create
798: 	// files with correct permissions
799: 	cmd := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromSlice([]string{
800: 		"bash", "-c",
801: 		fmt.Sprintf("su -s /bin/bash admin -c '/opt/srlinux/bin/sr_cli -ed < %s'", defaultCfgPath),
802: 	})
803: 
804: 	execResult, err := n.RunExec(ctx, cmd)
805: 	if err != nil {
806: 		return err
807: 	}
808: 
809: 	log.Debugf(
810: 		"node %s. stdout: %s, stderr: %s",
811: 		n.Cfg.ShortName,
812: 		execResult.GetStdOutString(),
813: 		execResult.GetStdErrString(),
814: 	)
815: 
816: 	return nil
817: }
818: 
819: // populateInterfaceConfig adds endpoint settings to the default configuration,
820: // returning an error for interface names that cannot be mapped to SR Linux interfaces.
821: func (n *srl) populateInterfaceConfig(tplData *srlTemplateData) error {
822: 	const ethernetSplitParts = 3
823: 
824: 	const ethernetMTUOverhead = 14
825: 
826: 	for _, e := range n.Endpoints {
827: 		ifName := e.GetIfaceName()
828: 
829: 		// Runtime-discovered endpoints bypass topology interface-name validation.
830: 		if !normalizedInterfaceRegexp.MatchString(ifName) {
831: 			return fmt.Errorf(
832: 				"invalid SR Linux interface %q: expected %s or %s",
833: 				ifName, mgmt0InterfaceName, InterfaceHelp,
834: 			)
835: 		}
836: 
837: 		// Restored runtime endpoints may have no topology link or configured MTU.
838: 		mtu := clabconstants.DefaultLinkMTU
839: 		if link := e.GetLink(); link != nil {
840: 			mtu = link.GetMTU()
841: 		}
842: 
843: 		if ifName == mgmt0InterfaceName {
844: 			if mtu != clabconstants.DefaultLinkMTU {
845: 				tplData.MgmtMTU = mtu
846: 				tplData.MgmtIPMTU = mtu - ethernetMTUOverhead
847: 			}
848: 
849: 			continue
850: 		}
851: 
852: 		ifNameParts := strings.SplitN(strings.TrimPrefix(ifName, "e"), "-", ethernetSplitParts)
853: 
854: 		iface := tplIFace{}
855: 
856: 		iface.BaseName = fmt.Sprintf("ethernet-%s/%s", ifNameParts[0], ifNameParts[1])
857: 		if len(ifNameParts) == ethernetSplitParts {
858: 			iface.FullName = fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s", iface.BaseName, ifNameParts[2])
859: 			iface.HasBreakout = true
860: 		} else {
861: 			iface.FullName = iface.BaseName
862: 		}
863: 
864: 		if mtu != clabconstants.DefaultLinkMTU {
865: 			iface.Mtu = mtu
866: 		}
867: 
868: 		if a := e.GetIPv4Addr(); a.IsValid() {
869: 			iface.IPv4 = a.String()
870: 		}
871: 
872: 		if a := e.GetIPv6Addr(); a.IsValid() {
873: 			iface.IPv6 = a.String()
874: 		}
875: 
876: 		tplData.IFaces[ifName] = iface
877: 	}
878: 
879: 	return nil
880: }
881: 
882: // addOverlayCLIConfig adds CLI formatted config that is read out of a file provided via
883: // startup-config directive.
884: func (n *srl) addOverlayCLIConfig(ctx context.Context) error {
885: 	if len(n.startupCliCfg) == 0 {
886: 		log.Debugf(
887: 			"node %q: startup-config empty, committing existing candidate",
888: 			n.Config().ShortName,
889: 		)
890: 
891: 		return nil
892: 	}
893: 
894: 	cfgStr := string(n.startupCliCfg)
895: 
896: 	log.Debugf(
897: 		"Node %q additional config from startup-config file %s:\n%s",
898: 		n.Cfg.ShortName,
899: 		n.Cfg.StartupConfig,
900: 		cfgStr,
901: 	)
902: 
903: 	// Copy overlay config to container
904: 	tmpFilePath, err := clabutils.WriteToTempFile(cfgStr)
905: 	if err != nil {
906: 		return err
907: 	}
908: 	defer os.Remove(tmpFilePath)
909: 
910: 	err = n.Runtime.CopyToContainer(ctx, n.Cfg.LongName, overlayCfgPath, tmpFilePath)
911: 	if err != nil {
912: 		return fmt.Errorf("error copying configuration to container: %w", err)
913: 	}
914: 
915: 	// Load overlay config
916: 	cmd := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromSlice([]string{
917: 		"bash", "-c",
918: 		fmt.Sprintf("su -s /bin/bash admin -c '/opt/srlinux/bin/sr_cli -ed < %s'", overlayCfgPath),
919: 	})
920: 
921: 	execResult, err := n.RunExec(ctx, cmd)
922: 	if err != nil {
923: 		return err
924: 	}
925: 
926: 	if execResult.GetStdErrString() != "" {
927: 		return fmt.Errorf("%w:%s", clabnodes.ErrCommandExecError, execResult.GetStdErrString())
928: 	}
929: 
930: 	log.Debugf(
931: 		"node %s. stdout: %s, stderr: %s",
932: 		n.Cfg.ShortName,
933: 		execResult.GetStdOutString(),
934: 		execResult.GetStdErrString(),
935: 	)
936: 
937: 	return nil
938: }
939: 
940: // commitConfig commits and saves default+overlay config to the startup-config file.
941: func (n *srl) commitConfig(ctx context.Context) error {
942: 	log.Debugf("Node %q: committing configuration", n.Cfg.ShortName)
943: 
944: 	cmd, err := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(`bash -c "/opt/srlinux/bin/sr_cli -ed commit save"`)
945: 	if err != nil {
946: 		return err
947: 	}
948: 
949: 	execResult, err := n.RunExec(ctx, cmd)
950: 	if err != nil {
951: 		return err
952: 	}
953: 
954: 	if execResult.GetStdErrString() != "" {
955: 		return fmt.Errorf("%w:%s", clabnodes.ErrCommandExecError, execResult.GetStdErrString())
956: 	}
957: 
958: 	log.Debugf(
959: 		"node %s. stdout: %s, stderr: %s",
960: 		n.Cfg.ShortName,
961: 		execResult.GetStdOutString(),
962: 		execResult.GetStdErrString(),
963: 	)
964: 
965: 	return nil
966: }
967: 
968: func (n *srl) generateCheckpoint(ctx context.Context) error {
969: 	cmd, err := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(
970: 		`bash -c '/opt/srlinux/bin/sr_cli /tools system configuration generate-checkpoint ` +
971: 			`name clab-initial comment \"set by containerlab\"'`,
972: 	)
973: 	if err != nil {
974: 		return err
975: 	}
976: 
977: 	execResult, err := n.RunExec(ctx, cmd)
978: 	if err != nil {
979: 		return err
980: 	}
981: 
982: 	if execResult.GetStdErrString() != "" {
983: 		return fmt.Errorf("%w:%s", clabnodes.ErrCommandExecError, execResult.GetStdErrString())
984: 	}
985: 
986: 	log.Debugf(
987: 		"node %s. stdout: %s, stderr: %s",
988: 		n.Cfg.ShortName,
989: 		execResult.GetStdOutString(),
990: 		execResult.GetStdErrString(),
991: 	)
992: 
993: 	return nil
994: }
995: 
996: // populateHosts adds container hostnames for other nodes of a lab to SR Linux /etc/hosts file
997: // to mitigate the fact that srlinux uses non default netns for management and thus
998: // can't leverage docker DNS service.
999: func (n *srl) populateHosts(ctx context.Context, nodes map[string]clabnodes.Node) error {
1000: 	hosts, err := n.Runtime.GetHostsPath(ctx, n.Cfg.LongName)
1001: 	if err != nil {
1002: 		log.Warnf("Unable to locate /etc/hosts file for srl node %v: %v", n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
1003: 		return err
1004: 	}
1005: 
1006: 	var entriesv4, entriesv6 bytes.Buffer
1007: 
1008: 	const (
1009: 		v4Prefix = "###### CLAB-v4-START ######"
1010: 		v4Suffix = "###### CLAB-v4-END ######"
1011: 		v6Prefix = "###### CLAB-v6-START ######"
1012: 		v6Suffix = "###### CLAB-v6-END ######"
1013: 	)
1014: 	fmt.Fprintf(&entriesv4, "\n%s\n", v4Prefix)
1015: 	fmt.Fprintf(&entriesv6, "\n%s\n", v6Prefix)
1016: 
1017: 	for node, params := range nodes {
1018: 		if v4 := params.Config().MgmtIPv4Address; v4 != "" {
1019: 			fmt.Fprintf(&entriesv4, "%s\t%s\n", v4, node)
1020: 		}
1021: 
1022: 		if v6 := params.Config().MgmtIPv6Address; v6 != "" {
1023: 			fmt.Fprintf(&entriesv6, "%s\t%s\n", v6, node)
1024: 		}
1025: 	}
1026: 
1027: 	fmt.Fprintf(&entriesv4, "%s\n", v4Suffix)
1028: 	fmt.Fprintf(&entriesv6, "%s\n", v6Suffix)
1029: 
1030: 	// world-writable for container /etc/hosts
1031: 	const hostsFilePerm = 0o666
1032: 
1033: 	file, err := os.OpenFile(hosts, os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, hostsFilePerm) // skipcq: GSC-G302
1034: 	if err != nil {
1035: 		log.Warnf("Unable to open /etc/hosts file for srl node %v: %v", n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
1036: 		return err
1037: 	}
1038: 
1039: 	_, err = file.Write(entriesv4.Bytes())
1040: 	if err != nil {
1041: 		return err
1042: 	}
1043: 
1044: 	_, err = file.Write(entriesv6.Bytes())
1045: 	if err != nil {
1046: 		return err
1047: 	}
1048: 
1049: 	return file.Close()
1050: }
1051: 
1052: func (n *srl) GetMappedInterfaceName(ifName string) (string, error) {
1053: 	captureGroups, err := clabutils.GetRegexpCaptureGroups(n.InterfaceRegexp, ifName)
1054: 	if err != nil {
1055: 		return "", err
1056: 	}
1057: 
1058: 	indexGroups := []string{"linecard", "port", "channel"}
1059: 	parsedIndices := make(map[string]int)
1060: 	foundIndices := make(map[string]bool)
1061: 
1062: 	for _, indexKey := range indexGroups {
1063: 		if index, found := captureGroups[indexKey]; found && index != "" {
1064: 			foundIndices[indexKey] = true
1065: 
1066: 			parsedIndices[indexKey], err = strconv.Atoi(index)
1067: 			if err != nil {
1068: 				return "", fmt.Errorf(
1069: 					"%q parsed %s index %q could not be cast to an integer",
1070: 					ifName,
1071: 					indexKey,
1072: 					index,
1073: 				)
1074: 			}
1075: 
1076: 			if parsedIndices[indexKey] < 1 {
1077: 				return "", fmt.Errorf(
1078: 					"%q parsed %q index %q does not match requirement >= 1",
1079: 					ifName,
1080: 					indexKey,
1081: 					index,
1082: 				)
1083: 			}
1084: 		} else {
1085: 			foundIndices[indexKey] = false
1086: 		}
1087: 	}
1088: 
1089: 	if foundIndices["linecard"] && foundIndices["port"] {
1090: 		if foundIndices["channel"] {
1091: 			return fmt.Sprintf("e%d-%d-%d", parsedIndices["linecard"],
1092: 				parsedIndices["port"], parsedIndices["channel"]), nil
1093: 		} else {
1094: 			return fmt.Sprintf("e%d-%d", parsedIndices["linecard"], parsedIndices["port"]), nil
1095: 		}
1096: 	} else {
1097: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("%q missing linecard or port index", ifName)
1098: 	}
1099: }
1100: 
1101: // CheckInterfaceName checks if a name of the interface referenced in the topology file correct.
1102: func (n *srl) CheckInterfaceName() error {
1103: 	nm := strings.ToLower(n.Cfg.NetworkMode)
1104: 
1105: 	err := n.CheckInterfaceOverlap()
1106: 	if err != nil {
1107: 		return err
1108: 	}
1109: 
1110: 	for _, e := range n.Endpoints {
1111: 		if !normalizedInterfaceRegexp.MatchString(e.GetIfaceName()) {
1112: 			return fmt.Errorf(
1113: 				"nokia sr linux interface name %q doesn't match the required pattern: %s",
1114: 				e.GetIfaceName(),
1115: 				n.InterfaceHelp,
1116: 			)
1117: 		}
1118: 
1119: 		if e.GetIfaceName() == mgmt0InterfaceName && nm != "none" {
1120: 			return fmt.Errorf(
1121: 				"mgmt0 interface name is not allowed for %s node when network mode is not set to none",
1122: 				n.Cfg.ShortName,
1123: 			)
1124: 		}
1125: 	}
1126: 
1127: 	return nil
1128: }

1151: 	if err != nil {
1152: 		return err

1157: 		n.Cfg.Binds,
1158: 		yumPath+":/etc/yum.repos.d/srlinux.repo:ro",
1159: 		aptPath+":/etc/apt/sources.list.d/srlinux.list:ro")

1161: 	return nil

(n *srl) PostDeploy(ctx context.Context, params *clabnodes.PostDeployParams) error {\n387: \tlog.Info(\"Running postdeploy actions\",\n388: \t\t\"kind\", n.Cfg.Kind,\n389: \t\t\"node\", \n444: func (n *srl) SaveConfig(ctx context.Context) (*clabnodes.SaveConfigResult, error) {\n445: \tcmd, _ := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(saveCmd)\n446: \n447: \texecResult, err := n.RunExec(ctx, }\n468: \n469: // Ready returns when the node boot sequence reached the stage when it is ready to accept config\n470: // commands\n471: // returns an error if not ready by the expiry of the timer readyTimeout.\n472: func (n *srl) Ready(ctx context.Context) error {\n473: \tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, readyTimeout)\n474: \tdefer cancel()\n475: \n476: \tvar err error\n477: \n478: \tlog.Debugf(\"Waiting for SR Linux node %q to boot...\", n.Cfg.ShortName)\n479: \n480: \tfor {\n481: \t\tselect {\n482: \t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n483: \t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n484: \t\t\t\t\"timed out running\n490: \t\t\tcmd, _ := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(mgmtServerRdyCmd)\n491: \n492: \t\t\texecResult, err := n.RunExec(ctx, cmd)\n493: \t\t\tif err != nil || (execResult != nil && execResult.GetReturnCode() != 0) {\n494: \t\t\t\tlogMsg := \"mgmt_server status check failed\"\n495: \n496: \t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n497: \t\t\t\t\tlogMsg += fmt.Sprintf(\" error: %v\", err)\n498: \t\t\t\t}\n499: \n500: \t\t\t\tif execResult != nil && execResult.GetReturnCode() != 0 {\n501: \t\t\t\t\tlogMsg += fmt.Sprintf(\", output: \\n%s\", execResult)\n502: \t\t\t\t}\n503: \n504: \t\t\t\tlog.Debug(logMsg)\n505: \t\t\t\ttime.Sleep(retryTimer)\n506: \n507: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n508: \t\t\t}\n509: \n510: \t\t\tif execResult.GetStdErrString() != \"\" {\n511: \t\t\t\tlog.Debugf(\n512: \t\t\t\t\t\"error during checking SR Linux boot status: %s\",\n513: \t\t\t\t\texecResult.GetStdErrString(),\n514: \t\t\t\t)\n515: \t\t\t\ttime.Sleep(retryTimer)\n516: \n517: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n518: \t\t\t}\n519: \n520: \t\t\tif !strings.Contains(execResult.GetStdOutString(), \"running\") {\n521: \t\t\t\ttime.Sleep(retryTimer)\n522: \n523: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n524: \t\t\t}\n525: \n526: \t\t\t// once mgmt server is running, we need to check if it is ready to accept configuration\n527: \t\t\t// commands\n528: \t\t\t// this is done with checking readyForConfigCmd\n529: \t\t\tcmd, _ = clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(readyForConfigCmd)\n530: \n531: \t\t\texecResult, err = n.RunExec(ctx, cmd)\n532: \t\t\tif err != nil {\n533: \t\t\t\tlog.Debugf(\"error during readyForConfigCmd execution: %s\", err)\n534: \t\t\t\ttime.Sleep(retryTimer)\n535: \n536: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n537: \t\t\t}\n538: \n539: \t\t\tif execResult.GetStdErrString() != \"\" {\n540: \t\t\t\tlog.Debugf(\"readyForConfigCmd stderr: %s\", execResult.GetStdErrString())\n541: \t\t\t\ttime.Sleep(retryTimer)\n542: \n543: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n544: \t\t\t}\n545: \n546: \t\t\tif !strings.Contains(execResult.GetStdOutString(), \"loaded initial configuration\") {\n547: \t\t\t\tlog.Debugf(\n548: \t\t\t\t\t\"Management server readiness files doesn't contain the marker string %s\",\n549: \t\t\t\t\texecResult.GetStdOutString(),\n550: \t\t\t\t)\n551: \t\t\t\ttime.Sleep(retryTimer)\n552: \n553: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n554: \t\t\t}\n555: \n556: \t\t\tlog.Debugf(\"Node %s is ready to accept configs\", n.Cfg.ShortName)\n557: \n558: \t\t\treturn nil\n559: \t\t}\n560: \t}\n561: }\n562: \n563: // checkKernelVersion emits a warning if the present kernel version is lower than the for interface names that cannot be mapped to SR Linux interfaces.\n821: func (n *srl) populateInterfaceConfig(tplData *srlTemplateData) error {\n822: \tconst ethernetSplitParts = 3\n823: \n824: \tconst ethernetMTUOverhead = 14\n825: \n826: \tfor _, e := range n.Endpoints {\n827: \t\tifName := e.GetIfaceName()\n828: \n829: \t\t// Runtime-discovered endpoints bypass topology interface-name validation.\n830: \t\tif !normalizedInterfaceRegexp.MatchString(ifName) {\n831: \t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n832: \t\t\t\t\"invalid SR Linux interface %q: expected %s or %s\",\n833: \t\t\t\tifName, mgmt0InterfaceName, InterfaceHelp,\n834: \t\t\t)\n835: \t\t}\n836: \n837: \t\t// Restored runtime endpoints may have no topology link or configured MTU.\n838: \t\tmtu := clabconstants.DefaultLinkMTU\n839: \t\tif link := e.GetLink(); link != nil {\n840: \t\t\tmtu = link.GetMTU()\n841: \t\t}\n842: \n843: \t\tif ifName == mgmt0InterfaceName {\n844: \t\t\tif mtu != clabconstants.DefaultLinkMTU {\n845: \t\t\t\ttplData.MgmtMTU = mtu\n846: \t\t\t\ttplData.MgmtIPMTU = mtu - ethernetMTUOverhead\n847: \t\t\t}\n848: \n849: \t\t\tcontinue\n850: \t\t}\n851: \n852: \t\tifNameParts := strings.SplitN(strings.TrimPrefix(ifName, \"e\"), \"-\", ethernetSplitParts)\n853: \n854: \t\tiface := tplIFace{}\n855: \n856: \t\tiface.BaseName = fmt.Sprintf(\"ethernet-%s/%s\", ifNameParts[0], ifNameParts[1])\n857: \t\tif len(ifNameParts) == ethernetSplitParts {\n858: \t\t\tiface.FullName = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s\", iface.BaseName, ifNameParts[2])\n859: \t\t\tiface.HasBreakout = true\n860: \t\t} else {\n861: \t\t\tiface.FullName = iface.BaseName\n862: \t\t}\n863: \n864: \t\tif mtu != clabconstants.DefaultLinkMTU {\n865: \t\t\tiface.Mtu = mtu\n866: \t\t}\n867: \n868: \t\tif a := e.GetIPv4Addr(); a.IsValid() {\n869: \t\t\tiface.IPv4 = a.String()\n870: \t\t}\n871: \n872: \t\tif a := e.GetIPv6Addr(); a.IsValid() {\n873: \t\t\tiface.IPv6 = a.String()\n874: \t\t}\n875: \n876: \t\ttplData.IFaces[ifName] = iface\n877: \t}\n878: \n879: \treturn nil\n880: }\n881: \n882: // addOverlayCLIConfig adds CLI formatted config that is read out of a file provided via\n883: // sequence reached the stage when it is ready to accept config\n// commands\n// returns an error if not ready by the expiry of the timer readyTimeout.\nfunc (n *srl) Ready(ctx context.Context) error {\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, readyTimeout)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tvar err error\n\n\tlog.Debugf(\"Waiting for SR Linux node %q to boot...\", n.Cfg.ShortName)\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\t\"timed out waiting for SR Linux node %s to boot: %v\",\n\t\t\t\tn.Cfg.ShortName,\n\t\t\t\terr,\n\t\t\t)\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t// two (execResult != nil && execResult.GetReturnCode() != 0) {\n\t\t\t\tlogMsg := \"mgmt_server status check failed\"\n\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tlogMsg += fmt.Sprintf(\" error: %v\", execResult)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tlog.Debug(logMsg)\n\t\t\t\ttime.Sleep(retryTimer)\n\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif execResult.GetStdErrString() != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Debugf(\n\t\t\t\t\t\"error during checking SR Linux boot status: {\n\t\t\t\ttime.Sleep(retryTimer)\n\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// once mgmt server is running, we need to check if it is ready to accept configuration\n\t\t\t// commands\n\t\t\t// this is {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Debugf(\n\t\t\t\t\t\"Management server readiness files doesn't contain the marker string %s\",\n\t\t\t\t\texecResult.GetStdOutString(),\n\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t\ttime.Sleep(retryTimer)\n\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tlog.Debugf(\"Node %s is ready to accept configs\", n.Cfg.ShortName)\n\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// checkKernelVersion emits a warning if the present kernel version is lower than the required one.\nfunc (*srl) checkKernelVersion() error string\n\tIPv6        string\n}\n\n// addDefaultConfig adds srl default configuration such as tls certs, gnmi/json-rpc, login-banner.\nfunc (n *srl) addDefaultConfig(ctx context.Context) error {\n\tb,     grpcConfig,\n\t\tOCServerConfig:   \"\",\n\t\tNDKServerConfig:  \"\",\n\t\tDNSServersConfig: \"\",\n\t}\n\n\tif err := n.setVersionSpecificParams(&tplData); err != nil {\n\t\treturn configuration,\n// returning an error for interface names that cannot be mapped to SR Linux interfaces.\nfunc (n *srl) populateInterfaceConfig(tplData *srlTemplateData) error {\n\tconst ethernetSplitParts = 3\n\n\tconst ethernetMTUOverhead = 14\n\n\tfor _, e := range n.Endpoints {\n\t\tifName := e.GetIfaceName()\n\n\t\t// Runtime-discovered endpoints bypass topology interface-name validation.\n\t\tif !normalizedInterfaceRegexp.MatchString(ifName) {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\t\"invalid SR Linux interface %q: expected %s or %s\",\n\t\t\t\tifName, mgmt0InterfaceName, InterfaceHelp,\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Restored runtime endpoints may have no topology link or configured MTU.\n\t\tmtu := clabconstants.DefaultLinkMTU\n\t\tif link := e.GetLink(); link != nil {\n\t\t\tmtu = link.GetMTU()\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif ifName == mgmt0InterfaceName {\n\t\t\tif mtu != clabconstants.DefaultLinkMTU {\n\t\t\t\ttplData.MgmtMTU = mtu\n\t\t\t\ttplData.MgmtIPMTU = mtu - ethernetMTUOverhead\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tifNameParts := strings.SplitN(strings.TrimPrefix(ifName, \"e\"), \"-\", ethernetSplitParts)\n\n\t\tiface := tplIFace{}\n\n\t\tiface.BaseName = fmt.Sprintf(\"ethernet-%s/%s\", ifNameParts[0], ifNameParts[1])\n\t\tif len(ifNameParts) == ethernetSplitParts {\n\t\t\tiface.FullName = fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s\", iface.BaseName, ifNameParts[2])\n\t\t\tiface.HasBreakout = true\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tiface.FullName = iface.BaseName\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif mtu != clabconstants.DefaultLinkMTU {\n\t\t\tiface.Mtu = mtu\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif a := e.GetIPv4Addr(); a.IsValid() {\n\t\t\tiface.IPv4 = a.String()\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif a := e.GetIPv6Addr(); a.IsValid() {\n\t\t\tiface.IPv6 = 1: package sros
2: 
3: import (
4: 	"context"
5: 	"errors"
6: 	"fmt"
7: 	"maps"
8: 
9: 	clabutils "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/utils"
10: 
11: 	clabconstants "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/constants"
12: 	clabnodes "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/nodes"
13: 	clabruntime "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/runtime"
14: 	clabtypes "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/types"
15: 	"github.com/vishvananda/netns"
16: )
17: 
18: // namespaceNode owns the shared network namespace of a components-based SR-SIM node.
19: type namespaceNode struct {
20: 	clabnodes.DefaultNode
21: }
22: 
23: func (n *namespaceNode) Init(cfg *clabtypes.NodeConfig, opts ...clabnodes.NodeOption) error {
24: 	n.DefaultNode = *clabnodes.NewDefaultNode(n)
25: 	n.Cfg = cfg
26: 	n.StopSignal = clabtypes.SIGTERM
27: 
28: 	for _, o := range opts {
29: 		o(n)
30: 	}
31: 
32: 	return nil
33: }
34: 
35: func (n *sros) newNetnsConfig() *clabtypes.NodeConfig {
36: 	labels := maps.Clone(n.Cfg.Labels)
37: 
38: 	if labels == nil {
39: 		labels = map[string]string{}
40: 	}
41: 
42: 	shortName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-%s", n.Cfg.ShortName, "netns")
43: 	labels[clabconstants.NodeName] = shortName
44: 
45: 	longName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-%s", n.Cfg.LongName, "netns")
46: 	labels[clabconstants.LongName] = longName
47: 
48: 	labels[clabconstants.NodeType] = "srsim-netns"
49: 	labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] = "true"
50: 
51: 	// this node will be the root node
52: 	labels[clabconstants.RootNodeName] = n.Cfg.ShortName
53: 	labels[clabconstants.RootNodeLongName] = n.Cfg.LongName
54: 
55: 	return &clabtypes.NodeConfig{
56: 		ShortName:             shortName,
57: 		LongName:              longName,
58: 		LabDir:                n.Cfg.LabDir,
59: 		Index:                 n.Cfg.Index,
60: 		Kind:                  n.Cfg.Kind,
61: 		NodeType:              "srsim-netns",
62: 		Image:                 n.Cfg.Image,
63: 		ImagePullPolicy:       n.Cfg.ImagePullPolicy,
64: 		RestartPolicy:         n.Cfg.RestartPolicy,
65: 		Sysctls:               maps.Clone(n.Cfg.Sysctls),
66: 		User:                  "0:0",
67: 		Entrypoint:            "/bin/sh",
68: 		Cmd:                   `-c "trap 'exit 0' TERM INT; sleep infinity & wait $!"`,
69: 		Env:                   map[string]string{},
70: 		PortBindings:          n.Cfg.PortBindings,
71: 		PortSet:               n.Cfg.PortSet,
72: 		NetworkMode:           n.Cfg.NetworkMode,
73: 		MgmtNet:               n.Cfg.MgmtNet,
74: 		MgmtIntf:              n.Cfg.MgmtIntf,
75: 		MgmtIPv4Address:       n.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Address,
76: 		MgmtIPv4AddressAuto:   n.Cfg.MgmtIPv4AddressAuto,
77: 		MgmtIPv4PrefixLength:  n.Cfg.MgmtIPv4PrefixLength,
78: 		MgmtIPv6Address:       n.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Address,
79: 		MgmtIPv6AddressAuto:   n.Cfg.MgmtIPv6AddressAuto,
80: 		MgmtIPv6PrefixLength:  n.Cfg.MgmtIPv6PrefixLength,
81: 		MgmtIPv4Gateway:       n.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Gateway,
82: 		MgmtIPv6Gateway:       n.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Gateway,
83: 		MacAddress:            n.Cfg.MacAddress,
84: 		Aliases:               n.Cfg.Aliases,
85: 		DNS:                   n.Cfg.DNS,
86: 		ExtraHosts:            n.Cfg.ExtraHosts,
87: 		Labels:                labels,
88: 		Runtime:               n.Cfg.Runtime,
89: 		ResultingPortBindings: n.Cfg.ResultingPortBindings,
90: 	}
91: }
92: 
93: func (n *sros) ensureNetnsRunning(ctx context.Context) error {
94: 	switch status := n.netnsNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx); status {
95: 	case clabruntime.Running:
96: 		// The namespace holder intentionally survives regular node lifecycle operations.
97: 	case clabruntime.Created, clabruntime.Stopped:
98: 		if err := n.netnsNode.Start(ctx); err != nil {
99: 			return fmt.Errorf("node %q network namespace container start error: %w",
100: 				n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
101: 		}
102: 	case clabruntime.Paused:
103: 		if err := n.Runtime.UnpauseContainer(ctx, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName); err != nil {
104: 			return fmt.Errorf("node %q network namespace container unpause error: %w",
105: 				n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
106: 		}
107: 	case clabruntime.NotFound:
108: 		return fmt.Errorf(
109: 			"node %q network namespace container %q not found; recreate the node",
110: 			n.Cfg.ShortName, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName,
111: 		)
112: 	default:
113: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q network namespace container %q is %s",
114: 			n.Cfg.ShortName, n.netnsNode.Config().LongName, status)
115: 	}
116: 
117: 	return nil
118: }
119: 
120: func (n *sros) cleanupParkingNetNS() error {
121: 	name := clabutils.ParkingNetnsName(n.Cfg.LongName)
122: 	if _, err := clabutils.GetNamedNetNS(name); err != nil {
123: 		return nil
124: 	}
125: 
126: 	return netns.DeleteNamed(name)
127: }
128: 
129: // GetNSPath retrieves the Namespace Path.
130: func (n *sros) GetNSPath(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {
131: 	if n.isStandaloneNode() || (n.isDistributedCardNode() && n.rootCtrName == "") {
132: 		return n.DefaultNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
133: 	} else if n.isDistributedCardNode() {
134: 		return n.Runtime.GetNSPath(ctx, n.rootCtrName)
135: 	}
136: 	return n.netnsNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
137: }
138: 
139: // DeleteNetnsSymlink deletes the symlink file created for the container netns.
140: func (n *sros) DeleteNetnsSymlink() error {
141: 	var errs []error
142: 
143: 	// if it is the base node, then we need to delete the symlink for all the components.
144: 	if n.isDistributedBaseNode() {
145: 		for _, componentNode := range n.componentNodes {
146: 			errs = append(errs, componentNode.DeleteNetnsSymlink())
147: 		}
148: 		if n.netnsNode != nil {
149: 			errs = append(errs, n.netnsNode.DeleteNetnsSymlink())
150: 		}
151: 		errs = append(errs, clabutils.DeleteNetnsSymlink(n.Cfg.LongName))
152: 	}
153: 
154: 	errs = append(errs, n.DefaultNode.DeleteNetnsSymlink(), n.cleanupParkingNetNS())
155: 
156: 	return errors.Join(errs...)
157: }

Let me examine how sros handles Deploy/Stop/Delete/GetContainers etc., to compare and see what the srl version is missing. Also check how the sros netns node is integrated in the sros.go (e.g., in Deploy, and how links are handled for the holder).

Key areas to investigate:
1. How nodes are enumerated for destroy/inspect — does the srl netns holder get discovered via labels (`InternalNode`)? Check `constants` for `InternalNode`, `RootNodeName` etc.
2. The `Deploy` override in srl — does it skip things DefaultNode.Deploy does (like links setup, CheckDeploymentConditions, cert, env, DNS setup)? Let me check DefaultNode.Deploy.
3. `GetContainers` uses `cnts[0]` — if DefaultNode.GetContainers returns multiple containers matching the name (unlikely since filter by name match).
4. Stop order: DefaultNode.Stop stops the srl container first, then stops holder — but the holder is in the same netns... Actually wait, the comment says "Stop also stops the holder, so that the next start gets a fresh network namespace without interfaces left behind". The srl container is a "container:holder" network mode, so stopping the srl container keeps interfaces in the holder's netns? Actually veths attached to a netns stay. So they stop the holder too.
5. Start: ensureNetnsRunning then DefaultNode.Start then linkNetns. But DefaultNode.Start — what does it do? Check the DefaultNode implementation.
6. The `Deploy` override: does it handle endpoints? The DefaultNode.Deploy presumably creates links (the link setup happens elsewhere via the lab's deploy which calls netns setup). The `linkNetns` symlink points to the holder's NS path.

Let me read nodes/default_node.go and the constants.
