46: // DefaultNode implements the Node interface and is embedded to the structs of all other nodes.
47: // It has common fields and methods that every node should typically have. Nodes can override
48: // methods if needed.
49: type DefaultNode struct {
50: 	Cfg              *clabtypes.NodeConfig
51: 	Mgmt             *clabtypes.MgmtNet
52: 	Runtime          clabruntime.ContainerRuntime
53: 	HostRequirements *clabtypes.HostRequirements
54: 	// SSHConfig is the SSH client configuration that a clab node requires.
55: 	SSHConfig *clabtypes.SSHConfig
56: 	// Indicates that the node should not start without no license file defined
57: 	LicensePolicy clabtypes.LicensePolicy
58: 	// OverwriteNode stores the interface used to overwrite methods defined
59: 	// for DefaultNode, so that particular nodes can provide custom implementations.
60: 	OverwriteNode NodeOverwrites
61: 	// List of link endpoints that are connected to the node.
62: 	Endpoints []clablinks.Endpoint
63: 	// Preserve topology endpoint objects while they belong to the parking node.
64: 	parkingNode *clablinks.ParkingNode
65: 	// Interface aliasing-related variables
66: 	InterfaceRegexp       *regexp.Regexp
67: 	InterfaceMappedPrefix string
68: 	InterfaceOffset       int
69: 	InterfaceHelp         string
70: 	FirstDataIfIndex      int
71: 	// State of the node
72: 	state      clabnodesstate.NodeState
73: 	statemutex sync.RWMutex
74: 	StopSignal clabtypes.Signal
75: }
76: 
77: // NewDefaultNode initializes the DefaultNode structure and receives a NodeOverwrites interface
78: // which is implemented by the node struct of a particular kind.
79: // This allows DefaultNode to access fields of the specific node struct in the methods defined for
80: // DefaultNode.
81: func NewDefaultNode(n NodeOverwrites) *DefaultNode {
82: 	dn := &DefaultNode{
83: 		HostRequirements: clabtypes.NewHostRequirements(),
84: 		OverwriteNode:    n,
85: 		LicensePolicy:    clabtypes.LicensePolicyNone,
86: 		SSHConfig:        clabtypes.NewSSHConfig(),
87: 	}
88: 
89: 	dn.InterfaceMappedPrefix = "eth"
90: 	dn.InterfaceOffset = 0
91: 	dn.FirstDataIfIndex = 0
92: 
93: 	return dn
94: }
95: 
96: func (d *DefaultNode) RequireMgmtReachable() error {
97: 	m := d.Runtime.Mgmt()
98: 	if m == nil || m.Driver != clabtypes.MgmtDriverMacvlan || m.MacvlanAuxEnabled() {
99: 		return nil
100: 	}
101: 
102: 	return fmt.Errorf(
103: 		"node %q post-deploy needs host-to-node management access, enable mgmt macvlan aux interface",
104: 		d.Cfg.ShortName,
105: 	)
106: }
107: 
108: func (d *DefaultNode) WithMgmtNet(mgmt *clabtypes.MgmtNet)                   { d.Mgmt = mgmt }
109: func (d *DefaultNode) WithRuntime(r clabruntime.ContainerRuntime)            { d.Runtime = r }
110: func (d *DefaultNode) GetRuntime() clabruntime.ContainerRuntime              { return d.Runtime }
111: func (d *DefaultNode) Config() *clabtypes.NodeConfig                         { return d.Cfg }
112: func (*DefaultNode) PostDeploy(_ context.Context, _ *PostDeployParams) error { return nil }
113: func (*DefaultNode) PreStop(context.Context) error                           { return nil }
114: func (*DefaultNode) PreDestroy(context.Context) error                        { return nil }
115: 
116: // PreDeploy is a common method for all nodes that is called before the node is deployed.
117: func (d *DefaultNode) PreDeploy(_ context.Context, params *PreDeployParams) error {
118: 	if d.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased || d.Cfg.SkipUniquenessCheck {
119: 		return nil
120: 	}
121: 	_, err := d.LoadOrGenerateCertificate(params.Cert, params.TopologyName)
122: 	if err != nil {
123: 		return fmt.Errorf("loading or generating certificate for node %q: %w", d.Cfg.ShortName, err)
124: 	}
125: 	return nil
126: }
127: 
128: func (d *DefaultNode) SaveConfig(_ context.Context) (*SaveConfigResult, error) {
129: 	// nodes should have the save method defined on their respective structs.
130: 	// By default SaveConfig is a noop.
131: 	log.Debugf("Save operation is currently not supported for %q node kind", d.Cfg.Kind)
132: 	return nil, nil
133: }
134: 
135: // CheckDeploymentConditions wraps individual functions that check if a node
136: // satisfies deployment requirements.
137: func (d *DefaultNode) CheckDeploymentConditions(ctx context.Context) error {
138: 	err := d.OverwriteNode.VerifyHostRequirements()
139: 	if err != nil {
140: 		return err
141: 	}
142: 
143: 	err = d.OverwriteNode.VerifyStartupConfig(d.Cfg.LabDir)
144: 	if err != nil {
145: 		return err
146: 	}
147: 
148: 	err = d.OverwriteNode.CheckInterfaceName()
149: 	if err != nil {
150: 		return err
151: 	}
152: 
153: 	err = d.OverwriteNode.VerifyLicenseFileExists(ctx)
154: 	if err != nil {
155: 		return err
156: 	}
157: 
158: 	err = d.OverwriteNode.VerifyContainerName()
159: 	if err != nil {
160: 		return err
161: 	}
162: 
163: 	return nil
164: }
165: 
166: func (d *DefaultNode) PullImage(ctx context.Context) error {
167: 	for imageKey, imageName := range d.OverwriteNode.GetImages(ctx) {
168: 		if imageName == "" {
169: 			return fmt.Errorf(
170: 				"missing required %q attribute for node %q",
171: 				imageKey,
172: 				d.Cfg.ShortName,
173: 			)
174: 		}
175: 		err := d.Runtime.PullImage(ctx, imageName, d.Config().ImagePullPolicy)
176: 		if err != nil {
177: 			return err
178: 		}
179: 	}
180: 	return nil
181: }
182: 
183: func (d *DefaultNode) VerifyHostRequirements() error {
184: 	return d.HostRequirements.Verify(d.Cfg.Kind, d.Cfg.ShortName)
185: }
186: 
187: func (d *DefaultNode) Deploy(ctx context.Context, _ *DeployParams) error {
188: 	// Set the "CLAB_INTFS" variable to the number of interfaces (endpoints) a node has.
189: 	// This env var does not count in the eth0 interface that is automatically created by the
190: 	// container runtime. This env var is used by some containers (e.g. vrnetlab systems) to
191: 	// postpone the startup until all interfaces
192: 	// have been added to the container namespace.
193: 	d.Config().Env[clabconstants.ClabEnvIntfs] = strconv.Itoa(len(d.GetEndpoints()))
194: 
195: 	// create the container
196: 	cID, err := d.Runtime.CreateContainer(ctx, d.Cfg)
197: 	if err != nil {
198: 		return err
199: 	}
200: 
201: 	// start the container
202: 	_, err = d.Runtime.StartContainer(ctx, cID, d)
203: 	if err != nil {
204: 		return err
205: 	}
206: 
207: 	// Update the nodes state
208: 	d.SetState(clabnodesstate.Deployed)
209: 
210: 	return nil
211: }
212: 
213: // GetNSPath retrieves the nodes nspath.
214: func (d *DefaultNode) GetNSPath(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {
215: 	var err error
216: 	nsp := ""
217: 
218: 	if d.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased {
219: 		netns, err := ns.GetCurrentNS()
220: 		if err != nil {
221: 			return "", err
222: 		}
223: 		nsp = netns.Path()
224: 	}
225: 	if nsp == "" {
226: 		nsp, err = d.Runtime.GetNSPath(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())
227: 		if err != nil {
228: 			log.Errorf("Unable to determine NetNS Path for node %s: %v", d.Cfg.ShortName, err)
229: 			return "", err
230: 		}
231: 	}
232: 
233: 	return nsp, err
234: }
235: 
236: // ShouldSkipLifecycle reports whether lifecycle operations should no-op for this node.
237: func (d *DefaultNode) ShouldSkipLifecycle() bool {
238: 	return d.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased || d.Cfg.AutoRemove
239: }
240: 
241: // LinkApplyMode returns the conservative default for node kinds that do not
242: // explicitly opt into live hotplug or restart handling.
243: func (d *DefaultNode) LinkApplyMode(ctx context.Context) LinkApplyMode {
244: 	if d.Cfg != nil && (d.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased || d.Cfg.SkipUniquenessCheck) {
245: 		return LinkApplyModeLive
246: 	}
247: 	return d.ImageLinkApplyMode(ctx, LinkApplyModeRecreate)
248: }
249: 
250: // ImageLinkApplyMode allows node kinds to keep their normal fallback behavior
251: // while opting into live link apply when their image declares support for it.
252: func (d *DefaultNode) ImageLinkApplyMode(
253: 	ctx context.Context,
254: 	fallback LinkApplyMode,
255: ) LinkApplyMode {
256: 	if d.imageHasBoxenVendorLabel(ctx) {
257: 		return LinkApplyModeLive
258: 	}
259: 
260: 	return fallback
261: }
262: 
263: func (d *DefaultNode) imageHasBoxenVendorLabel(ctx context.Context) bool {
264: 	if d == nil || d.Cfg == nil || d.Cfg.Image == "" {
265: 		return false
266: 	}
267: 	if d.Runtime == nil {
268: 		log.Debug("Skipping image label check for apply link hotplug support",
269: 			"image", d.Cfg.Image,
270: 			"reason", "node runtime is not initialized",
271: 		)
272: 		return false
273: 	}
274: 
275: 	inspect, err := d.Runtime.InspectImage(ctx, d.Cfg.Image)
276: 	if err != nil {
277: 		log.Debug("Failed to inspect image for apply link hotplug support",
278: 			"image", d.Cfg.Image,
279: 			"error", err,
280: 		)
281: 		return false
282: 	}
283: 	if inspect == nil || inspect.Config.Labels == nil {
284: 		return false
285: 	}
286: 
287: 	return inspect.Config.Labels[boxenImageVendorLabel] == boxenImageVendorValue
288: }
289: 
290: func (d *DefaultNode) ComputeDiff(oldCfg, newCfg *clabtypes.NodeConfig) *clabtypes.TopologyDiff {
291: 	diff := &clabtypes.TopologyDiff{}
292: 
293: 	if oldCfg == nil || newCfg == nil {
294: 		return diff
295: 	}
296: 
297: 	if oldCfg.NodeType != newCfg.NodeType {
298: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Type")
299: 	}
300: 	if oldCfg.Kind != newCfg.Kind {
301: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Kind")
302: 	}
303: 	if oldCfg.Image != newCfg.Image {
304: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Image")
305: 	}
306: 	if oldCfg.GetHostname() != newCfg.GetHostname() {
307: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Hostname")
308: 	}
309: 	if oldCfg.Entrypoint != newCfg.Entrypoint {
310: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Entrypoint")
311: 	}
312: 	if oldCfg.Cmd != newCfg.Cmd {
313: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Cmd")
314: 	}
315: 	if !clabutils.SlicesEqualOrBothEmpty(oldCfg.Exec, newCfg.Exec) {
316: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Exec")
317: 	}
318: 	if !clabutils.MapsEqualOrBothEmpty(oldCfg.Env, newCfg.Env) {
319: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Env")
320: 	}
321: 	if !clabutils.SlicesEqualOrBothEmpty(oldCfg.Binds, newCfg.Binds) {
322: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Binds")
323: 	}
324: 	if !clabutils.SlicesEqualOrBothEmpty(oldCfg.Devices, newCfg.Devices) {
325: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Devices")
326: 	}
327: 	if !clabutils.SlicesEqualOrBothEmpty(oldCfg.CapAdd, newCfg.CapAdd) {
328: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "CapAdd")
329: 	}
330: 	if oldCfg.Privileged != newCfg.Privileged {
331: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Privileged")
332: 	}
333: 	if oldCfg.CgroupnsMode != newCfg.CgroupnsMode {
334: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "CgroupnsMode")
335: 	}
336: 	if oldCfg.CgroupParent != newCfg.CgroupParent {
337: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "CgroupParent")
338: 	}
339: 	if oldCfg.PidMode != newCfg.PidMode {
340: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "PidMode")
341: 	}
342: 	if !clabutils.MapsEqualOrBothEmpty(oldCfg.Tmpfs, newCfg.Tmpfs) {
343: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Tmpfs")
344: 	}
345: 	if !clabutils.SlicesEqualOrBothEmpty(oldCfg.SecurityOpts, newCfg.SecurityOpts) {
346: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "SecurityOpts")
347: 	}
348: 	if oldCfg.ShmSize != newCfg.ShmSize {
349: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "ShmSize")
350: 	}
351: 	if !clabutils.PortSetsEqual(oldCfg.PortSet, newCfg.PortSet) {
352: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Ports")
353: 	}
354: 	if oldCfg.User != newCfg.User {
355: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "User")
356: 	}
357: 	if oldCfg.NetworkMode != newCfg.NetworkMode {
358: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "NetworkMode")
359: 	}
360: 	if oldCfg.MgmtNet != newCfg.MgmtNet {
361: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "MgmtNet")
362: 	}
363: 	if oldCfg.Runtime != newCfg.Runtime {
364: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Runtime")
365: 	}
366: 	if oldCfg.CPU != newCfg.CPU {
367: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "CPU")
368: 	}
369: 	if oldCfg.CPUSet != newCfg.CPUSet {
370: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "CPUSet")
371: 	}
372: 	if oldCfg.Memory != newCfg.Memory {
373: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "Memory")
374: 	}
375: 	if oldCfg.License != newCfg.License {
376: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "License")
377: 	}
378: 	_, oldInvalid := oldCfg.KindSpecificConfig.(InvalidKindSpecificConfig)
379: 	_, newInvalid := newCfg.KindSpecificConfig.(InvalidKindSpecificConfig)
380: 
381: 	if oldInvalid || newInvalid ||
382: 		!reflect.DeepEqual(oldCfg.KindSpecificConfig, newCfg.KindSpecificConfig) {
383: 		diff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, "KindSpecificConfig")
384: 	}
385: 
386: 	return diff
387: }
388: 
389: func (d *DefaultNode) ComputeReconcilePlan(diff *clabtypes.TopologyDiff) *ReconcileResult {
390: 	result := &ReconcileResult{Action: clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionNone}
391: 
392: 	if diff == nil || !diff.HasDiff() {
393: 		return result
394: 	}
395: 
396: 	action := diff.DefaultAction()
397: 	result.Action = action
398: 
399: 	switch action {
400: 	case clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRestart:
401: 		result.Restarted = []string{d.Cfg.LongName}
402: 	case clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRecreate:
403: 		result.Recreated = []string{d.Cfg.LongName}
404: 	}
405: 
406: 	return result
407: }
408: 
409: func (d *DefaultNode) GetReconcilePlan(
410: 	_ context.Context,
411: 	diff *clabtypes.TopologyDiff,
412: ) (*ReconcileResult, error) {
413: 	return d.ComputeReconcilePlan(diff), nil
414: }
415: 
416: // Reconcile applies the diff and executes the appropriate action (restart/recreate).
417: func (d *DefaultNode) Reconcile(
418: 	ctx context.Context,
419: 	diff *clabtypes.TopologyDiff,
420: ) (*ReconcileResult, error) {
421: 	result := d.ComputeReconcilePlan(diff)
422: 
423: 	if result.Action == clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionNone {
424: 		return result, nil
425: 	}
426: 
427: 	log.Info(
428: 		"Applying node changes",
429: 		"node",
430: 		d.Cfg.ShortName,
431: 		"action",
432: 		result.Action,
433: 		"fields",
434: 		diff.Fields,
435: 	)
436: 
437: 	switch result.Action {
438: 	case clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRestart:
439: 		if err := d.Stop(ctx); err != nil {
440: 			return result, fmt.Errorf("stop failed: %w", err)
441: 		}
442: 		if err := d.Start(ctx); err != nil {
443: 			return result, fmt.Errorf("start failed: %w", err)
444: 		}
445: 
446: 	case clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRecreate:
447: 		if err := d.Delete(ctx); err != nil {
448: 			return result, fmt.Errorf("delete failed: %w", err)
449: 		}
450: 	}
451: 
452: 	return result, nil
453: }
454: 
455: func (d *DefaultNode) parkingNetNSName() string {
456: 	return clabutils.ParkingNetnsName(d.Cfg.LongName)
457: }

459: func (d *DefaultNode) parkingNetNSPath() (string, error) {
460: 	return clabutils.GetNamedNetNS(d.parkingNetNSName())
461: }

463: // cleanupParkingNetNS removes the node-owned parking namespace if it exists.
464: func (d *DefaultNode) cleanupParkingNetNS() error {
465: 	if _, err := d.parkingNetNSPath(); err != nil {
466: 		return nil
467: 	}
468: 
469: 	return netns.DeleteNamed(d.parkingNetNSName())
470: }

472: func (d *DefaultNode) Delete(ctx context.Context) error {
473: 	for _, e := range d.Endpoints {
474: 		err := e.GetLink().Remove(ctx)
475: 		if err != nil {
476: 			return err
477: 		}
478: 	}
479: 
480: 	if d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx) == clabruntime.NotFound {
481: 		return nil
482: 	}
483: 
484: 	return d.Runtime.DeleteContainer(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())
485: }

487: func (d *DefaultNode) GetImages(_ context.Context) map[string]string {
488: 	return map[string]string{
489: 		ImageKey: d.Cfg.Image,
490: 	}
491: }

493: func (d *DefaultNode) GetContainers(ctx context.Context) ([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, error) {
494: 	cnts, err := d.Runtime.ListContainers(ctx, []*clabtypes.GenericFilter{
495: 		{
496: 			FilterType: "name",
497: 			Match:      d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(),
498: 		},
499: 	})
500: 	if err != nil {
501: 		return nil, err
502: 	}
503: 	// check that we retrieved some container information
504: 	// otherwise throw ErrContainersNotFound error
505: 	if len(cnts) == 0 {
506: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
507: 			"Node: %s. %w",
508: 			d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(),
509: 			ErrContainersNotFound,
510: 		)
511: 	}
512: 
513: 	return cnts, err
514: }
515: 
516: func (d *DefaultNode) RunExecFromConfig(ctx context.Context, ec *clabexec.ExecCollection) error {
517: 	for _, e := range d.Config().Exec {
518: 		exec, err := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(e)
519: 		if err != nil {
520: 			log.Warnf("Failed to parse the command string: %s, %v", e, err)
521: 		}
522: 
523: 		res, err := d.OverwriteNode.RunExec(ctx, exec)
524: 		if err != nil {
525: 			// kinds which do not support exec functionality are skipped
526: 			continue
527: 		}
528: 
529: 		ec.Add(d.GetShortName(), res)
530: 	}
531: 	return nil
532: }
533: 
534: func (d *DefaultNode) UpdateConfigWithRuntimeInfo(ctx context.Context) error {
535: 	cnts, err := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainers(ctx)
536: 	if err != nil {
537: 		return err
538: 	}
539: 
540: 	if len(cnts) == 0 {
541: 		return fmt.Errorf("no container runtime information retrieved")
542: 	}
543: 
544: 	// network settings of a first container only
545: 	netSettings := cnts[0].NetworkSettings
546: 
547: 	d.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Address = netSettings.IPv4addr
548: 	d.Cfg.MgmtIPv4PrefixLength = netSettings.IPv4pLen
549: 	d.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Address = netSettings.IPv6addr
550: 	d.Cfg.MgmtIPv6PrefixLength = netSettings.IPv6pLen
551: 	d.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Gateway = netSettings.IPv4Gw
552: 	d.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Gateway = netSettings.IPv6Gw
553: 
554: 	d.Cfg.ContainerID = cnts[0].ID
555: 
556: 	d.Cfg.ResultingPortBindings = cnts[0].Ports
557: 
558: 	return nil
559: }

561: // DeleteNetnsSymlink deletes the symlink file created for the container netns.
562: func (d *DefaultNode) DeleteNetnsSymlink() error {
563: 	log.Debugf("Deleting %s network namespace", d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())
564: 	return clabutils.DeleteNetnsSymlink(d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())
565: }
566: 
567: func (d *DefaultNode) CheckInterfaceOverlap() error {
568: 	var seenEps []clablinks.Endpoint
569: 
570: 	for _, ep := range d.Endpoints {
571: 		ifName := ep.GetIfaceName()
572: 		for _, seenEp := range seenEps {
573: 			if ifName == seenEp.GetIfaceName() {
574: 				return fmt.Errorf("%q and %q have overlapping interface names", ep, seenEp)
575: 			}
576: 		}
577: 		seenEps = append(seenEps, ep)
578: 	}
579: 
580: 	return nil
581: }
582: 
583: // CheckInterfaceName checks if a name of the interface referenced in the topology file is in the
584: // expected range of name values.
585: // A no-op for the default node, specific nodes should implement this method.
586: func (d *DefaultNode) CheckInterfaceName() error {
587: 	return d.CheckInterfaceOverlap()
588: }
589: 
590: // CalculateInterfaceIndex parses the supplied interface name with the InterfaceRegexp.
591: // Using the the port offset, it calculates the mapped interface index based on the InterfaceOffset
592: // and the first data interface index.
593: func (d *DefaultNode) CalculateInterfaceIndex(ifName string) (int, error) {
594: 	captureGroups, err := clabutils.GetRegexpCaptureGroups(d.InterfaceRegexp, ifName)
595: 	if err != nil {
596: 		return 0, err
597: 	}
598: 
599: 	if parsedIndex, found := captureGroups["port"]; found {
600: 		parsedIndexInt, err := strconv.Atoi(parsedIndex)
601: 		if err != nil {
602: 			return 0, fmt.Errorf(
603: 				"%q parsed index %q could not be cast to an integer",
604: 				ifName,
605: 				parsedIndex,
606: 			)
607: 		}
608: 		calculatedIndex := parsedIndexInt - d.InterfaceOffset + d.FirstDataIfIndex
609: 		return calculatedIndex, nil
610: 	} else {
611: 		return 0, fmt.Errorf(
612: 			"%q does not have extracted interface index with regexp %q, 'port' capture group missing?",
613: 			ifName,
614: 			d.InterfaceRegexp,
615: 		)
616: 	}
617: }
618: 
619: // GetMappedInterfaceName returns with a mapped interface name based on the mapped interface prefix
620: // and calculated mapped interface index.
621: func (d *DefaultNode) GetMappedInterfaceName(ifName string) (string, error) {
622: 	ifIndex, err := d.OverwriteNode.CalculateInterfaceIndex(ifName)
623: 	if err != nil {
624: 		return "", err
625: 	}
626: 	if ifIndex < d.FirstDataIfIndex {
627: 		return "", fmt.Errorf(
628: 			"extracted interface index for %q is out of bounds: %d ! >= %d",
629: 			ifName,
630: 			ifIndex,
631: 			d.FirstDataIfIndex,
632: 		)
633: 	}
634: 	mappedIfName := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d", d.InterfaceMappedPrefix, ifIndex)
635: 
636: 	return mappedIfName, nil
637: }
638: 
639: // VerifyStartupConfig verifies that startup config files exists on disks.
640: func (d *DefaultNode) VerifyStartupConfig(topoDir string) error {
641: 	cfg := d.Config().StartupConfig
642: 	if cfg == "" {
643: 		return nil
644: 	}
645: 
646: 	rcfg := clabutils.ResolvePath(cfg, topoDir)
647: 	if !clabutils.FileExists(rcfg) {
648: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q startup-config file not found by the path %s",
649: 			d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), rcfg)
650: 	}
651: 
652: 	return nil
653: }
654: 
655: // GenerateConfig generates configuration for the nodes
656: // out of the template `t` based on the node configuration and saves the result to dst.
657: // If the config file is already present in the node dir we do not regenerate the config unless
658: // EnforceStartupConfig is explicitly set to true and startup-config points to a file this will
659: // persist the changes that users make to a running config when booted from some startup config.
660: func (d *DefaultNode) GenerateConfig(dst, t string) error {
661: 	// Check for incompatible options
662: 	if d.Cfg.EnforceStartupConfig && d.Cfg.SuppressStartupConfig {
663: 		return ErrIncompatibleOptions
664: 	}
665: 	if d.Cfg.EnforceStartupConfig && d.Cfg.StartupConfig == "" {
666: 		return ErrNoStartupConfig
667: 	}
668: 
669: 	if d.Cfg.SuppressStartupConfig {
670: 		log.Info("Startup config generation suppressed", "node", d.Cfg.ShortName)
671: 		return nil
672: 	}
673: 
674: 	if !d.Cfg.EnforceStartupConfig && clabutils.FileExists(dst) {
675: 		log.Debug("Existing config found", "node", d.Cfg.ShortName, "path", dst)
676: 		return nil
677: 	} else {
678: 		log.Debug("Generating config", "node", d.Cfg.ShortName, "file", d.Cfg.StartupConfig)
679: 
680: 		cfgBuf, err := clabutils.SubstituteEnvsAndTemplate(strings.NewReader(t), d.Cfg)
681: 		if err != nil {
682: 			return err
683: 		}
684: 		log.Debug("Generated config", "node", d.Cfg.ShortName, "content", cfgBuf.String())
685: 
686: 		f, err := os.Create(dst)
687: 		if err != nil {
688: 			return err
689: 		}
690: 		defer f.Close()
691: 
692: 		_, err = f.Write(cfgBuf.Bytes())
693: 		if err != nil {
694: 			return err
695: 		}
696: 	}
697: 
698: 	return nil
699: }
700: 
701: // NodeOverwrites is an interface that every node implements.
702: // It is used to enable DefaultNode to get access to the particular node structs
703: // and is provided as an argument of the NewDefaultNode function.
704: // The methods defined for this interfaces are the methods that particular node has a custom
705: // implementation of.
706: type NodeOverwrites interface {
707: 	VerifyStartupConfig(topoDir string) error
708: 	CheckInterfaceName() error
709: 	CalculateInterfaceIndex(ifName string) (int, error)
710: 	GetMappedInterfaceName(ifName string) (string, error)
711: 	VerifyHostRequirements() error
712: 	PullImage(ctx context.Context) error
713: 	GetImages(ctx context.Context) map[string]string
714: 	GetContainers(ctx context.Context) ([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, error)
715: 	GetContainerName() string
716: 	GetContainerStatus(ctx context.Context) clabruntime.ContainerStatus
717: 	VerifyContainerName() error
718: 	VerifyLicenseFileExists(context.Context) error
719: 	PreStop(context.Context) error
720: 	RunExec(context.Context, *clabexec.ExecCmd) (*clabexec.ExecResult, error)
721: 	GetNSPath(ctx context.Context) (string, error)
722: }
723: 
724: // LoadStartupConfigFileVr templates a startup-config using the file specified for VM-based nodes in
725: // the topo
726: // and puts the resulting config file by the LabDir/configDirName/startupCfgFName path.
727: func LoadStartupConfigFileVr(node Node, configDirName, startupCfgFName string) error {
728: 	nodeCfg := node.Config()
729: 	// create config directory that will be bind mounted to vrnetlab container at / path
730: 	clabutils.CreateDirectory(path.Join(nodeCfg.LabDir, configDirName),
731: 		clabconstants.PermissionsOpen)
732: 
733: 	if nodeCfg.StartupConfig != "" {
734: 		// dstCfg is a path to a file on the clab host that will have rendered configuration
735: 		dstCfg := filepath.Join(nodeCfg.LabDir, configDirName, startupCfgFName)
736: 
737: 		c, err := os.ReadFile(nodeCfg.StartupConfig)
738: 		if err != nil {
739: 			return err
740: 		}
741: 
742: 		cfgTemplate := string(c)
743: 
744: 		err = node.GenerateConfig(dstCfg, cfgTemplate)
745: 		if err != nil {
746: 			log.Errorf("node=%s, failed to generate config: %v", nodeCfg.ShortName, err)
747: 		}
748: 	}
749: 	return nil
750: }
751: 
752: // GetContainerName returns the name used by the runtime to identify the container
753: // e.g. ext-container nodes use the name as defined in the topo file, while most other containers
754: // use long (prefixed) name.
755: func (d *DefaultNode) GetContainerName() string {
756: 	return d.Cfg.LongName
757: }
758: 
759: func (d *DefaultNode) VerifyContainerName() error {
760: 	containerName := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName()
761: 	if !containerNamePatternRe.MatchString(containerName) {
762: 		return fmt.Errorf("Node name contains invalid characters: %s", containerName)
763: 	}
764: 
765: 	if len(containerName) > maxNameLength {
766: 		log.Warn(
767: 			"Node name will not resolve via DNS",
768: 			"name",
769: 			containerName,
770: 			"reason",
771: 			fmt.Sprintf("name exceeds %d characters", maxNameLength),
772: 		)
773: 	}
774: 
775: 	if strings.ContainsAny(containerName, dnsIncompatibleChars) {
776: 		log.Warn(
777: 			"Node name will not resolve via DNS",
778: 			"name",
779: 			containerName,
780: 			"reason",
781: 			"name contains invalid characters such as '.' and/or '_'",
782: 		)
783: 	}
784: 
785: 	return nil
786: }
787: 
788: // RunExec executes a single command for a node.
789: func (d *DefaultNode) RunExec(
790: 	ctx context.Context,
791: 	execCmd *clabexec.ExecCmd,
792: ) (*clabexec.ExecResult, error) {
793: 	execResult, err := d.GetRuntime().Exec(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), execCmd)
794: 	if err != nil {
795: 		log.Errorf("%s: failed to execute cmd: %q with error %v",
796: 			d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), execCmd.GetCmdString(), err)
797: 		return nil, err
798: 	}
799: 	return execResult, nil
800: }
801: 
802: // RunExecNotWait executes a command for a node, and doesn't block waiting for the output.
803: // Should be overridden if the nodes implementation differs.
804: func (d *DefaultNode) RunExecNotWait(ctx context.Context, execCmd *clabexec.ExecCmd) error {
805: 	err := d.GetRuntime().ExecNotWait(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), execCmd)
806: 	if err != nil {
807: 		log.Errorf("%s: failed to execute cmd: %q with error %v",
808: 			d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), execCmd.GetCmdString(), err)
809: 		return err
810: 	}
811: 	return nil
812: }
813: 
814: // VerifyLicenseFileExists checks if a license file with a provided path exists.
815: func (d *DefaultNode) VerifyLicenseFileExists(_ context.Context) error {
816: 	if d.Config().License == "" {
817: 		switch d.LicensePolicy {
818: 		// if a license is required by the kind but not provided
819: 		case clabtypes.LicensePolicyRequired:
820: 			return fmt.Errorf(
821: 				"node %s of kind %s requires a license. Provide one via 'license' knob in the topology file",
822: 				d.Config().ShortName,
823: 				d.Cfg.Kind,
824: 			)
825: 		case clabtypes.LicensePolicyWarn:
826: 			// just warn when no license is provided
827: 			log.Warnf(
828: 				"node %s of kind %s requires a license. Make sure to provide it in some way (e.g. license knob or baked into image)",
829: 				d.Config().ShortName,
830: 				d.Cfg.Kind,
831: 			)
832: 			return nil
833: 		case clabtypes.LicensePolicyNone:
834: 			// license is not required
835: 			return nil
836: 		default:
837: 			return fmt.Errorf("unknown license policy value %s for node %s kind %s",
838: 				d.LicensePolicy, d.Config().ShortName, d.Cfg.Kind)
839: 		}
840: 	}
841: 	// if license is provided check path exists
842: 	rlic := clabutils.ResolvePath(d.Config().License, d.Cfg.LabDir)
843: 	if !clabutils.FileExists(rlic) {
844: 		return fmt.Errorf(
845: 			"license file for node %q is not found by the path %s",
846: 			d.Config().ShortName,
847: 			rlic,
848: 		)
849: 	}
850: 
851: 	return nil
852: }
853: 
854: // LoadOrGenerateCertificate loads a certificate using a certificate storage provider
855: // provided in certInfra or generates a new one if it does not exist.
856: func (d *DefaultNode) LoadOrGenerateCertificate(
857: 	certInfra *clabcert.Cert,
858: 	topoName string,
859: ) (nodeCert *clabcert.Certificate, err error) {
860: 	// early return if certificate generation is not required
861: 	if d.Cfg.Certificate == nil || !*d.Cfg.Certificate.Issue {
862: 		return nil, nil
863: 	}
864: 
865: 	nodeConfig := d.Cfg
866: 
867: 	// try loading existing certificates from disk and generate new ones if they do not exist
868: 	nodeCert, err = certInfra.LoadNodeCert(nodeConfig.ShortName)
869: 	if err != nil {
870: 		log.Debugf("creating node certificate for %s", nodeConfig.ShortName)
871: 
872: 		hosts := []string{
873: 			nodeConfig.ShortName,
874: 			nodeConfig.LongName,
875: 			nodeConfig.ShortName + "." + topoName + ".io",
876: 		}
877: 		// add the SANs provided via config
878: 		hosts = append(hosts, nodeConfig.Certificate.SANs...)
879: 
880: 		// add mgmt IPs as SANs to CSR
881: 		for _, ip := range []string{d.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Address, d.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Address} {
882: 			if ip != "" {
883: 				hosts = append(hosts, ip)
884: 			}
885: 		}
886: 
887: 		certInput := &clabcert.NodeCSRInput{
888: 			CommonName:   nodeConfig.ShortName + "." + topoName + ".io",
889: 			Hosts:        hosts,
890: 			Organization: "containerlab",
891: 			Country:      "US",
892: 			KeySize:      d.Cfg.Certificate.KeySize,
893: 			Expiry:       d.Cfg.Certificate.ValidityDuration,
894: 		}
895: 		// Generate the cert for the node
896: 		nodeCert, err = certInfra.GenerateAndSignNodeCert(certInput)
897: 		if err != nil {
898: 			return nil, err
899: 		}
900: 
901: 		// persist the cert via certStorage
902: 		err = certInfra.StoreNodeCert(nodeConfig.ShortName, nodeCert)
903: 		if err != nil {
904: 			return nil, err
905: 		}
906: 	}
907: 
908: 	return nodeCert, nil
909: }
910: 
911: func (d *DefaultNode) GetHostsEntries(ctx context.Context) (clabtypes.HostEntries, error) {
912: 	containers, err := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainers(ctx)
913: 	if err != nil {
914: 		return nil, err
915: 	}
916: 
917: 	result := clabtypes.HostEntries{}
918: 
919: 	for idx := range containers {
920: 		if len(containers[idx].Names) == 0 {
921: 			continue
922: 		}
923: 		if containers[idx].NetworkSettings.IPv4addr != "" {
924: 			result = append(result, clabtypes.NewHostEntry(
925: 				containers[idx].NetworkSettings.IPv4addr,
926: 				containers[idx].Names[0],
927: 				clabtypes.IpVersionV4,
928: 			).SetDescription(fmt.Sprintf("Kind: %s", d.Cfg.Kind)).SetContainerID(containers[idx].ID))
929: 		}
930: 		if containers[idx].NetworkSettings.IPv6addr != "" {
931: 			result = append(result, clabtypes.NewHostEntry(
932: 				containers[idx].NetworkSettings.IPv6addr,
933: 				containers[idx].Names[0],
934: 				clabtypes.IpVersionV6,
935: 			).SetDescription(fmt.Sprintf("Kind: %s", d.Cfg.Kind)).SetContainerID(containers[idx].ID))
936: 		}
937: 	}
938: 	return result, nil
939: }
940: 
941: func (d *DefaultNode) AddLinkToContainer(
942: 	ctx context.Context,
943: 	link netlink.Link,
944: 	f func(ns.NetNS) error,
945: ) error {
946: 	if !d.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased {
947: 		st := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx)
948: 		if !clabruntime.ContainerHasJoinableNetns(st) {
949: 			if st == clabruntime.Stopped {
950: 				containerName := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName()
951: 				d.GetRuntime().LogNonRunningContainerOutput(ctx, containerName)
952: 			}
953: 			return fmt.Errorf(
954: 				"node %q: cannot attach link, container network namespace is not available (status=%s)",
955: 				d.Cfg.ShortName,
956: 				st,
957: 			)
958: 		}
959: 	}
960: 	// retrieve nodes nspath
961: 	nsp, err := d.OverwriteNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
962: 	if err != nil {
963: 		return err
964: 	}
965: 	// retrieve the namespace handle
966: 	netns, err := ns.GetNS(nsp)
967: 	if err != nil {
968: 		return err
969: 	}
970: 	// move veth endpoint to namespace
971: 	if err := netlink.LinkSetNsFd(link, int(netns.Fd())); err != nil {
972: 		return err
973: 	}
974: 	// execute the given function
975: 	err = netns.Do(f)
976: 	if err != nil {
977: 		return err
978: 	}
979: 	return nil
980: }
981: 
982: // ExecFunction executes the given function in the nodes network namespace.
983: func (d *DefaultNode) ExecFunction(ctx context.Context, f func(ns.NetNS) error) error {
984: 	if !d.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased {
985: 		st := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx)
986: 		if !clabruntime.ContainerHasJoinableNetns(st) {
987: 			if st == clabruntime.Stopped {
988: 				containerName := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName()
989: 				d.GetRuntime().LogNonRunningContainerOutput(ctx, containerName)
990: 			}
991: 			return fmt.Errorf(
992: 				"node %q: cannot exec a command, container network namespace is not available (status=%s)",
993: 				d.Cfg.ShortName,
994: 				st,
995: 			)
996: 		}
997: 	}
998: 	// retrieve nodes nspath
999: 	nspath, err := d.OverwriteNode.GetNSPath(ctx)
1000: 	if err != nil {
1001: 		return err
1002: 	}
1003: 
1004: 	if d.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased {
1005: 		nshandle, err := ns.GetCurrentNS()
1006: 		if err != nil {
1007: 			return err
1008: 		}
1009: 		nspath = nshandle.Path()
1010: 	}
1011: 
1012: 	if nspath == "" {
1013: 		return fmt.Errorf("nspath is not set for node %q", d.GetShortName())
1014: 	}
1015: 
1016: 	// retrieve the namespace handle
1017: 	netns, err := ns.GetNS(nspath)
1018: 	if err != nil {
1019: 		return err
1020: 	}
1021: 	// execute the given function
1022: 	return netns.Do(f)
1023: }
1024: 
1025: // AddEndpoint maps the endpoint name to before adding it to the node endpoints if it matches the
1026: // interface alias regexp. Returns an error if the mapping goes wrong.
1027: func (d *DefaultNode) AddEndpoint(e clablinks.Endpoint) error {
1028: 	endpointName := e.GetIfaceName()
1029: 	if d.InterfaceRegexp != nil && d.InterfaceRegexp.MatchString(endpointName) {
1030: 		mappedName, err := d.OverwriteNode.GetMappedInterfaceName(endpointName)
1031: 		if err != nil {
1032: 			return fmt.Errorf(
1033: 				"%q interface name %q could not be mapped: %w",
1034: 				d.Cfg.ShortName,
1035: 				e.GetIfaceName(),
1036: 				err,
1037: 			)
1038: 		}
1039: 		log.Debugf(
1040: 			"Interface Mapping: Mapping interface %q (ifAlias) to %q (ifName)",
1041: 			endpointName,
1042: 			mappedName,
1043: 		)
1044: 		e.SetIfaceName(mappedName)
1045: 		e.SetIfaceAlias(endpointName)
1046: 	}
1047: 
1048: 	if e.GetNode() == nil {
1049: 		e.SetNode(d)
1050: 	}
1051: 
1052: 	d.Endpoints = append(d.Endpoints, e)
1053: 	return nil
1054: }
1055: 
1056: func (d *DefaultNode) AdoptEndpoint(e clablinks.Endpoint) error {
1057: 	if e == nil {
1058: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q cannot adopt a nil endpoint", d.Cfg.ShortName)
1059: 	}
1060: 
1061: 	owner := e.GetNode()
1062: 	if owner == nil {
1063: 		return fmt.Errorf(
1064: 			"node %q cannot adopt endpoint %q without an owner",
1065: 			d.Cfg.ShortName,
1066: 			e.GetIfaceName(),
1067: 		)
1068: 	}
1069: 
1070: 	if owner.GetShortName() != d.Cfg.ShortName {
1071: 		return fmt.Errorf(
1072: 			"node %q cannot adopt endpoint %q owned by %q",
1073: 			d.Cfg.ShortName,
1074: 			e.GetIfaceName(),
1075: 			owner.GetShortName(),
1076: 		)
1077: 	}
1078: 
1079: 	for _, owned := range d.Endpoints {
1080: 		if owned == e {
1081: 			return nil
1082: 		}
1083: 		if owned.GetIfaceName() == e.GetIfaceName() {
1084: 			return fmt.Errorf(
1085: 				"node %q already tracks interface %q",
1086: 				d.Cfg.ShortName,
1087: 				e.GetIfaceName(),
1088: 			)
1089: 		}
1090: 	}
1091: 
1092: 	d.Endpoints = append(d.Endpoints, e)
1093: 
1094: 	return nil
1095: }
1096: 
1097: func (d *DefaultNode) ReleaseEndpoint(e clablinks.Endpoint) error {
1098: 	for i, ep := range d.Endpoints {
1099: 		if ep != e {
1100: 			continue
1101: 		}
1102: 
1103: 		d.Endpoints = append(d.Endpoints[:i], d.Endpoints[i+1:]...)
1104: 		return nil
1105: 	}
1106: 
1107: 	return fmt.Errorf("node %q does not own endpoint %q", d.Cfg.ShortName, e.GetIfaceName())
1108: }
1109: 
1110: func (d *DefaultNode) GetEndpoints() []clablinks.Endpoint {
1111: 	return d.Endpoints
1112: }

1114: // GetLinkEndpointType returns a veth link endpoint type for default nodes.
1115: // The LinkEndpointTypeVeth indicates a veth endpoint which doesn't require special handling.
1116: func (*DefaultNode) GetLinkEndpointType() clablinks.LinkEndpointType {
1117: 	return clablinks.LinkEndpointTypeVeth
1118: }

1120: func (d *DefaultNode) GetShortName() string {
1121: 	return d.Cfg.ShortName
1122: }
1123: 
1124: // DeployEndpoints deploys endpoints associated with the node.
1125: // The deployment of endpoints is done by deploying a link with the endpoint triggering it.
1126: func (d *DefaultNode) DeployEndpoints(ctx context.Context) error {
1127: 	for _, ep := range d.Endpoints {
1128: 		if ep.IsRuntimeDiscovered() {
1129: 			continue
1130: 		}
1131: 
1132: 		if err := ep.Deploy(ctx); err != nil {
1133: 			return err
1134: 		}
1135: 	}
1136: 
1137: 	return nil
1138: }
1139: 
1140: func (*DefaultNode) PostDeployEndpoints(context.Context) error {
1141: 	return nil
1142: }

1144: func (d *DefaultNode) GetState() clabnodesstate.NodeState {

1146: 	defer d.statemutex.RUnlock()
1147: 	return d.state
1148: }
1149:

1151: 	d.statemutex.Lock()
1152: 	defer d.statemutex.Unlock()
1153: 	d.state = s
1154: }

1156: func (d *DefaultNode) GetSSHConfig() *clabtypes.SSHConfig {
1157: 	return d.SSHConfig
1158: }

1160: func (d *DefaultNode) GetContainerStatus(ctx context.Context) clabruntime.ContainerStatus {
1161: 	return d.Runtime.GetContainerStatus(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())
1162: }
1163: 
1164: func (d *DefaultNode) IsHealthy(ctx context.Context) (bool, error) {
1165: 	return d.Runtime.IsHealthy(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())
1166: }
1167: 
1168: // ParkEndpoints moves all tracked endpoints into the node-owned parking namespace.
1169: func (d *DefaultNode) ParkEndpoints(ctx context.Context) error {
1170: 	parkPath, err := clabutils.CreateOrGetNamedNetNS(d.parkingNetNSName())
1171: 	if err != nil {
1172: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create parking netns for node %q: %w", d.Cfg.ShortName, err)
1173: 	}
1174: 	// Reuse the parking node retained by a failed restore so it keeps the parked endpoints.
1175: 	parkingNode := d.parkingNode
1176: 	reused := parkingNode != nil
1177: 	if !reused {
1178: 		parkingNode = clablinks.NewParkingNode(d.Cfg.LongName, parkPath)
1179: 	}
1180: 
1181: 	if err := parkingNode.CaptureFrom(ctx, d); err != nil {
1182: 		if !reused {
1183: 			_ = d.cleanupParkingNetNS()
1184: 		}
1185: 		return err
1186: 	}
1187: 
1188: 	if err := parkingNode.RepointSymlink(); err != nil {
1189: 		repointErr := fmt.Errorf("failed to repoint symlink for node %q: %w", d.Cfg.ShortName, err)
1190: 		if _, restoreErr := parkingNode.RestoreTo(ctx, d); restoreErr != nil {
1191: 			// Keep the parking netns and its endpoints so a later restore can recover them.
1192: 			d.parkingNode = parkingNode
1193: 			return errors.Join(repointErr, restoreErr)
1194: 		}
1195: 		d.parkingNode = nil
1196: 		if cleanupErr := d.cleanupParkingNetNS(); cleanupErr != nil {
1197: 			return errors.Join(repointErr, cleanupErr)
1198: 		}
1199: 		return repointErr
1200: 	}
1201: 	d.parkingNode = parkingNode
1202: 
1203: 	return nil
1204: }
1205: 
1206: // RestoreEndpoints tries to get the parking node to unpark its interfaces.
1207: func (d *DefaultNode) RestoreEndpoints(ctx context.Context) error {
1208: 	parkPath, err := d.parkingNetNSPath()
1209: 	if err != nil {
1210: 		// No parking netns means the node had no parked interfaces - e.g. it was
1211: 		// stopped outside of containerlab (so its interfaces were never parked) or
1212: 		// it has no dataplane links. Nothing to restore.
1213: 		d.parkingNode = nil
1214: 		log.Debugf("node %q has no parking netns, nothing to restore", d.Cfg.ShortName)
1215: 		return nil
1216: 	}
1217: 	parkingNode := d.parkingNode
1218: 	if parkingNode == nil {
1219: 		parkingNode = clablinks.NewParkingNode(d.Cfg.LongName, parkPath)
1220: 	}
1221: 
1222: 	restored, err := parkingNode.RestoreTo(ctx, d)
1223: 	if err != nil {
1224: 		return err
1225: 	}
1226: 
1227: 	for _, ep := range restored {
1228: 		log.Info("Restored link", "node", d.Cfg.ShortName, "interface", ep.GetIfaceName())
1229: 	}
1230: 
1231: 	if err := d.cleanupParkingNetNS(); err != nil {
1232: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to cleanup parking netns for node %q: %w", d.Cfg.ShortName, err)
1233: 	}
1234: 	d.parkingNode = nil
1235: 
1236: 	return nil
1237: }
1238: 
1239: func (d *DefaultNode) Stop(ctx context.Context) error {
1240: 	cfg := d.Config()
1241: 
1242: 	if d.ShouldSkipLifecycle() {
1243: 		log.Debugf("node %q skips lifecycle stop", cfg.ShortName)
1244: 		return nil
1245: 	}
1246: 
1247: 	status := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx)
1248: 	switch status {
1249: 	case clabruntime.Stopped:
1250: 		log.Debugf("node %q already stopped, skipping", cfg.ShortName)
1251: 		return nil
1252: 	case clabruntime.NotFound:
1253: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q container %q not found", cfg.ShortName, cfg.LongName)
1254: 	}
1255: 
1256: 	if err := d.OverwriteNode.PreStop(ctx); err != nil {
1257: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q pre-stop hook failed: %w", cfg.ShortName, err)
1258: 	}
1259: 
1260: 	if err := d.ParkEndpoints(ctx); err != nil {
1261: 		return err
1262: 	}
1263: 
1264: 	if err := d.Runtime.StopContainer(ctx, cfg.LongName, d.StopSignal); err != nil {
1265: 		// Docker/podman may return an error while the container is already stopped.
1266: 		// if ctr is already stopped, this is OK
1267: 		if d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx) == clabruntime.Stopped {
1268: 			log.Warnf(
1269: 				"node %q stop returned error but container is stopped: %v",
1270: 				cfg.ShortName,
1271: 				err,
1272: 			)
1273: 			return nil
1274: 		}
1275: 
1276: 		if restoreErr := d.RestoreEndpoints(ctx); restoreErr != nil {
1277: 			return errors.Join(
1278: 				fmt.Errorf("node %q failed stopping container: %w", cfg.ShortName, err),
1279: 				fmt.Errorf("failed to restore endpoints: %w", restoreErr),
1280: 			)
1281: 		}
1282: 
1283: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q failed stopping container: %w", cfg.ShortName, err)
1284: 	}
1285: 
1286: 	return nil
1287: }
1288: 
1289: // Start restarts a stopped container and restores its parked dataplane interfaces.
1290: func (d *DefaultNode) Start(ctx context.Context) error {
1291: 	cfg := d.Config()
1292: 
1293: 	if d.ShouldSkipLifecycle() {
1294: 		log.Debugf("node %q skips lifecycle start", cfg.ShortName)
1295: 		return nil
1296: 	}
1297: 
1298: 	status := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx)
1299: 
1300: 	switch status {
1301: 	case clabruntime.Running:
1302: 		log.Debugf("node %q already running, skipping", cfg.ShortName)
1303: 		return nil
1304: 	case clabruntime.NotFound:
1305: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q container %q not found", cfg.ShortName, cfg.LongName)
1306: 	}
1307: 
1308: 	if _, err := d.Runtime.StartContainer(ctx, cfg.LongName, d); err != nil {
1309: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q failed starting container: %w", cfg.ShortName, err)
1310: 	}
1311: 
1312: 	if err := d.RestoreEndpoints(ctx); err != nil {
1313: 		_ = d.Runtime.StopContainer(ctx, cfg.LongName, d.StopSignal)
1314: 		return err
1315: 	}
1316: 
1317: 	// Re-run topology exec commands on lifecycle start to restore node-local interface config
1318: 	// (for example IP addresses added during deploy exec phase).
1319: 	execCollection := clabexec.NewExecCollection()
1320: 	if err := d.RunExecFromConfig(ctx, execCollection); err != nil {
1321: 		log.Errorf(
1322: 			"failed to run exec commands for node %q on lifecycle start: %v",
1323: 			cfg.ShortName,
1324: 			err,
1325: 		)
1326: 	}
1327: 	execCollection.Log()
1328: 
1329: 	return nil
1330: }

oldCfg.Privileged != newCfg.Privileged {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"Privileged\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.CgroupnsMode != newCfg.CgroupnsMode {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"CgroupnsMode\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.CgroupParent != newCfg.CgroupParent {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"CgroupParent\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.PidMode != newCfg.PidMode {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"PidMode\")\n\t}\n\tif !clabutils.MapsEqualOrBothEmpty(oldCfg.Tmpfs, newCfg.Tmpfs) {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"Tmpfs\")\n\t}\n\tif !clabutils.SlicesEqualOrBothEmpty(oldCfg.SecurityOpts, newCfg.SecurityOpts) {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"SecurityOpts\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.ShmSize != newCfg.ShmSize {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"ShmSize\")\n\t}\n\tif !clabutils.PortSetsEqual(oldCfg.PortSet, newCfg.PortSet) {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"Ports\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.User != newCfg.User {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"User\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.NetworkMode != newCfg.NetworkMode {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"NetworkMode\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.MgmtNet != newCfg.MgmtNet {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"MgmtNet\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.Runtime != newCfg.Runtime {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"Runtime\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.CPU != newCfg.CPU {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"CPU\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.CPUSet != newCfg.CPUSet {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"CPUSet\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.Memory != newCfg.Memory {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"Memory\")\n\t}\n\tif oldCfg.License != newCfg.License {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"License\")\n\t}\n\t_, oldInvalid := oldCfg.KindSpecificConfig.(InvalidKindSpecificConfig)\n\t_, newInvalid := newCfg.KindSpecificConfig.(InvalidKindSpecificConfig)\n\n\tif oldInvalid || newInvalid ||\n\t\t!reflect.DeepEqual(oldCfg.KindSpecificConfig, newCfg.KindSpecificConfig) {\n\t\tdiff.Fields = append(diff.Fields, \"KindSpecificConfig\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn diff\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) ComputeReconcilePlan(diff *clabtypes.TopologyDiff) *ReconcileResult {\n\tresult := &ReconcileResult{Action: clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionNone}\n\n\tif diff == nil || !diff.HasDiff() {\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\n\taction := diff.DefaultAction()\n\tresult.Action = action\n\n\tswitch action {\n\tcase clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRestart:\n\t\tresult.Restarted = []string{d.Cfg.LongName}\n\tcase clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRecreate:\n\t\tresult.Recreated = []string{d.Cfg.LongName}\n\t}\n\n\treturn result\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) GetReconcilePlan(\n\t_ context.Context,\n\tdiff *clabtypes.TopologyDiff,\n) (*ReconcileResult, error) {\n\treturn d.ComputeReconcilePlan(diff), nil\n}\n\n// Reconcile applies the diff and executes the appropriate action (restart/recreate).\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) Reconcile(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tdiff *clabtypes.TopologyDiff,\n) (*ReconcileResult, error) {\n\tresult := d.ComputeReconcilePlan(diff)\n\n\tif result.Action == clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionNone {\n\t\treturn result, nil\n\t}\n\n\tlog.Info(\n\t\t\"Applying node changes\",\n\t\t\"node\",\n\t\td.Cfg.ShortName,\n\t\t\"action\",\n\t\tresult.Action,\n\t\t\"fields\",\n\t\tdiff.Fields,\n\t)\n\n\tswitch result.Action {\n\tcase clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRestart:\n\t\tif err := d.Stop(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"stop failed: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := d.Start(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"start failed: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRecreate:\n\t\tif err := d.Delete(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"delete failed: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) parkingNetNSName() string {\n\treturn clabutils.ParkingNetnsName(d.Cfg.LongName)\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) parkingNetNSPath() (string, error) {\n\treturn clabutils.GetNamedNetNS(d.parkingNetNSName())\n}\n\n// cleanupParkingNetNS removes the node-owned parking namespace if it exists.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) cleanupParkingNetNS() error {\n\tif _, err := d.parkingNetNSPath(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn netns.DeleteNamed(d.parkingNetNSName())\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) Delete(ctx context.Context) error {\n\tfor _, e := range d.Endpoints {\n\t\terr := e.GetLink().Remove(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx) == clabruntime.NotFound {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn d.Runtime.DeleteContainer(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) GetImages(_ context.Context) map[string]string {\n\treturn map[string]string{\n\t\tImageKey: d.Cfg.Image,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) GetContainers(ctx context.Context) ([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, error) {\n\tcnts, err := d.Runtime.ListContainers(ctx, []*clabtypes.GenericFilter{\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tFilterType: \"name\",\n\t\t\tMatch:      d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(),\n\t\t},\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\t// check that we retrieved some container information\n\t// otherwise throw ErrContainersNotFound error\n\tif len(cnts) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\"Node: %s. %w\",\n\t\t\td.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(),\n\t\t\tErrContainersNotFound,\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\treturn cnts, err\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) RunExecFromConfig(ctx context.Context, ec *clabexec.ExecCollection) error {\n\tfor _, e := range d.Config().Exec {\n\t\texec, err := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(e)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Warnf(\"Failed to parse the command string: %s, %v\", e, err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tres, err := d.OverwriteNode.RunExec(ctx, exec)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t// kinds which do not support exec functionality are skipped\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tec.Add(d.GetShortName(), res)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) UpdateConfigWithRuntimeInfo(ctx context.Context) error {\n\tcnts, err := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainers(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tif len(cnts) == 0 {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"no container runtime information retrieved\")\n\t}\n\n\t// network settings of a first container only\n\tnetSettings := cnts[0].NetworkSettings\n\n\td.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Address = netSettings.IPv4addr\n\td.Cfg.MgmtIPv4PrefixLength = netSettings.IPv4pLen\n\td.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Address = netSettings.IPv6addr\n\td.Cfg.MgmtIPv6PrefixLength = netSettings.IPv6pLen\n\td.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Gateway = netSettings.IPv4Gw\n\td.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Gateway = netSettings.IPv6Gw\n\n\td.Cfg.ContainerID = cnts[0].ID\n\n\td.Cfg.ResultingPortBindings = cnts[0].Ports\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// DeleteNetnsSymlink deletes the symlink file created for the container netns.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) DeleteNetnsSymlink() error {\n\tlog.Debugf(\"Deleting %s network namespace\", d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())\n\treturn clabutils.DeleteNetnsSymlink(d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) CheckInterfaceOverlap() error {\n\tvar seenEps []clablinks.Endpoint\n\n\tfor _, ep := range d.Endpoints {\n\t\tifName := ep.GetIfaceName()\n\t\tfor _, seenEp := range seenEps {\n\t\t\tif ifName == seenEp.GetIfaceName() {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"%q and %q have overlapping interface names\", ep, seenEp)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tseenEps = append(seenEps, ep)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// CheckInterfaceName checks if a name of the interface referenced in the topology file is in the\n// expected range of name values.\n// A no-op for the default node, specific nodes should implement this method.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) CheckInterfaceName() error {\n\treturn d.CheckInterfaceOverlap()\n}\n\n// CalculateInterfaceIndex parses the supplied interface name with the InterfaceRegexp.\n// Using the the port offset, it calculates the mapped interface index based on the InterfaceOffset\n// and the first data interface index.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) CalculateInterfaceIndex(ifName string) (int, error) {\n\tcaptureGroups, err := clabutils.GetRegexpCaptureGroups(d.InterfaceRegexp, ifName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\n\tif parsedIndex, found := captureGroups[\"port\"]; found {\n\t\tparsedIndexInt, err := strconv.Atoi(parsedIndex)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\t\"%q parsed index %q could not be cast to an integer\",\n\t\t\t\tifName,\n\t\t\t\tparsedIndex,\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t\tcalculatedIndex := parsedIndexInt - d.InterfaceOffset + d.FirstDataIfIndex\n\t\treturn calculatedIndex, nil\n\t} else {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\"%q does not have extracted interface index with regexp %q, 'port' capture group missing?\",\n\t\t\tifName,\n\t\t\td.InterfaceRegexp,\n\t\t)\n\t}\n}\n\n// GetMappedInterfaceName returns with a mapped interface name based on the mapped interface prefix\n// and calculated mapped interface index.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) GetMappedInterfaceName(ifName string) (string, error) {\n\tifIndex, err := d.OverwriteNode.CalculateInterfaceIndex(ifName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tif ifIndex < d.FirstDataIfIndex {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\"extracted interface index for %q is out of bounds: %d ! >= %d\",\n\t\t\tifName,\n\t\t\tifIndex,\n\t\t\td.FirstDataIfIndex,\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\tmappedIfName := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s%d\", d.InterfaceMappedPrefix, ifIndex)\n\n\treturn mappedIfName, nil\n}\n\n// VerifyStartupConfig verifies that startup config files exists on disks.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) VerifyStartupConfig(topoDir string) error {\n\tcfg := d.Config().StartupConfig\n\tif cfg == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\trcfg := clabutils.ResolvePath(cfg, topoDir)\n\tif !clabutils.FileExists(rcfg) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"node %q startup-config file not found by the path %s\",\n\t\t\td.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), rcfg)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// GenerateConfig generates configuration for the nodes\n// out of the template `t` based on the node configuration and saves the result to dst.\n// If the config file is already present in the node dir we do not regenerate the config unless\n// EnforceStartupConfig is explicitly set to true and startup-config points to a file this will\n// persist the changes that users make to a running config when booted from some startup config.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) GenerateConfig(dst, t string) error {\n\t// Check for incompatible options\n\tif d.Cfg.EnforceStartupConfig && d.Cfg.SuppressStartupConfig {\n\t\treturn ErrIncompatibleOptions\n\t}\n\tif d.Cfg.EnforceStartupConfig && d.Cfg.StartupConfig == \"\" {\n\t\treturn ErrNoStartupConfig\n\t}\n\n\tif d.Cfg.SuppressStartupConfig {\n\t\tlog.Info(\"Startup config generation suppressed\", \"node\", d.Cfg.ShortName)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tif !d.Cfg.EnforceStartupConfig && clabutils.FileExists(dst) {\n\t\tlog.Debug(\"Existing config found\", \"node\", d.Cfg.ShortName, \"path\", dst)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t} else {\n\t\tlog.Debug(\"Generating config\", \"node\", d.Cfg.ShortName, \"file\", d.Cfg.StartupConfig)\n\n\t\tcfgBuf, err := clabutils.SubstituteEnvsAndTemplate(strings.NewReader(t), d.Cfg)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tlog.Debug(\"Generated config\", \"node\", d.Cfg.ShortName, \"content\", cfgBuf.String())\n\n\t\tf, err := os.Create(dst)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tdefer f.Close()\n\n\t\t_, err = f.Write(cfgBuf.Bytes())\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// NodeOverwrites is an interface that every node implements.\n// It is used to enable DefaultNode to get access to the particular node structs\n// and is provided as an argument of the NewDefaultNode function.\n// The methods defined for this interfaces are the methods that particular node has a custom\n// implementation of.\ntype NodeOverwrites interface {\n\tVerifyStartupConfig(topoDir string) error\n\tCheckInterfaceName() error\n\tCalculateInterfaceIndex(ifName string) (int, error)\n\tGetMappedInterfaceName(ifName string) (string, error)\n\tVerifyHostRequirements() error\n\tPullImage(ctx context.Context) error\n\tGetImages(ctx context.Context) map[string]string\n\tGetContainers(ctx context.Context) ([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, error)\n\tGetContainerName() string\n\tGetContainerStatus(ctx context.Context) clabruntime.ContainerStatus\n\tVerifyContainerName() error\n\tVerifyLicenseFileExists(context.Context) error\n\tPreStop(context.Context) error\n\tRunExec(context.Context, *clabexec.ExecCmd) (*clabexec.ExecResult, error)\n\tGetNSPath(ctx context.Context) (string, error)\n}\n\n// LoadStartupConfigFileVr templates a startup-config using the file specified for VM-based nodes in\n// the topo\n// and puts the resulting config file by the LabDir/configDirName/startupCfgFName path.\nfunc LoadStartupConfigFileVr(node Node, configDirName, startupCfgFName string) error {\n\tnodeCfg := node.Config()\n\t// create config directory that will be bind mounted to vrnetlab container at / path\n\tclabutils.CreateDirectory(path.Join(nodeCfg.LabDir, configDirName),\n\t\tclabconstants.PermissionsOpen)\n\n\tif nodeCfg.StartupConfig != \"\" {\n\t\t// dstCfg is a path to a file on the clab host that will have rendered configuration\n\t\tdstCfg := filepath.Join(nodeCfg.LabDir, configDirName, startupCfgFName)\n\n\t\tc, err := os.ReadFile(nodeCfg.StartupConfig)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcfgTemplate := string(c)\n\n\t\terr = node.GenerateConfig(dstCfg, cfgTemplate)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Errorf(\"node=%s, failed to generate config: %v\", nodeCfg.ShortName, err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// GetContainerName returns the name used by the runtime to identify the container\n// e.g. ext-container nodes use the name as defined in the topo file, while most other containers\n// use long (prefixed) name.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) GetContainerName() string {\n\treturn d.Cfg.LongName\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) VerifyContainerName() error {\n\tcontainerName := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName()\n\tif !containerNamePatternRe.MatchString(containerName) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"Node name contains invalid characters: %s\", containerName)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(containerName) > maxNameLength {\n\t\tlog.Warn(\n\t\t\t\"Node name will not resolve via DNS\",\n\t\t\t\"name\",\n\t\t\tcontainerName,\n\t\t\t\"reason\",\n\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"name exceeds %d characters\", maxNameLength),\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\tif strings.ContainsAny(containerName, dnsIncompatibleChars) {\n\t\tlog.Warn(\n\t\t\t\"Node name will not resolve via DNS\",\n\t\t\t\"name\",\n\t\t\tcontainerName,\n\t\t\t\"reason\",\n\t\t\t\"name contains invalid characters such as '.' and/or '_'\",\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// RunExec executes a single command for a node.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) RunExec(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\texecCmd *clabexec.ExecCmd,\n) (*clabexec.ExecResult, error) {\n\texecResult, err := d.GetRuntime().Exec(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), execCmd)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Errorf(\"%s: failed to execute cmd: %q with error %v\",\n\t\t\td.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), execCmd.GetCmdString(), err)\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn execResult, nil\n}\n\n// RunExecNotWait executes a command for a node, and doesn't block waiting for the output.\n// Should be overridden if the nodes implementation differs.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) RunExecNotWait(ctx context.Context, execCmd *clabexec.ExecCmd) error {\n\terr := d.GetRuntime().ExecNotWait(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), execCmd)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Errorf(\"%s: failed to execute cmd: %q with error %v\",\n\t\t\td.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName(), execCmd.GetCmdString(), err)\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// VerifyLicenseFileExists checks if a license file with a provided path exists.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) VerifyLicenseFileExists(_ context.Context) error {\n\tif d.Config().License == \"\" {\n\t\tswitch d.LicensePolicy {\n\t\t// if a license is required by the kind but not provided\n\t\tcase clabtypes.LicensePolicyRequired:\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\t\"node %s of kind %s requires a license. Provide one via 'license' knob in the topology file\",\n\t\t\t\td.Config().ShortName,\n\t\t\t\td.Cfg.Kind,\n\t\t\t)\n\t\tcase clabtypes.LicensePolicyWarn:\n\t\t\t// just warn when no license is provided\n\t\t\tlog.Warnf(\n\t\t\t\t\"node %s of kind %s requires a license. Make sure to provide it in some way (e.g. license knob or baked into image)\",\n\t\t\t\td.Config().ShortName,\n\t\t\t\td.Cfg.Kind,\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\tcase clabtypes.LicensePolicyNone:\n\t\t\t// license is not required\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"unknown license policy value %s for node %s kind %s\",\n\t\t\t\td.LicensePolicy, d.Config().ShortName, d.Cfg.Kind)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// if license is provided check path exists\n\trlic := clabutils.ResolvePath(d.Config().License, d.Cfg.LabDir)\n\tif !clabutils.FileExists(rlic) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\"license file for node %q is not found by the path %s\",\n\t\t\td.Config().ShortName,\n\t\t\trlic,\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// LoadOrGenerateCertificate loads a certificate using a certificate storage provider\n// provided in certInfra or generates a new one if it does not exist.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) LoadOrGenerateCertificate(\n\tcertInfra *clabcert.Cert,\n\ttopoName string,\n) (nodeCert *clabcert.Certificate, err error) {\n\t// early return if certificate generation is not required\n\tif d.Cfg.Certificate == nil || !*d.Cfg.Certificate.Issue {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tnodeConfig := d.Cfg\n\n\t// try loading existing certificates from disk and generate new ones if they do not exist\n\tnodeCert, err = certInfra.LoadNodeCert(nodeConfig.ShortName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Debugf(\"creating node certificate for %s\", nodeConfig.ShortName)\n\n\t\thosts := []string{\n\t\t\tnodeConfig.ShortName,\n\t\t\tnodeConfig.LongName,\n\t\t\tnodeConfig.ShortName + \".\" + topoName + \".io\",\n\t\t}\n\t\t// add the SANs provided via config\n\t\thosts = append(hosts, nodeConfig.Certificate.SANs...)\n\n\t\t// add mgmt IPs as SANs to CSR\n\t\tfor _, ip := range []string{d.Cfg.MgmtIPv4Address, d.Cfg.MgmtIPv6Address} {\n\t\t\tif ip != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\thosts = append(hosts, ip)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcertInput := &clabcert.NodeCSRInput{\n\t\t\tCommonName:   nodeConfig.ShortName + \".\" + topoName + \".io\",\n\t\t\tHosts:        hosts,\n\t\t\tOrganization: \"containerlab\",\n\t\t\tCountry:      \"US\",\n\t\t\tKeySize:      d.Cfg.Certificate.KeySize,\n\t\t\tExpiry:       d.Cfg.Certificate.ValidityDuration,\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Generate the cert for the node\n\t\tnodeCert, err = certInfra.GenerateAndSignNodeCert(certInput)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// persist the cert via certStorage\n\t\terr = certInfra.StoreNodeCert(nodeConfig.ShortName, nodeCert)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nodeCert, nil\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) GetHostsEntries(ctx context.Context) (clabtypes.HostEntries, error) {\n\tcontainers, err := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainers(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tresult := clabtypes.HostEntries{}\n\n\tfor idx := range containers {\n\t\tif len(containers[idx].Names) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif containers[idx].NetworkSettings.IPv4addr != \"\" {\n\t\t\tresult = append(result, clabtypes.NewHostEntry(\n\t\t\t\tcontainers[idx].NetworkSettings.IPv4addr,\n\t\t\t\tcontainers[idx].Names[0],\n\t\t\t\tclabtypes.IpVersionV4,\n\t\t\t).SetDescription(fmt.Sprintf(\"Kind: %s\", d.Cfg.Kind)).SetContainerID(containers[idx].ID))\n\t\t}\n\t\tif containers[idx].NetworkSettings.IPv6addr != \"\" {\n\t\t\tresult = append(result, clabtypes.NewHostEntry(\n\t\t\t\tcontainers[idx].NetworkSettings.IPv6addr,\n\t\t\t\tcontainers[idx].Names[0],\n\t\t\t\tclabtypes.IpVersionV6,\n\t\t\t).SetDescription(fmt.Sprintf(\"Kind: %s\", d.Cfg.Kind)).SetContainerID(containers[idx].ID))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) AddLinkToContainer(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tlink netlink.Link,\n\tf func(ns.NetNS) error,\n) error {\n\tif !d.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased {\n\t\tst := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx)\n\t\tif !clabruntime.ContainerHasJoinableNetns(st) {\n\t\t\tif st == clabruntime.Stopped {\n\t\t\t\tcontainerName := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName()\n\t\t\t\td.GetRuntime().LogNonRunningContainerOutput(ctx, containerName)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\t\"node %q: cannot attach link, container network namespace is not available (status=%s)\",\n\t\t\t\td.Cfg.ShortName,\n\t\t\t\tst,\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// retrieve nodes nspath\n\tnsp, err := d.OverwriteNode.GetNSPath(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\t// retrieve the namespace handle\n\tnetns, err := ns.GetNS(nsp)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\t// move veth endpoint to namespace\n\tif err := netlink.LinkSetNsFd(link, int(netns.Fd())); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\t// execute the given function\n\terr = netns.Do(f)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ExecFunction executes the given function in the nodes network namespace.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) ExecFunction(ctx context.Context, f func(ns.NetNS) error) error {\n\tif !d.Cfg.IsRootNamespaceBased {\n\t\tst := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx)\n\t\tif !clabruntime.ContainerHasJoinableNetns(st) {\n\t\t\tif st == clabruntime.Stopped {\n\t\t\t\tcontainerName := = append(d.Endpoints, e)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) AdoptEndpoint(e clablinks.Endpoint) error {\n\tif e == nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"node %q cannot adopt a nil endpoint\", d.Cfg.ShortName)\n\t}\n\n\towner := e.GetNode()\n\tif owner == nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\"node %q cannot adopt endpoint %q without an clablinks.Endpoint) error {\n\tfor i, ep := range d.Endpoints {\n\t\tif ep != e {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\td.Endpoints = append(d.Endpoints[:i], d.Endpoints[i+1:]...)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"node %q does not own endpoint %q\", d.Cfg.ShortName, e.GetIfaceName())\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) GetEndpoints() []clablinks.Endpoint {\n\treturn d.Runtime.IsHealthy(ctx, d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerName())\n}\n\n// ParkEndpoints moves all tracked endpoints into the node-owned parking namespace.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) ParkEndpoints(ctx context.Context) error {\n\tparkPath, err := clabutils.CreateOrGetNamedNetNS(d.parkingNetNSName())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create parking netns for node %q: %w\", d.Cfg.ShortName, err)\n\t}\n\t// Reuse the parking node retained by a failed restore so it keeps the parked endpoints.\n\tparkingNode := d.parkingNode\n\treused := parkingNode != nil\n\tif !reused {\n\t\tparkingNode = clablinks.NewParkingNode(d.Cfg.LongName, parkPath)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := parkingNode.CaptureFrom(ctx, d); err != nil {\n\t\tif !reused {\n\t\t\t_ = its interfaces.\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) RestoreEndpoints(ctx context.Context) error {\n\tparkPath, err := d.parkingNetNSPath()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// No parking netns means the node had no parked interfaces - e.g. it was\n\t\t// stopped outside of containerlab (so its interfaces were never parked) or\n\t\t// it has no dataplane links. Nothing to restore.\n\t\td.parkingNode = nil\n\t\tlog.Debugf(\"node %q has no parking netns, nothing to restore\", d.Cfg.ShortName)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tparkingNode := d.parkingNode\n\tif parkingNode == nil {\n\t\tparkingNode = clablinks.NewParkingNode(d.Cfg.LongName, parkPath)\n\t}\n\n\trestored, err := parkingNode.RestoreTo(ctx, d)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, ep := range restored {\n\t\tlog.Info(\"Restored link\", \"node\", d.Cfg.ShortName, \"interface\", ep.GetIfaceName())\n\t}\n\n\tif err := d.cleanupParkingNetNS(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to cleanup parking netns for node %q: %w\", d.Cfg.ShortName, err)\n\t}\n\td.parkingNode = nil\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (d *DefaultNode) Stop(ctx context.Context) error {\n\tcfg := d.Config()\n\n\tif d.ShouldSkipLifecycle() {\n\t\tlog.Debugf(\"node %q skips lifecycle stop\", cfg.ShortName)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tstatus := d.OverwriteNode.GetContainerStatus(ctx)\n\tswitch status {\n\tcase clabruntime.Stopped:\n\t\tlog.Debugf(\"node %q already stopped, skipping\", cfg.ShortName)\n\t\treturn nil\n\tcase clabruntime.NotFound:\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"node %q container %q not found\", cfg.ShortName, cfg.LongName)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := d.OverwriteNode.PreStop(ctx); Now let me understand the important interplay:

1. `srl.Stop` calls `n.DefaultNode.Stop(ctx)` first — this parks endpoints (ParkEndpoints), then stops the srl container. Then it stops the holder container. But wait: ParkEndpoints moves endpoints to a parking namespace named after the node (ParkingNetnsName(n.Cfg.LongName)), then restores on Start. With the holder, links are attached to the holder's netns. Actually wait — the endpoints are deployed to the node's netns path (via GetNSPath which returns the holder's path). So the veth endpoints live in the holder's netns. On Stop, ParkEndpoints captures them into the parking netns. Then the srl container is stopped. Then the holder is stopped (its netns destroyed), which is fine since endpoints were parked.

Actually wait — on Stop, ParkEndpoints moves interfaces into the parking netns *before* stopping the srl container, so this works.

On Start: ensureNetnsRunning (starts the holder) → DefaultNode.Start (starts srl container with `container:netns` network mode — the netns must exist; the holder being started creates the netns; RestoreEndpoints restores endpoints into the node's netns via the symlink... but the symlink is repointed in linkNetns AFTER DefaultNode.Start returns. Hmm — DefaultNode.Start calls RestoreEndpoints which uses the node's netns symlink (from the labdir? Let me check how the symlink works). So it doesn't depend on the symlink. OK.

But there's an ordering subtlety: Start calls DefaultNode.Start → RestoreEndpoints (moves veth endpoints into holder netns) → then linkNetns repoints the symlink. Fine.

2. During `Deploy`, the flow: the lab deploy presumably calls node.Deploy(ctx, params) for each node, then deploys links (DeployEndpoints?). Hmm, wait — actually there's a subtlety: with the holder, does Stop of the srl container leave the interfaces in the holder's netns? Yes. And then they park them and stop the holder anyway. So both mechanisms co-exist. OK.

Reasoning
Now, an important observation: `core/topology_reconcile.go` line 731 checks `topo.GetNodeLabels(nodeName)[clabconstants.InternalNode] == "true"`. And `core/tailscale.go` — the srl netns node's labels set InternalNode=true, good, similar to sros.

But wait: the srl netns node's labels are set on the *holder* container config only (newNetnsConfig clones n.Cfg.Labels and adds labels). The srl node itself doesn't get InternalNode label — good.

Now, key question for reconcile/restore: `core/topology_reconcile.go` — how does the reconcile flow treat the srl node with the netnsNode? Let me read that file, particularly around node enumeration and restore.

Also, how does `destroy` enumerate containers? cmd/inspect.go line 449: "if !o.Destroy.All && containers[idx].Labels[clabconstants.InternalNode] == 'true'" — filters internal nodes from destroy list unless --all. But destroy then re-instantiates nodes from the topology file and calls node.Delete... Actually the destroy flow: it loads the lab from the topology file (parsed from topology file, or from auto-generated clab file in lab dir). The srl node's Init will create the netnsNode (if config loaded from file has the labels?). Hmm — when destroy re-parses the topology, does the saved labels include InternalNode? Let's think about how destroy works: `Destroy` loads the topology and creates nodes; srl.Init creates netnsNode; then Destroy calls node.Delete which deletes both containers. So destroy works.

But what about `clab destroy --cleanup` in the test: "Verify no lab containers are left after destroy" — checks no containers with label containerlab=lab-name remain. The holder has the containerlab label (cloned) so it would be caught if left. OK.

Now the critical reconcile path: `core/topology_reconcile.go` — this is a new-ish feature that compares the deployed state with the topology and restarts/recreates nodes. Let me read it, especially how it collects containers for nodes and whether the srl netns node could confuse it.

106: func (p *applyPlan) isNonContainerNode(nodeName string) bool {
107: 	return p.isExternallyManaged(nodeName) || p.isRootNamespaceNode(nodeName)
108: }

110: // isNewNonContainerNode reports whether a non-container node (bridge, host,
111: // ext-container) is absent from the previously deployed topology. Such nodes
112: // exist outside the lab, so they are always listed as current nodes, even
113: // when the topology has just added them.
114: func (p *applyPlan) isNewNonContainerNode(nodeName string) bool {
115: 	if !p.isNonContainerNode(nodeName) || p.state == nil || p.state.Topology == nil {
116: 		return false
117: 	}
118: 	_, exists := p.state.Topology.Nodes[nodeName]
119: 	return !exists
120: }
