122: // networkModeContainerTarget returns the referenced node name for a
123: // "network-mode: container:<name>" config, or "" if networkMode does not
124: // share another container's network namespace.
125: func networkModeContainerTarget(networkMode string) string {
126: 	if target, shared := strings.CutPrefix(networkMode, "container:"); shared {
127: 		return target
128: 	}
129: 	return ""
130: }
131: 
132: // checkApplyNetworkModeTargets rejects apply when a node's network-mode:
133: // container:<target> target is being removed from the topology while the
134: // dependent node stays desired. Deleting the target would leave the
135: // dependent silently attached to a defunct network namespace.
136: func (c *CLab) checkApplyNetworkModeTargets(plan *applyPlan) error {
137: 	if plan == nil {
138: 		return nil
139: 	}
140: 
141: 	for _, nodeName := range sortedNodeNames(c.Nodes) {
142: 		networkMode := c.Nodes[nodeName].Config().NetworkMode
143: 		target := networkModeContainerTarget(networkMode)
144: 		if target == "" {
145: 			continue
146: 		}
147: 		if _, deleted := plan.deletedNodeSet[target]; deleted {
148: 			return fmt.Errorf(
149: 				"node %q has network-mode %q, but %q is being removed from the topology; "+
150: 					"remove or retarget %q as well",
151: 				nodeName,
152: 				networkMode,
153: 				target,
154: 				nodeName,
155: 			)
156: 		}
157: 	}
158: 
159: 	return nil
160: }
161: 
162: // planNetworkModeCascade forces recreation of nodes whose network-mode:
163: // container:<target> target is itself being recreated. Docker binds
164: // NetworkMode to the target container's ID at creation time, so a plain
165: // restart of the dependent cannot rebind it to the target's new container;
166: // only recreating the dependent does. A reverse dependency graph visits each
167: // node and dependency at most once, including long namespace-sharing chains.
168: func (c *CLab) planNetworkModeCascade(plan *applyPlan) {
169: 	if plan == nil || len(plan.recreatedNodeSet) == 0 {
170: 		return
171: 	}
172: 
173: 	dependents := make(map[string][]string)
174: 	for name, node := range c.Nodes {
175: 		if target := networkModeContainerTarget(node.Config().NetworkMode); target != "" {
176: 			dependents[target] = append(dependents[target], name)
177: 		}
178: 	}
179: 	queue := make([]string, 0, len(c.Nodes))
180: 	for name := range plan.recreatedNodeSet {
181: 		queue = append(queue, name)
182: 	}
183: 	for i := 0; i < len(queue); i++ {
184: 		target := queue[i]
185: 		for _, nodeName := range dependents[target] {
186: 			if _, recreated := plan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName]; recreated {
187: 				continue
188: 			}
189: 			if _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName]; added {
190: 				continue
191: 			}
192: 
193: 			plan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
194: 			delete(plan.restartNodeSet, nodeName)
195: 			delete(plan.linkRestartNodeSet, nodeName)
196: 			plan.nodeChangeReasons[nodeName] = fmt.Sprintf(
197: 				"network-mode target %q recreated", target,
198: 			)
199: 			queue = append(queue, nodeName)
200: 		}
201: 	}
202: }
203: 
204: func (c *CLab) planApply(
205: 	ctx context.Context,
206: 	currentNodes map[string]*runtimeNodeGroup,
207: ) (*applyPlan, error) {
208: 	state, err := c.LoadState()
209: 	if err != nil {
210: 		log.Warn("Failed to load state file", "error", err)
211: 	}
212: 
213: 	plan := newApplyPlan(currentNodes, state)
214: 
215: 	for _, nodeName := range sortedNodeNames(c.Nodes) {
216: 		if _, exists := currentNodes[nodeName]; !exists {
217: 			status := c.Nodes[nodeName].GetContainerStatus(ctx)
218: 			if status != clabruntime.NotFound {
219: 				return nil, fmt.Errorf(
220: 					"node %q container %q already exists outside the runtime lab state",
221: 					nodeName,
222: 					c.Nodes[nodeName].Config().LongName,
223: 				)
224: 			}
225: 
226: 			plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
227: 			continue
228: 		}
229: 
230: 		if plan.isNewNonContainerNode(nodeName) {
231: 			plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
232: 		}
233: 	}
234: 
235: 	for _, nodeName := range sortedRuntimeNodeGroupNames(currentNodes) {
236: 		if _, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]; !exists {
237: 			plan.deletedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
238: 		}
239: 	}
240: 
241: 	if err := c.checkApplyNetworkModeTargets(plan); err != nil {
242: 		return nil, err
243: 	}
244: 
245: 	if err := c.planNodeReconciliation(ctx, plan); err != nil {
246: 		return nil, err
247: 	}
248: 
249: 	c.planParkedNodes(ctx, plan)
250: 	c.planStoppedNodes(ctx, plan)
251: 
252: 	for _, linkIdx := range sortedLinkIndexes(c.Links) {
253: 		for _, ep := range clablinks.RuntimeEndpoints(c.Links[linkIdx]) {
254: 			plan.desiredEndpointSet[endpointKeyFromEndpoint(ep)] = struct{}{}
255: 		}
256: 	}
257: 
258: 	if err := c.discoverLiveApplyEndpoints(ctx, plan); err != nil {
259: 		return nil, err
260: 	}
261: 
262: 	if err := c.validateDesiredEndpointOwnership(ctx, plan); err != nil {
263: 		return nil, err
264: 	}
265: 
266: 	c.planDeletedEndpoints(ctx, plan)
267: 
268: 	for _, linkIdx := range sortedLinkIndexes(c.Links) {
269: 		link := c.Links[linkIdx]
270: 		if !plan.linkNeedsDeploy(link) {
271: 			continue
272: 		}
273: 
274: 		plan.addDeployApplyLink(linkIdx, link)
275: 
276: 		for _, ep := range clablinks.RuntimeEndpoints(link) {
277: 			nodeName := ep.GetNode().GetShortName()
278: 			if _, exists := currentNodes[nodeName]; !exists {
279: 				continue
280: 			}
281: 			if _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName]; added {
282: 				continue
283: 			}
284: 
285: 			c.planAffectedApplyNode(ctx, plan, nodeName, "added link")
286: 		}
287: 	}
288: 
289: 	// Link reconciliation can request additional recreations. Propagate namespace
290: 	// dependencies only after those decisions, then park every affected live node.
291: 	c.planNetworkModeCascade(plan)
292: 	c.planTailscaleSidecarRecreates(plan)
293: 	c.planParkedNodes(ctx, plan)
294: 	c.planRecreatedNodeLinks(plan)
295: 	for nodeName := range plan.recreatedNodeSet {
296: 		delete(plan.restartNodeSet, nodeName)
297: 		delete(plan.linkRestartNodeSet, nodeName)
298: 		delete(plan.startNodeSet, nodeName)
299: 	}
300: 	if err := c.planNetworkModeRestarts(plan); err != nil {
301: 		return nil, err
302: 	}
303: 
304: 	return plan, nil
305: }
306: 
307: func (p *applyPlan) addDeployApplyLink(linkIdx int, link clablinks.Link) {
308: 	if _, planned := p.plannedLinkSet[linkIdx]; planned {
309: 		return
310: 	}
311: 
312: 	p.plannedLinkSet[linkIdx] = struct{}{}
313: 	p.addedLinks = append(p.addedLinks, link)
314: }

316: func (c *CLab) planParkedNodes(ctx context.Context, plan *applyPlan) {
317: 	if plan == nil {
318: 		return
319: 	}
320: 
321: 	for nodeName := range plan.recreatedNodeSet {
322: 		if _, parked := plan.parkedNodeSet[nodeName]; parked {
323: 			continue
324: 		}
325: 		node, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]
326: 		if !exists {
327: 			continue
328: 		}
329: 		if clabruntime.ContainerHasJoinableNetns(node.GetContainerStatus(ctx)) {
330: 			plan.parkedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
331: 		}
332: 	}
333: }
334: 
335: func (c *CLab) planStoppedNodes(ctx context.Context, plan *applyPlan) {
336: 	if plan == nil {
337: 		return
338: 	}
339: 
340: 	for nodeName := range plan.currentNodes {
341: 		if plan.isNonContainerNode(nodeName) {
342: 			continue
343: 		}
344: 		node, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]
345: 		if !exists {
346: 			continue
347: 		}
348: 		if _, deleted := plan.deletedNodeSet[nodeName]; deleted {
349: 			continue
350: 		}
351: 		if _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName]; added {
352: 			continue
353: 		}
354: 		if _, recreated := plan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName]; recreated {
355: 			continue
356: 		}
357: 
358: 		switch node.GetContainerStatus(ctx) {
359: 		case clabruntime.Stopped, clabruntime.Created:
360: 			plan.startNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
361: 		}
362: 	}
363: }

365: func (c *CLab) planRecreatedNodeLinks(plan *applyPlan) {
366: 	if plan == nil || len(plan.recreatedNodeSet) == 0 {
367: 		return
368: 	}
369: 
370: 	rebuildSet := make(map[string]struct{}, len(plan.recreatedNodeSet))
371: 	for nodeName := range plan.recreatedNodeSet {
372: 		if _, parked := plan.parkedNodeSet[nodeName]; parked {
373: 			continue
374: 		}
375: 		rebuildSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
376: 	}
377: 
378: 	for _, linkIdx := range sortedLinkIndexes(c.Links) {
379: 		link := c.Links[linkIdx]
380: 		if !linkTouchesNodeSet(link, rebuildSet) {
381: 			continue
382: 		}
383: 
384: 		plan.addDeployApplyLink(linkIdx, link)
385: 	}
386: }
387: 
388: func linkTouchesNodeSet(link clablinks.Link, nodeSet map[string]struct{}) bool {
389: 	if len(nodeSet) == 0 {
390: 		return false
391: 	}
392: 
393: 	for _, ep := range clablinks.RuntimeEndpoints(link) {
394: 		key := endpointKeyFromEndpoint(ep)
395: 		if _, exists := nodeSet[key.node]; exists {
396: 			return true
397: 		}
398: 	}
399: 
400: 	return false
401: }
402: 
403: func (c *CLab) planDeletedEndpoints(ctx context.Context, plan *applyPlan) {
404: 	plannedEndpointSet := map[applyEndpointKey]struct{}{}
405: 
406: 	for _, key := range sortedEndpointKeys(plan.liveEndpointSet) {
407: 		if _, exists := plan.desiredEndpointSet[key]; exists {
408: 			continue
409: 		}
410: 		c.addStaleApplyEndpoint(ctx, plan, plannedEndpointSet, key)
411: 	}
412: }
413: 
414: func (c *CLab) addStaleApplyEndpoint(
415: 	ctx context.Context,
416: 	plan *applyPlan,
417: 	plannedEndpointSet map[applyEndpointKey]struct{},
418: 	key applyEndpointKey,
419: ) {
420: 	if key.node == "" || key.iface == "" {
421: 		return
422: 	}
423: 	if _, exists := plannedEndpointSet[key]; exists {
424: 		return
425: 	}
426: 	if _, desired := plan.desiredEndpointSet[key]; desired {
427: 		return
428: 	}
429: 
430: 	node, exists := plan.endpointNode(key.node)
431: 	if !exists {
432: 		node, exists = c.applyLinkNode(key.node)
433: 	}
434: 	if !exists {
435: 		return
436: 	}
437: 
438: 	plannedEndpointSet[key] = struct{}{}
439: 	plan.staleEndpoints = append(plan.staleEndpoints, applyEndpointRef{key: key, node: node})
440: 
441: 	if _, exists := c.Nodes[key.node]; exists {
442: 		if _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[key.node]; !added {
443: 			c.planAffectedApplyNode(ctx, plan, key.node, "deleted endpoint")
444: 		}
445: 	}
446: 
447: 	c.addDerivedHostStaleEndpoints(plan, plannedEndpointSet, key)
448: }
449: 
450: func (c *CLab) addDerivedHostStaleEndpoints(
451: 	plan *applyPlan,
452: 	plannedEndpointSet map[applyEndpointKey]struct{},
453: 	key applyEndpointKey,
454: ) {
455: 	if key.node == "host" {
456: 		return
457: 	}
458: 	if _, exists := c.Nodes[key.node]; !exists {
459: 		return
460: 	}
461: 
462: 	hostNode, exists := c.applyLinkNode("host")
463: 	if !exists {
464: 		return
465: 	}
466: 
467: 	for _, iface := range []string{
468: 		fmt.Sprintf("ve-%s_%s", key.node, key.iface),
469: 		fmt.Sprintf("vx-%s_%s", key.node, key.iface),
470: 	} {
471: 		hostKey := applyEndpointKey{node: "host", iface: iface}
472: 		if _, planned := plannedEndpointSet[hostKey]; planned {
473: 			continue
474: 		}
475: 		if _, desired := plan.desiredEndpointSet[hostKey]; desired {
476: 			continue
477: 		}
478: 
479: 		plannedEndpointSet[hostKey] = struct{}{}
480: 		plan.staleEndpoints = append(
481: 			plan.staleEndpoints,
482: 			applyEndpointRef{key: hostKey, node: hostNode, bestEffort: true},
483: 		)
484: 	}
485: }
486: 
487: func (c *CLab) discoverLiveApplyEndpoints(
488: 	ctx context.Context,
489: 	plan *applyPlan,
490: ) error {
491: 	desiredNodes := c.applyEndpointDiscoveryNodes(plan)
492: 	for _, nodeName := range sortedLinkNodeNames(desiredNodes) {
493: 		n := desiredNodes[nodeName]
494: 		if _, exists := plan.currentNodes[nodeName]; !exists && !isApplySpecialNode(nodeName) {
495: 			continue
496: 		}
497: 
498: 		if _, recreate := plan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName]; recreate {
499: 			if _, parked := plan.parkedNodeSet[nodeName]; !parked {
500: 				continue
501: 			}
502: 		}
503: 		if _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName]; added {
504: 			if _, parked := plan.parkedNodeSet[nodeName]; !parked {
505: 				continue
506: 			}
507: 		}
508: 		if _, start := plan.startNodeSet[nodeName]; start {
509: 			parkingNode, ok := c.applyParkingLinkNode(nodeName)
510: 			if !ok {
511: 				continue
512: 			}
513: 			n = parkingNode
514: 		} else if !isApplySpecialNode(nodeName) && !plan.isRootNamespaceNode(nodeName) {
515: 			status := c.Nodes[nodeName].GetContainerStatus(ctx)
516: 			if !clabruntime.ContainerHasJoinableNetns(status) {
517: 				if plan.isExternallyManaged(nodeName) {
518: 					return fmt.Errorf(
519: 						"externally managed node %q is %s; "+
520: 							"start it outside containerlab before running apply",
521: 						nodeName,
522: 						status,
523: 					)
524: 				}
525: 				return fmt.Errorf(
526: 					"node %q is %s; apply requires existing nodes to have a joinable network namespace",
527: 					nodeName,
528: 					status,
529: 				)
530: 			}
531: 		}
532: 
533: 		plan.setEndpointNode(nodeName, n)
534: 
535: 		interfaces, err := clablinks.DiscoverOwnedInterfacesFor(
536: 			ctx,
537: 			n,
538: 			nodeName,
539: 			c.applyKnownEndpointNames(plan, nodeName),
540: 		)
541: 		if err != nil {
542: 			return fmt.Errorf(
543: 				"failed to discover runtime interfaces for node %q: %w",
544: 				nodeName,
545: 				err,
546: 			)
547: 		}
548: 
549: 		for _, iface := range interfaces {
550: 			key := applyEndpointKey{node: nodeName, iface: iface.Name}
551: 			plan.liveEndpointSet[key] = struct{}{}
552: 			plan.liveEndpointInfo[key] = iface
553: 		}
554: 	}
555: 
556: 	return nil
557: }
558: 
559: func (c *CLab) validateDesiredEndpointOwnership(
560: 	ctx context.Context,
561: 	plan *applyPlan,
562: ) error {
563: 	for _, key := range sortedEndpointKeys(plan.desiredEndpointSet) {
564: 		if _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[key.node]; added {
565: 			continue
566: 		}
567: 
568: 		node, exists := plan.endpointNode(key.node)
569: 		if !exists {
570: 			node, exists = c.applyLinkNode(key.node)
571: 		}
572: 		if !exists {
573: 			continue
574: 		}
575: 
576: 		if err := clablinks.ValidateOwnedInterfaceFor(
577: 			ctx,
578: 			node,
579: 			key.node,
580: 			key.iface,
581: 		); err != nil {
582: 			return fmt.Errorf(
583: 				"desired endpoint %s cannot be reconciled: %w",
584: 				key.String(),
585: 				err,
586: 			)
587: 		}
588: 	}
589: 
590: 	return nil
591: }
592: 
593: func (c *CLab) applyParkingLinkNode(nodeName string) (clablinks.Node, bool) {
594: 	node, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]
595: 	if !exists || node.Config() == nil || node.Config().LongName == "" {
596: 		return nil, false
597: 	}
598: 
599: 	parkPath, err := clabutils.GetNamedNetNS(
600: 		clabutils.ParkingNetnsName(node.Config().LongName),
601: 	)
602: 	if err != nil {
603: 		return nil, false
604: 	}
605: 
606: 	return clablinks.NewParkingNode(node.Config().LongName, parkPath), true
607: }
608: 
609: func (c *CLab) applyKnownEndpointNames(
610: 	plan *applyPlan,
611: 	nodeName string,
612: ) map[string]struct{} {
613: 	if !isApplySpecialNode(nodeName) {
614: 		return nil
615: 	}
616: 
617: 	names := map[string]struct{}{}
618: 	for key := range plan.desiredEndpointSet {
619: 		if key.node == nodeName {
620: 			names[key.iface] = struct{}{}
621: 		}
622: 	}
623: 
624: 	return names
625: }
626: 
627: func (c *CLab) applyEndpointDiscoveryNodes(plan *applyPlan) map[string]clablinks.Node {
628: 	nodes := make(map[string]clablinks.Node, len(c.Nodes)+2)
629: 	for nodeName, node := range c.Nodes {
630: 		nodes[nodeName] = node
631: 	}
632: 
633: 	for _, key := range sortedEndpointKeys(plan.desiredEndpointSet) {
634: 		if _, exists := nodes[key.node]; exists {
635: 			continue
636: 		}
637: 		if node, exists := c.applyLinkNode(key.node); exists {
638: 			nodes[key.node] = node
639: 		}
640: 	}
641: 
642: 	return nodes
643: }
644: 
645: func (c *CLab) applyLinkNode(nodeName string) (clablinks.Node, bool) {
646: 	if node, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]; exists {
647: 		return node, true
648: 	}
649: 	node, exists := c.getSpecialLinkNodes()[nodeName]
650: 	return node, exists
651: }
652: 
653: func isApplySpecialNode(nodeName string) bool {
654: 	return nodeName == "host" || nodeName == "mgmt-net"
655: }
656: 
657: func (c *CLab) planAffectedApplyNode(
658: 	ctx context.Context,
659: 	plan *applyPlan,
660: 	nodeName string,
661: 	change string,
662: ) {
663: 	if nodeName == "" {
664: 		return
665: 	}
666: 	if _, planned := plan.linkRestartNodeSet[nodeName]; planned {
667: 		return
668: 	}
669: 	if _, planned := plan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName]; planned {
670: 		return
671: 	}
672: 
673: 	node, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]
674: 	if !exists {
675: 		return
676: 	}
677: 	if plan.isExternallyManaged(nodeName) {
678: 		log.Info(
679: 			"Applying link change without node lifecycle action",
680: 			"node", nodeName,
681: 			"change", change,
682: 			"externally-managed", true,
683: 		)
684: 		return
685: 	}
686: 
687: 	overridden := clabnodes.LinkApplyModeOverrideForNode(node) != ""
688: 
689: 	switch clabnodes.LinkApplyModeForNode(ctx, node) {
690: 	case clabnodes.LinkApplyModeLive:
691: 		log.Info(
692: 			"Applying link change without node lifecycle action",
693: 			"node",
694: 			nodeName,
695: 			"change",
696: 			change,
697: 		)
698: 	case clabnodes.LinkApplyModeRestart:
699: 		plan.linkRestartNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
700: 		plan.nodeChangeReasons[nodeName] = change
701: 		log.Info("Node will be restarted to apply link change", "node", nodeName, "change", change)
702: 	case clabnodes.LinkApplyModeRecreate:
703: 		plan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
704: 		delete(plan.restartNodeSet, nodeName)
705: 		delete(plan.linkRestartNodeSet, nodeName)
706: 		plan.nodeChangeReasons[nodeName] = change
707: 
708: 		if overridden {
709: 			log.Info("Node will be recreated to apply link change",
710: 				"node", nodeName, "change", change, "link-apply-mode", "recreate")
711: 		} else {
712: 			log.Info(
713: 				"Node will be recreated to apply link change since its kind does not declare "+
714: 					"live link support; if the NOS detects hot-plugged interfaces, set "+
715: 					"link-apply-mode: live on the node to avoid the recreation",
716: 				"node", nodeName,
717: 				"change", change,
718: 			)
719: 		}
720: 	}
721: }
722: 
723: func (c *CLab) resolveNodeConfigFromTopology(
724: 	topo *clabtypes.Topology,
725: 	nodeName string,
726: ) *clabtypes.NodeConfig {
727: 	if topo == nil {
728: 		return nil
729: 	}
730: 	if topo.GetNodeKind(nodeName) == clabnodestailscale.KindName &&
731: 		topo.GetNodeLabels(nodeName)[clabconstants.InternalNode] == "true" {
732: 		topo = &clabtypes.Topology{Nodes: map[string]*clabtypes.NodeDefinition{
733: 			nodeName: topo.Nodes[nodeName],
734: 		}}
735: 	}
736: 
737: 	binds, _ := topo.GetNodeBinds(nodeName)
738: 	portSet, _, _ := topo.GetNodePorts(nodeName)
739: 	kind := topo.GetNodeKind(nodeName)
740: 	privileged := topo.GetNodePrivileged(
741: 		nodeName,
742: 		c.privilegedByDefault(strings.ToLower(kind)),
743: 	)
744: 
745: 	nodeCfg := &clabtypes.NodeConfig{
746: 		ShortName:    nodeName,
747: 		Hostname:     topo.GetNodeHostname(nodeName),
748: 		Kind:         kind,
749: 		NodeType:     topo.GetNodeType(nodeName),
750: 		Image:        topo.GetNodeImage(nodeName),
751: 		Entrypoint:   topo.GetNodeEntrypoint(nodeName),
752: 		Cmd:          topo.GetNodeCmd(nodeName),
753: 		Exec:         topo.GetNodeExec(nodeName),
754: 		Env:          topo.GetNodeEnv(nodeName),
755: 		Binds:        binds,
756: 		Devices:      topo.GetNodeDevices(nodeName),
757: 		CapAdd:       topo.GetNodeCapAdd(nodeName),
758: 		Privileged:   privileged,
759: 		CgroupnsMode: topo.GetNodeCgroupnsMode(nodeName),
760: 		CgroupParent: topo.GetNodeCgroupParent(nodeName),
761: 		PidMode:      topo.GetNodePidMode(nodeName),
762: 		Tmpfs:        topo.GetNodeTmpfs(nodeName),
763: 		SecurityOpts: topo.GetNodeSecurityOpts(nodeName),
764: 		ShmSize:      topo.GetNodeShmSize(nodeName),
765: 		PortSet:      portSet,
766: 		User:         topo.GetNodeUser(nodeName),
767: 		NetworkMode:  topo.GetNodeNetworkMode(nodeName),
768: 		MgmtNet:      topo.GetNodeMgmtNet(nodeName),
769: 		Runtime:      topo.GetNodeRuntime(nodeName),
770: 		CPU:          topo.GetNodeCPU(nodeName),
771: 		CPUSet:       topo.GetNodeCPUSet(nodeName),
772: 		Memory:       topo.GetNodeMemory(nodeName),
773: 		License:      topo.GetNodeLicense(nodeName),
774: 	}
775: 
776: 	kindSpecificConfig, err := c.decodeKindSpecificConfig(topo, nodeName, strings.ToLower(kind))
777: 	if err != nil {
778: 		// A state file can hold keys a later release rejects; such a node counts as changed.
779: 		kindSpecificConfig = clabnodes.InvalidKindSpecificConfig{Err: err.Error()}
780: 	}
781: 
782: 	nodeCfg.KindSpecificConfig = kindSpecificConfig
783: 
784: 	return nodeCfg
785: }
786: 
787: func (c *CLab) planNodeReconciliation(ctx context.Context, plan *applyPlan) error {
788: 	if plan == nil || plan.currentNodes == nil {
789: 		return nil
790: 	}
791: 
792: 	var oldTopo *clabtypes.Topology
793: 	if plan.state != nil {
794: 		oldTopo = plan.state.Topology
795: 	}
796: 
797: 	for nodeName := range plan.currentNodes {
798: 		node, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]
799: 		if !exists {
800: 			continue
801: 		}
802: 		if _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName]; added {
803: 			continue
804: 		}
805: 
806: 		oldNodeConfig := c.resolveNodeConfigFromTopology(oldTopo, nodeName)
807: 		newNodeConfig := c.resolveNodeConfigFromTopology(c.Config.Topology, nodeName)
808: 		diff := node.ComputeDiff(oldNodeConfig, newNodeConfig)
809: 		plan.nodeDiffs[nodeName] = diff
810: 
811: 		result, err := node.GetReconcilePlan(ctx, diff)
812: 		if err != nil {
813: 			return fmt.Errorf("reconcile planning failed for node %q: %w", nodeName, err)
814: 		}
815: 		if plan.isNonContainerNode(nodeName) && result.Action != clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionNone {
816: 			return fmt.Errorf(
817: 				"node %q is externally managed and cannot be %s for %s",
818: 				nodeName,
819: 				result.Action,
820: 				configDriftReason(diff),
821: 			)
822: 		}
823: 
824: 		switch result.Action {
825: 		case clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRestart:
826: 			plan.restartNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
827: 			plan.nodeChangeReasons[nodeName] = configDriftReason(diff)
828: 		case clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRecreate:
829: 			plan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}
830: 			plan.nodeChangeReasons[nodeName] = configDriftReason(diff)
831: 		}
832: 	}
833: 
834: 	return nil
835: }
836: 
837: func configDriftReason(diff *clabtypes.TopologyDiff) string {
838: 	if diff == nil || len(diff.Fields) == 0 {
839: 		return "config drift"
840: 	}
841: 
842: 	return "config drift: " + strings.Join(diff.Fields, ", ")
843: }
844: 
845: func (c *CLab) reconcileNodes(ctx context.Context, plan *applyPlan) error {
846: 	if plan == nil || plan.currentNodes == nil {
847: 		return nil
848: 	}
849: 
850: 	for nodeName := range plan.currentNodes {
851: 		node, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]
852: 		if !exists {
853: 			continue
854: 		}
855: 		if _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName]; added {
856: 			continue
857: 		}
858: 		if plan.isRootNamespaceNode(nodeName) {
859: 			continue
860: 		}
861: 		// Recreated nodes are handled exclusively by the apply pipeline
862: 		// (park -> delete -> deploy -> restore). Calling Reconcile here would
863: 		// delete the container before parkRecreatedNodes can capture its
864: 		// interfaces, breaking the recreate flow.
865: 		if _, recreated := plan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName]; recreated {
866: 			continue
867: 		}
868: 
869: 		diff := plan.nodeDiffs[nodeName]
870: 
871: 		result, err := node.Reconcile(ctx, diff)
872: 		if err != nil {
873: 			return fmt.Errorf("reconcile failed for node %q: %w", nodeName, err)
874: 		}
875: 
876: 		if result.Action == clabtypes.TopologyDiffActionRestart {
877: 			if err := c.waitNodeRunning(ctx, node); err != nil {
878: 				return err
879: 			}
880: 			if err := c.waitNodeHealthyIfAvailable(ctx, node); err != nil {
881: 				return err
882: 			}
883: 		}
884: 	}
885: 
886: 	return nil
887: }
888: 
889: func (p *applyPlan) linkNeedsDeploy(link clablinks.Link) bool {
890: 	for _, ep := range clablinks.RuntimeEndpoints(link) {
891: 		key := endpointKeyFromEndpoint(ep)
892: 		if _, parked := p.parkedNodeSet[key.node]; !parked {
893: 			if _, added := p.addedNodeSet[key.node]; added {
894: 				return true
895: 			}
896: 		}
897: 		if _, live := p.liveEndpointSet[key]; !live {
898: 			return true
899: 		}
900: 	}
901: 
902: 	return !p.linkIntact(link)
903: }
904: 
905: func (p *applyPlan) linkIntact(link clablinks.Link) bool {
906: 	endpoints := clablinks.RuntimeEndpoints(link)
907: 	if len(endpoints) != 2 || len(p.liveEndpointInfo) == 0 {
908: 		return true
909: 	}
910: 
911: 	left, leftOK := p.liveEndpointInfo[endpointKeyFromEndpoint(endpoints[0])]
912: 	right, rightOK := p.liveEndpointInfo[endpointKeyFromEndpoint(endpoints[1])]
913: 	if !leftOK || !rightOK {
914: 		return false
915: 	}
916: 
917: 	if bridgeMasterMismatch(endpoints[0], left) || bridgeMasterMismatch(endpoints[1], right) {
918: 		return false
919: 	}
920: 
921: 	return left.PeerIndex != 0 &&
922: 		right.PeerIndex != 0 &&
923: 		left.PeerIndex == right.Index &&
924: 		right.PeerIndex == left.Index
925: }
926: 
927: // bridgeMasterMismatch reports whether the live interface of a bridge endpoint is
928: // attached to a bridge other than the one the endpoint resolves to, e.g. after a
929: // mgmt-net link moved to another management network. mgmt-net endpoints whose
930: // management network bridge is not resolved are not checked.
931: func bridgeMasterMismatch(ep clablinks.Endpoint, live clablinks.OwnedInterface) bool {
932: 	node := ep.GetNode()
933: 	if node.GetLinkEndpointType() != clablinks.LinkEndpointTypeBridge ||
934: 		clablinks.IsUnresolvedMgmtBridgeEndpoint(ep) {
935: 		return false
936: 	}
937: 
938: 	return live.MasterName != node.GetShortName()
939: }
940: 
941: func (p *applyPlan) deployNodeNames() []string {
942: 	if p == nil {
943: 		return nil
944: 	}
945: 
946: 	nodeNames := make([]string, 0, len(p.addedNodeSet)+len(p.recreatedNodeSet))
947: 
948: 	for nodeName := range p.addedNodeSet {
949: 		nodeNames = append(nodeNames, nodeName)
950: 	}
951: 	for nodeName := range p.recreatedNodeSet {
952: 		if _, added := p.addedNodeSet[nodeName]; !added {
953: 			nodeNames = append(nodeNames, nodeName)
954: 		}
955: 	}
956: 
957: 	sort.Strings(nodeNames)
958: 	return nodeNames
959: }
960: 
961: func (c *CLab) deleteApplyEndpoints(
962: 	ctx context.Context,
963: 	refs []applyEndpointRef,
964: ) error {
965: 	for _, ref := range refs {
966: 		log.Info("Deleting link endpoint", "endpoint", ref.key.String())
967: 		if err := clablinks.RemoveOwnedInterface(ctx, ref.node, ref.key.iface); err != nil {
968: 			if ref.bestEffort && errors.Is(err, clablinks.ErrInterfaceUnowned) {
969: 				log.Debugf("skipping best-effort endpoint delete: %v", err)
970: 				continue
971: 			}
972: 			return err
973: 		}
974: 	}
975: 
976: 	return nil
977: }
978: 
979: func (p *applyPlan) setEndpointNode(nodeName string, node clablinks.Node) {
980: 	if nodeName == "" || node == nil {
981: 		return
982: 	}
983: 	p.endpointNodes[nodeName] = node
984: }
985: 
986: func (p *applyPlan) endpointNode(nodeName string) (clablinks.Node, bool) {
987: 	node, ok := p.endpointNodes[nodeName]
988: 	return node, ok
989: }
990: 
991: func endpointKeyFromEndpoint(ep clablinks.Endpoint) applyEndpointKey {
992: 	if ep == nil || ep.GetNode() == nil {
993: 		return applyEndpointKey{}
994: 	}
995: 	nodeName := ep.GetNode().GetShortName()
996: 	if ep.IsNodeless() && ep.GetNode().GetLinkEndpointType() == clablinks.LinkEndpointTypeBridge {
997: 		nodeName = "mgmt-net"
998: 	}
999: 	return applyEndpointKey{
1000: 		node:  nodeName,
1001: 		iface: ep.GetIfaceName(),
1002: 	}
1003: }
1004: 
1005: func applyLinkName(link clablinks.Link) string {
1006: 	endpoints := clablinks.RuntimeEndpoints(link)
1007: 	names := make([]string, 0, len(endpoints))
1008: 	for _, ep := range endpoints {
1009: 		key := endpointKeyFromEndpoint(ep)
1010: 		if key.node == "" || key.iface == "" {
1011: 			continue
1012: 		}
1013: 		names = append(names, key.String())
1014: 	}
1015: 	sort.Strings(names)
1016: 
1017: 	return strings.Join(names, " -- ")
1018: }
1019: 
1020: func applyLinkNames(links []clablinks.Link) []string {
1021: 	names := make([]string, 0, len(links))
1022: 	for _, link := range links {
1023: 		names = append(names, applyLinkName(link))
1024: 	}
1025: 	return names
1026: }
1027: 
1028: func applyDeletedEndpointNames(refs []applyEndpointRef) []string {
1029: 	names := make([]string, 0, len(refs))
1030: 	for _, ref := range refs {
1031: 		if ref.bestEffort {
1032: 			continue
1033: 		}
1034: 		names = append(names, ref.key.String())
1035: 	}
1036: 	return names
1037: }
1038: 
1039: func sortedNodeNames(nodes map[string]clabnodes.Node) []string {
1040: 	names := make([]string, 0, len(nodes))
1041: 	for name := range nodes {
1042: 		names = append(names, name)
1043: 	}
1044: 	sort.Strings(names)
1045: 	return names
1046: }
1047: 
1048: func sortedLinkNodeNames(nodes map[string]clablinks.Node) []string {
1049: 	names := make([]string, 0, len(nodes))
1050: 	for name := range nodes {
1051: 		names = append(names, name)
1052: 	}
1053: 	sort.Strings(names)
1054: 	return names
1055: }
1056: 
1057: func sortedLinkIndexes(links map[int]clablinks.Link) []int {
1058: 	indexes := make([]int, 0, len(links))
1059: 	for idx := range links {
1060: 		indexes = append(indexes, idx)
1061: 	}
1062: 	sort.Ints(indexes)
1063: 	return indexes
1064: }
1065: 
1066: func sortedEndpointKeys(endpointSet map[applyEndpointKey]struct{}) []applyEndpointKey {
1067: 	keys := make([]applyEndpointKey, 0, len(endpointSet))
1068: 	for key := range endpointSet {
1069: 		keys = append(keys, key)
1070: 	}
1071: 	sort.Slice(keys, func(i, j int) bool {
1072: 		if keys[i].node == keys[j].node {
1073: 			return keys[i].iface < keys[j].iface
1074: 		}
1075: 		return keys[i].node < keys[j].node
1076: 	})
1077: 	return keys
1078: }
1079: 
1080: func sortedStringSet(values map[string]struct{}) []string {
1081: 	result := make([]string, 0, len(values))
1082: 	for value := range values {
1083: 		result = append(result, value)
1084: 	}
1085: 	sort.Strings(result)
1086: 	return result
1087: }
1088: 
1089: func unionStringSets(sets ...map[string]struct{}) map[string]struct{} {
1090: 	result := map[string]struct{}{}
1091: 	for _, set := range sets {
1092: 		for value := range set {
1093: 			result[value] = struct{}{}
1094: 		}
1095: 	}
1096: 	return result
1097: }

Such nodes\n// exist outside the lab, so they are always listed as current nodes, even\n// when the topology has just added them.\nfunc (p *applyPlan) isNewNonContainerNode(nodeName string) bool {\n\tif !p.isNonContainerNode(nodeName) || p.state == nil || p.state.Topology == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\t_, exists := p.state.Topology.Nodes[nodeName]\n\treturn !exists\n}\n\n// networkModeContainerTarget returns the referenced node name for a\n// \"network-mode: container:<name>\" config, or \"\" if networkMode does not\n// share another container's network namespace.\nfunc networkModeContainerTarget(networkMode string) string {\n\tif target, shared := strings.CutPrefix(networkMode, \"container:\"); shared {\n\t\treturn target\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\n// checkApplyNetworkModeTargets rejects apply when a node's network-mode:\n// container:<target> target is being removed from the topology while the\n// dependent node stays desired. Deleting the target would leave the\n// dependent silently attached to a defunct network namespace.\nfunc (c *CLab) checkApplyNetworkModeTargets(plan *applyPlan) error {\n\tif plan == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, nodeName := range sortedNodeNames(c.Nodes) {\n\t\tnetworkMode := c.Nodes[nodeName].Config().NetworkMode\n\t\ttarget := networkModeContainerTarget(networkMode)\n\t\tif target == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, deleted := plan.deletedNodeSet[target]; deleted {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\t\"node %q has network-mode %q, but %q is being removed from the topology; \"+\n\t\t\t\t\t\"remove or retarget %q as well\",\n\t\t\t\tnodeName,\n\t\t\t\tnetworkMode,\n\t\t\t\ttarget,\n\t\t\t\tnodeName,\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// planNetworkModeCascade forces recreation of nodes whose network-mode:\n// container:<target> target is itself being recreated. Docker binds\n// NetworkMode to the target container's ID at creation time, so a plain\n// restart of the dependent cannot rebind it to the target's new container;\n// only recreating the dependent does. A reverse dependency graph visits each\n// node and dependency at most once, including long namespace-sharing chains.\nfunc (c *CLab) planNetworkModeCascade(plan *applyPlan) {\n\tif plan == nil || len(plan.recreatedNodeSet) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tdependents := make(map[string][]string)\n\tfor name, node := range c.Nodes {\n\t\tif target := networkModeContainerTarget(node.Config().NetworkMode); target != \"\" {\n\t\t\tdependents[target] = append(dependents[target], name)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tqueue := make([]string, 0, len(c.Nodes))\n\tfor name := range plan.recreatedNodeSet {\n\t\tqueue = append(queue, name)\n\t}\n\tfor i := 0; i < len(queue); i++ {\n\t\ttarget := queue[i]\n\t\tfor _, nodeName := range dependents[target] {\n\t\t\tif _, recreated := plan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName]; recreated {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName]; added {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tplan.recreatedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}\n\t\t\tdelete(plan.restartNodeSet, nodeName)\n\t\t\tdelete(plan.linkRestartNodeSet, nodeName)\n\t\t\tplan.nodeChangeReasons[nodeName] = fmt.Sprintf(\n\t\t\t\t\"network-mode target %q recreated\", target,\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\tqueue = append(queue, nodeName)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (c *CLab) planApply(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tcurrentNodes map[string]*runtimeNodeGroup,\n) (*applyPlan, error) {\n\tstate, err := c.LoadState()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Warn(\"Failed to load state file\", \"error\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tplan := newApplyPlan(currentNodes, state)\n\n\tfor _, nodeName := range sortedNodeNames(c.Nodes) {\n\t\tif _, exists := currentNodes[nodeName]; !exists {\n\t\t\tstatus := c.Nodes[nodeName].GetContainerStatus(ctx)\n\t\t\tif status != clabruntime.NotFound {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\t\t\"node %q container %q already exists outside the runtime lab state\",\n\t\t\t\t\tnodeName,\n\t\t\t\t\tc.Nodes[nodeName].Config().LongName,\n\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tplan.addedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif plan.isNewNonContainerNode(nodeName) {\n\t\t\tplan.addedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, nodeName := range sortedRuntimeNodeGroupNames(currentNodes) {\n\t\tif _, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]; !exists {\n\t\t\tplan.deletedNodeSet[nodeName] = struct{}{}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif err := c.checkApplyNetworkModeTargets(plan); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tif err := c.planNodeReconciliation(ctx, plan); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tc.planParkedNodes(ctx, plan)\n\tc.planStoppedNodes(ctx, plan)\n\n\tfor _, linkIdx := range sortedLinkIndexes(c.Links) {\n\t\tfor _, ep := range clablinks.RuntimeEndpoints(c.Links[linkIdx]) {\n\t\t\tplan.desiredEndpointSet[endpointKeyFromEndpoint(ep)] = struct{}{}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif err := c.discoverLiveApplyEndpoints(ctx, plan); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tif err := c.validateDesiredEndpointOwnership(ctx, plan); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tc.planDeletedEndpoints(ctx, plan)\n\n\tfor _, linkIdx := range sortedLinkIndexes(c.Links) {\n\t\tlink := c.Links[linkIdx]\n\t\tif !plan.linkNeedsDeploy(link) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tplan.addDeployApplyLink(linkIdx, link)\n\n\t\tfor _, ep := range clablinks.RuntimeEndpoints(link) {\n\t\t\tnodeName := ep.GetNode().GetShortName()\n\t\t\tif _, exists := currentNodes[nodeName]; !exists {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif _, added := plan.addedNodeSet[nodeName]; added {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tc.planAffectedApplyNode(ctx, plan, nodeName, \"added link\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Link reconciliation can request additional recreations. Propagate namespace\n\t// dependencies only after those decisions, then park every affected live node.\n\tc.planNetworkModeCascade(plan)\n\tc.planTailscaleSidecarRecreates(plan)\n\tc.planParkedNodes(ctx, plan)\n\tc.planRecreatedNodeLinks(plan)\n\tfor nodeName := range plan.recreatedNodeSet {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tp.plannedLinkSet[linkIdx] = struct{}{}\n\tp.addedLinks = append(p.addedLinks, link)\n}\n\nfunc (c *CLab) planParkedNodes(ctx context.Context, plan *applyPlan) {\n\tif plan == *CLab) planRecreatedNodeLinks(plan *applyPlan) {\n\tif plan == nil || len(plan.recreatedNodeSet) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\trebuildSet := make(map[string]struct{}, len(plan.recreatedNodeSet))\n\tfor Reasoning
This is a large apply/reconcile feature. The key question for the srl netns change: does the apply pipeline know about the srl netns holder? Look at `planStoppedNodes` — it iterates `plan.currentNodes` — where do currentNodes come from? Probably from runtime enumeration of containers with the clab label, grouped by node name. The holder container has labels `clab-node-name: srl1-netns` and `containerlab: <lab>`. So the holder would appear as a runtime node group with name "srl1-netns"? Let me check `runtimeNodeGroup` and how currentNodes is built. If the holder is treated as a separate node, apply might try to delete it (since it's not in `c.Nodes`)! That would break: the holder would be in deletedNodeSet and get removed.

Let me look at where currentNodes is built — probably in core/apply.go or inspect.go. Let me grep for runtimeNodeGroup.
